### 11.3 Provider 同步时序

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Scheduler
    participant Channel
    participant Registry
    participant Provider
    participant Mapper
    participant Owner as Domain Owner
    participant Outbox
    Scheduler->>Channel: start sync kind connection
    Channel->>Registry: resolve signed capability
    Registry-->>Channel: adapter and policy
    Channel->>Provider: pull after cursor with deadline
    Provider-->>Channel: records next cursor receipt
    Channel->>Mapper: validate and normalize
    Mapper-->>Channel: canonical batch and row errors
    Channel->>Channel: begin UoW and persist raw hash mapping run row errors
    alt catalog or price batch
        Channel->>Outbox: append OfferSynced
    else inventory batch
        Channel->>Outbox: append StockSynced
    else statement batch
        Channel->>Outbox: append ChannelSettlementReceived
    end
    Channel->>Outbox: append SyncCompleted
    Channel->>Channel: advance cursor in same UoW after durable Outbox append
    Channel->>Channel: commit
    Outbox-->>Owner: deliver canonical event after commit
    Owner->>Owner: Inbox dedupe and apply in owner UoW
```

Cursor只证明Channel已持久化原始证据、规范事件和下一游标，不等待下游Owner同步回执。Owner通过自己的Inbox重放、Deadletter和Projection Watermark证明应用结果，不能把异步Consumer的accepted count当作Channel事务返回值。429 遵循 RetryAfter；401 或签名失败立即打开熔断并告警；单行脏数据进入错误报告，不污染 Canonical Product。

---

## 12. 核心模块间调用时序

### 12.1 登录、验证码与恢复

验证码不能绕过。收不到验证码时，修复发送链并提供受控恢复方法：

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User
    participant Auth
    participant Identity
    participant Risk
    participant Notify as NotificationPort
    participant DispatchJob
    participant Provider
    participant Session
    User->>Auth: request challenge
    Auth->>Identity: create challenge account device ip
    Identity->>Risk: IdentityRiskGate rate device account decision E58
    Risk-->>Identity: allow challenge deny
    Identity->>Notify: create one time code DispatchIntent E46
    Notify->>Notify: persist pending dispatch and enqueue job
    Notify-->>Identity: dispatch id accepted after durable write
    Identity-->>Auth: challenge id masked destination expiry
    Notify-->>DispatchJob: notification.dispatch after commit
    DispatchJob->>Provider: send with idempotency
    Provider-->>DispatchJob: delivery receipt or typed failure
    DispatchJob->>Notify: append attempt and final status
    User->>Auth: submit code
    Auth->>Identity: verify challenge once
    Identity->>Session: create tracked revocable session
    Session-->>Auth: secure target session
```

允许的恢复方式：已登记 TOTP、WebAuthn、一次性备用码，或双人审批的管理员辅助恢复。任何方式都必须提升风险审计，不提供固定 OTP、万能密码或验证码绕过。

### 12.2 Console 启动与 Scope 切换

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Browser
    participant Console
    participant Identity
    participant Access
    participant Capability
    participant Organization
    participant Registry
    participant Workspace
    Browser->>Console: load signed static asset
    Console->>Identity: identity.session.read
    Identity-->>Console: principal session assurance and identityVersion only
    Console->>Access: access.center.read for principal
    Access-->>Console: memberships authorized roots permissions and accessVersion
    Console->>Capability: capability.assignments.read for authorized roots
    Capability-->>Console: entitlements quotas and capabilityVersion
    Console->>Organization: organization.layers.read for Access roots
    Organization-->>Console: scope tree
    Console->>Registry: resolve route manifest
    Registry-->>Console: title permission capability lazy module
    Console->>Workspace: mount with explicit scope
    Workspace->>Workspace: start scoped queries
    Browser->>Console: switch scope
    Console->>Workspace: abort old scope requests and unmount
    Console->>Registry: resolve same or first permitted workspace
    Console->>Workspace: mount new explicit scope
```

删除 configureAdminBff 全局可变配置。Scope 是不可变调用参数，不是进程全局状态。

### 12.3 创建优业、喜悦会、鸿泰集团与两个商城

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Owner
    participant Organization
    participant Access
    participant Capability
    participant Audit
    Owner->>Organization: preview create Distributor 优业 and 喜悦会
    Organization-->>Owner: codes conflicts tenant impact
    Owner->>Organization: create with idempotency
    Organization->>Audit: append creation evidence
    Owner->>Organization: create hidden Tenant under 优业
    Owner->>Organization: create Enterprise 鸿泰集团
    Owner->>Capability: assign approved package and quota
    Owner->>Access: bind initial administrator
    Owner->>Organization: create Mall 鸿泰惠民通
    Owner->>Organization: create Mall 鸿泰甄选
    Organization->>Audit: append full hierarchy evidence
    Organization-->>Owner: authoritative tree and versions
```

创建命令必须有唯一 code、displayName、parentVersion、timezone、ownerMembership、packageVersion 和 idempotencyKey。任一步失败不会留下无管理员的活动商城。

### 12.4 商品接入、治理、定价和投放

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant ChannelOutbox
    participant CatalogInbox
    participant Catalog
    participant Review
    participant CatalogOutbox
    participant PricingInbox
    participant Pricing
    participant InventoryInbox
    participant Inventory
    participant Risk
    participant ExperienceInbox
    participant Experience
    ChannelOutbox-->>CatalogInbox: OfferSynced E26
    CatalogInbox->>Catalog: Inbox dedupe canonical source offer
    Catalog->>Catalog: normalize match deduplicate
    Catalog->>Review: changed field review
    Review-->>Catalog: approved version
    Catalog->>Risk: CatalogRiskGate listing policy E59
    Risk-->>Catalog: allow review deny
    Catalog->>CatalogOutbox: persist immutable Listing and ListingPublished in one UoW
    CatalogOutbox-->>PricingInbox: ListingPublished E08
    PricingInbox->>Pricing: build offer and price readiness via CatalogPricingPort
    CatalogOutbox-->>InventoryInbox: ListingPublished E09
    InventoryInbox->>Inventory: create sku stock identity via CatalogSkuPort
    ChannelOutbox-->>InventoryInbox: StockSynced E27
    InventoryInbox->>Inventory: apply provider stock observation idempotently
    CatalogOutbox-->>ExperienceInbox: ListingPublished
    ExperienceInbox->>Experience: invalidate draft and gate public head on price stock readiness
```

Catalog不得同步调用Pricing或Inventory写入，也不推进Channel Cursor。Channel在自己的UoW推进Cursor并发布OfferSynced/StockSynced；Catalog、Pricing、Inventory、Experience各用独立Inbox推进自己的事实。Listing元数据可以先发布，但Experience/public reader在Price/Stock Readiness缺失时Fail-closed，不展示“有商品无有效价/库存”的伪可售状态。

E09的目标Consumer必须显式落在`services/commerce/src/modules/inventory/application/consumer/ListingPublishedInboxHandler.ts`。Handler先以`eventId+listingId+listingVersion`领取Inventory Inbox，使用公开`CatalogSkuPort`读取最小SKU身份，在同一Inventory UoW create-if-absent建立StockItem/Availability骨架并写Inbox Applied；它不读Catalog表、不初始化伪库存、不推进Channel Cursor。`InventoryModule.ts`的Manifest以`eventId=catalog.listing.published`注册该Handler，缺注册、重复注册或转入通配Consumer均失败。精确测试恰好分三层：module-local `services/commerce/src/modules/inventory/application/consumer/ListingPublishedInboxHandler.test.ts`只验Handler分支、未知SKU与乱序旧version；`services/commerce/tests/message/inventory/ListingPublishedInboxHandler.test.ts`在真Inbox/UoW验重投、事务回滚、Crash-after-effect-before-receipt与Replay零额外效果；`tests/contract/edge/E09.contract.test.ts`只验Event/`CatalogSkuPort` Schema、Publisher/Consumer和边序，并证明Experience在Availability未就绪时fail-closed。三份测试的`testPurpose`必须唯一且禁止复制同一断言集，缺任一层都视为E09断链。

### 12.5 Checkout 并行预览

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User
    participant Checkout
    participant Cart
    participant Member
    participant Qualification
    participant Pricing
    participant Marketing
    participant Voucher
    participant Benefit
    participant Inventory
    User->>Checkout: selected items address tender
    Checkout->>Cart: canonical cart
    par independent bounded reads
        Checkout->>Member: AddressReadPort scoped snapshot E60
        Checkout->>Qualification: evaluate versioned rules
        Checkout->>Pricing: quote price tax shipping
        Checkout->>Marketing: quote promotions
        Checkout->>Voucher: preview applicable vouchers
        Checkout->>Benefit: preview balance and policy
        Checkout->>Inventory: display availability
    end
    Checkout->>Checkout: compose sign persist expiring quote
    Checkout-->>User: authoritative totals rejections versions expiry
```

并行只用于独立读，设置统一 Deadline 和 AbortSignal。任何关键子结果缺失不返回伪完整 Quote。

### 12.6 原子下单

PlaceOrder 从 Order 移到 Checkout 的 PurchaseProcess，保留当前正确的锁顺序与原子骨架：

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Client
    participant Checkout as PurchaseProcess
    participant Idem
    participant Cart
    participant Qual
    participant Price
    participant Stock
    participant Voucher
    participant Benefit
    participant Marketing
    participant Order
    participant Payment
    participant Tx as TransactionContext
    participant Outbox
    Client->>Checkout: confirm quote and idempotency key
    Checkout->>Tx: begin shared transaction
    Checkout->>Idem: claim key and hash
    Checkout->>Cart: lock selected cart
    Checkout->>Qual: revalidate through Port
    Checkout->>Price: validate quote versions
    Checkout->>Stock: reserve sorted sku ids
    Checkout->>Voucher: hold sorted voucher ids
    Checkout->>Benefit: hold sorted accounts
    Checkout->>Marketing: reserve campaign budget
    Checkout->>Order: create immutable order snapshots
    Checkout->>Payment: create intent and tender plan
    Checkout->>Cart: mark selected items converted
    Stock->>Outbox: append StockReserved as Inventory owner
    Order->>Outbox: append OrderPlaced as Order owner
    Cart->>Outbox: append CartChanged as Cart owner
    Checkout->>Outbox: append QuoteConfirmed as Checkout owner
    Checkout->>Idem: save response
    Checkout->>Tx: commit aggregate changes owner events and audit cause
    Checkout-->>Client: order receipt and payment action
```

以上调用共享TransactionContext，但调用者只能看到公开Port，不能读取目标表。Outbox由各Owner聚合或Handler产生，TransactionContext统一收集并提交；Checkout不得代发OrderPlaced、StockReserved或CartChanged。

### 12.7 支付、迟到支付与退款

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Client
    participant Payment
    participant JobQueue
    participant SubmitJob as ProviderSubmitJobHandler
    participant Provider
    participant ProviderInbox
    participant Order
    participant Benefit
    participant Voucher
    participant Inventory
    participant Marketing
    participant ReportingInbox
    participant OrderTimeline
    participant Fulfillment
    participant FulfillmentInbox
    participant PaymentOutbox
    participant RecoveryJob as RefundRecoveryJobHandler
    participant FinalizeRefund as FinalizeRefundLegHandler
    participant FinanceInbox
    participant Finance
    Client->>Payment: create or continue payment
    Payment->>Payment: persist pending attempt and enqueue payment.provider.submit in one UoW
    Payment-->>Client: pending action attempt id and poll or SSE location
    JobQueue->>SubmitJob: claim prepared attempt after commit
    SubmitJob->>Provider: create prepay with provider idempotency key
    Provider-->>SubmitJob: token failure or unknown
    SubmitJob->>Payment: FinalizeAttempt command once
    Payment-->>Client: poll or SSE yields ready failed or recovery
    Provider->>ProviderInbox: signed webhook
    ProviderInbox->>ProviderInbox: verify decrypt merchant app amount nonce
    ProviderInbox-->>Provider: accepted after durable write
    ProviderInbox->>Payment: idempotent provider observation
    alt order payable and every required owner commit succeeds
        Payment->>Order: confirm capture through PaymentOrderPort
        Payment->>Inventory: commit reservation through InventoryReservationPort
        Payment->>Voucher: capture held voucher through VoucherTenderPort
        Payment->>Benefit: capture held benefit through BenefitTenderPort
        Payment->>Marketing: commit budget through PromotionReservationPort
        Payment->>PaymentOutbox: append PaymentCaptured in same UoW
        PaymentOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
        FinanceInbox->>Finance: post balanced journal idempotently
        PaymentOutbox-->>FulfillmentInbox: deliver after commit
        FulfillmentInbox->>Fulfillment: start fulfillment idempotently
    else order cancelled expired or any required owner commit fails after authoritative capture
        Payment->>Payment: rollback shared owner transaction and open late capture recovery
        Payment->>PaymentOutbox: append LatePaymentRecoveryOpened with captured observation
        PaymentOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
        FinanceInbox->>Finance: post suspense journal idempotently
        PaymentOutbox-->>RecoveryJob: enqueue once by recovery id
        RecoveryJob->>Provider: query then auto refund by leg key
    end
    Client->>Payment: refund request
    Payment->>Payment: validate cumulative refundable amount and create RefundLegs
    par internal benefit leg
        Payment->>Benefit: restore through BenefitTenderPort with leg key
        Benefit-->>Payment: BenefitRestored receipt or typed failure
    and internal voucher leg
        Payment->>Voucher: reverse through VoucherTenderPort with leg key
        Voucher-->>Payment: VoucherReversed receipt or typed failure
    and external provider legs
        Payment->>JobQueue: enqueue payment.refund.recover action submitOrRecover in same UoW
        JobQueue->>RecoveryJob: claim committed Pending leg by leg key
        RecoveryJob->>Provider: submit refund outside transaction with provider idempotency key
        Provider->>ProviderInbox: refund observations
        ProviderInbox->>FinalizeRefund: finalize each external leg once
    end
    Payment->>Payment: aggregate leg states and amounts
    alt every required leg terminal success for requested amount
        Payment->>PaymentOutbox: append PaymentRefunded with immutable leg snapshot
        PaymentOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
        FinanceInbox->>Finance: post reversal journal idempotently
        PaymentOutbox-->>ReportingInbox: deliver after commit
        ReportingInbox->>OrderTimeline: project refund outcome idempotently
    else any leg unknown or retryable
        Payment->>PaymentOutbox: append RefundRecoveryOpened only
        PaymentOutbox-->>JobQueue: upsert same payment.refund.recover key
        JobQueue->>RecoveryJob: claim Unknown or Retryable leg
        RecoveryJob->>Provider: query first, then retry only when proven absent and policy permits
        RecoveryJob->>FinalizeRefund: persist observation and next terminal or scheduled state
    end
```

`RequestRefundHandler`在保存Refund/RefundLeg的同一UoW为每个外部Pending腿写`payment.refund.recover`，payload固定含`action=submitOrRecover`、refundLegId、provider、semanticKey和attemptVersion；HTTP与Inbox Handler不得直接调用Provider。`RefundRecoveryJobHandler`是外部退款初次提交、Unknown查单和Retryable重试的唯一外调点：Pending可按Provider幂等键首次提交，Unknown必须先查单，Retryable遵守Backoff且仅在确认无成功事实后重试；所有结果只经`FinalizeRefundLegHandler`推进。`PaymentRefunded`表示本次已批准退款金额的全部必需RefundLeg都到达成功终态，并携带原Tender、Leg Amount、Provider/Benefit/Voucher Receipt与累计退款快照；部分金额退款可以完成，但“部分Leg尚未完成”不能发布最终事件。`LatePaymentRecoveryOpened`只表示Provider已权威入账、但订单不可捕获或任一必需Owner提交失败，Finance据此暂记；`RefundRecoveryOpened`只表示既有退款腿Unknown/Retryable，不产生新资金事实，只有Payment Recovery Job消费，Finance不得订阅。Benefit与Voucher各自在自己的UoW发布`BenefitRestored`与`VoucherReversed`，Payment只汇总Receipt，不跨Owner写账。

售后退款按`returnRequired`分流：免退货售后的`AfterSaleApproved`可直接授权Payment创建Refund；需退货售后只创建Return并等待Fulfillment发布`ReturnInspected`。该事件必须携带afterSaleId、orderLineId、inspectionVersion、acceptedQty、rejectedQty、restockableQty、authorizedRefundMoney、evidenceHash和semanticKey。Payment Inbox只按`authorizedRefundMoney`创建退款腿，Inventory Inbox只按`restockableQty`回补，两个Consumer分别使用E50/E51并证明Duplicate、OutOfOrder、Replay效果为零。审核通过不等于质检通过，更不等于退款完成。

Accounting Leg的唯一记账来源固定如下；事件可以携带全量Tender Snapshot用于对账，但Finance PostingPolicy只能处理`accountingOwnerEventId`等于本行的腿。唯一键为`economicLegId + accountingOwnerEventId + sourceAggregateVersion`，同一Economic Leg被第二个Owner事件申报时必须Deadletter并告警，不能“按金额看起来一样”而重复入账。

| Economic Leg              | 捕获/增加唯一记账事件                          | 退款/恢复唯一记账事件                 | Payment聚合中的用途                                           |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 外部现金或Provider Tender | `PaymentCaptured`                              | `PaymentRefunded`                     | 保存Provider Receipt、累计捕获/退款和全量Tender Snapshot      |
| Voucher负债腿             | `VoucherRedeemed`；发行负债另由`VoucherIssued` | `VoucherReversed`                     | 只保存Voucher Receipt并等待RefundLeg终态，不重复记Voucher金额 |
| Benefit负债腿             | `BenefitDebited`；授予负债另由`BenefitGranted` | `BenefitRestored`                     | 只保存Benefit Receipt并等待RefundLeg终态，不重复记Benefit金额 |
| 迟到外部入账              | `LatePaymentRecoveryOpened`                    | 后续外部退款仍由最终`PaymentRefunded` | 先入Suspense并绑定Recovery ID；不得触发Fulfillment            |
| 退款Unknown/Retryable     | 无                                             | `RefundRecoveryOpened`不记账          | 仅驱动查单、重试或人工修复                                    |

迟到款的最终退款必须把`recoveryId`、`suspenseJournalId`和`recoveryKind=latecapture`带入对应外部RefundLeg与最终`PaymentRefunded`。Finance PostingPolicy遇到该Kind时只冲销原Suspense Journal，不生成“正常销售收入再退款”两套分录；普通退款则必须`recoveryId=null`并引用原Capture Journal。强制不变量为：每个迟到Recovery的Suspense余额最终为零或处于有Owner/Deadline的Open状态、收入不重复、退款不重复、原Journal引用连续，Crash/Replay后结果不变。

### 12.8 履约与外部未知状态

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Event as PaymentCaptured
    participant Fulfillment
    participant Channel
    participant Provider
    participant FulfillmentOutbox
    participant SupportInbox
    participant Support
    Event->>Fulfillment: idempotent consume
    Fulfillment->>Fulfillment: split supplier location method
    Fulfillment->>Channel: submit canonical remote order
    Channel->>Provider: submit with idempotency and deadline
    alt receipt
        Provider-->>Channel: remote order id
        Channel-->>Fulfillment: accepted
    else timeout unknown
        Channel-->>Fulfillment: unknown
        Fulfillment->>Fulfillment: schedule query not duplicate submit
    else definitive reject
        Channel-->>Fulfillment: rejected
        Fulfillment->>FulfillmentOutbox: append FulfillmentFailed
        FulfillmentOutbox-->>SupportInbox: deliver after commit
        SupportInbox->>Support: open task with evidence idempotently
    end
```

OrderExpiryJob只提交`ExpireOrder`命令；Order将其归一为带`reason=expired`的`OrderCancelled`，不创建第二个`OrderExpired`别名。Inventory、Benefit、Voucher、Payment和Marketing分别通过E52—E56的独立Inbox释放Reservation/Hold或关闭Attempt；Checkout/PurchaseProcess不消费该事件，也不代替Owner补偿。退货到仓后，Fulfillment质检聚合在同一UoW发布`ReturnInspected`，由Payment与Inventory通过E50/E51各自推进退款与回补。

### 12.9 卡券与门店核销

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Admin
    participant Voucher
    participant VoucherOutbox
    participant FinanceInbox
    participant Finance
    participant Store
    participant Verification
    Admin->>Voucher: approved reserve and issue
    Voucher->>Voucher: lock encrypted card pool in stable order
    Voucher->>VoucherOutbox: append VoucherIssued in same UoW
    VoucherOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
    FinanceInbox->>Finance: post liability journal idempotently
    Voucher-->>Admin: batch receipt
    Store->>Verification: device clerk dynamic code item amount
    Verification->>Verification: consume challenge once
    Verification->>Voucher: redeem eligible voucher
    Voucher->>VoucherOutbox: append VoucherRedeemed in same UoW
    VoucherOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
    FinanceInbox->>Finance: post redemption journal idempotently
    Voucher-->>Verification: receipt
    Verification-->>Store: safe result and amount source
```

### 12.10 对账、结算、开票与关账

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Provider
    participant Channel
    participant ChannelOutbox
    participant FinanceInbox
    participant Finance
    participant Reconcile
    participant Maker
    participant Checker
    participant Settlement
    participant Invoice
    Provider->>Channel: statement batch
    Channel->>Channel: persist normalized statement and cursor
    Channel->>ChannelOutbox: append ChannelSettlementReceived in same UoW
    ChannelOutbox-->>FinanceInbox: deliver after commit
    FinanceInbox->>Finance: ingest immutable statement snapshot idempotently
    Finance->>Reconcile: match immutable journal facts
    Reconcile-->>Maker: exact ambiguous missing extra
    Maker->>Reconcile: submit evidence based resolution
    Checker->>Reconcile: approve as different principal
    Reconcile->>Finance: append adjustment if required
    Finance->>Settlement: select reconciled unlocked items
    Checker->>Settlement: approve frozen settlement
    Settlement->>Invoice: eligible invoice basis
    Invoice-->>Finance: issued or red invoice event
    Finance->>Finance: close period only when all gates zero
```

### 12.11 报表投影与驾驶舱

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Outbox
    participant Inbox
    participant Projector
    participant Fact
    participant Projection
    participant Watermark
    participant Cache
    participant Cockpit
    Outbox->>Inbox: versioned event
    Inbox->>Projector: exactly once effect by consumer event
    Projector->>Watermark: reject aggregate version regression
    Projector->>Fact: append normalized fact
    Projector->>Projection: update scoped aggregate
    Projector->>Watermark: advance
    Projector->>Cache: invalidate versioned keys
    Cockpit->>Projection: query scope period dimensions
    Projection-->>Cockpit: metrics and watermark
```

### 12.12 商城装修发布

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Operator
    participant Experience
    participant Catalog
    participant Object
    participant CDN
    participant Head
    Operator->>Experience: save draft with expected version
    Operator->>Experience: validate
    Experience->>Catalog: verify visible listings and links
    Experience-->>Operator: report
    Operator->>Experience: publish validated version
    Experience->>Experience: create immutable snapshot
    Experience->>Object: stage manifest and assets
    Object-->>Experience: integrity receipt
    Experience->>CDN: warm
    CDN-->>Experience: ready
    Experience->>Head: atomic switch
    Experience-->>Operator: publication receipt
```

---

## 13. 每个领域模块内部数据流时序

以下 28 张图是模块内部标准主路径。共同规则：

- Controller 只解析合同与进入 Pipeline。
- Handler 不拼接跨 Schema SQL。
- Repository 只访问本模块拥有的 Schema。
- 同步依赖通过公开 Port，并共享显式 TransactionContext。
- 聚合写、幂等、Audit 和 Outbox 在同一 UnitOfWork。
- 外部调用只能在提交前只读预检或提交后异步执行。
- 失败返回合同声明的稳定错误，不通过 Error Message 猜测。

### 13.0 全数据流登记

后续 28 张时序图各展示一个代表性主命令路径；下面的登记表补齐查询、事件消费、Job、缓存/投影、失败与补偿，二者合在一起才构成模块内部数据流设计。生产完成仍须由 Module Manifest、Ownership Catalog、Contract Catalog 和测试证据自动生成同结构清单；人工表不能替代运行证据。

#### 13.0.1 入口、所有权与同步事务

| 模块          | 全部入口类别                                                | Owned Aggregate / Table Group                                     | 同步读写、UoW 与核心不变量                                                          |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Identity      | 注册、登录、挑战、找回、改密、Step-up、会话查询/撤销        | Identity、Credential、Session、Challenge                          | Credential 校验与 Session/Challenge 写同 UoW；Principal 唯一、Challenge 单次消费    |
| Organization  | 创建、移动、停启、管理员锚点、树与祖先查询                  | Platform、Distributor、Tenant、Enterprise、Mall、Closure          | 锁移动节点与父链；禁止环、跨租户父子和失联安全锚点                                  |
| Access        | Membership、Role、Grant、Deny、Decision、解释               | Membership、Role、Grant、Deny、AccessVersion                      | 变更与 AccessVersion 同 UoW；Deny 优先、Scope 交集、默认拒绝                        |
| Capability    | Plan、Package、Assignment、Quota 配置、消费与查询           | Plan、Package、Assignment、QuotaCounter                           | 版本化分配与原子计数；Capability 不等于菜单可见                                     |
| Partner       | Supplier、Brand、Store 入驻、协议、授权与停启               | Supplier、Brand、Store、Agreement                                 | 协议版本和主体状态同 UoW；Store 归属稳定，历史协议不物理删除                        |
| Member        | 档案、分组、等级、导入、身份绑定与查询                      | MemberProfile、Group、Level、ImportBatch、ImportRow               | PII 加密；批次分块独立 UoW；IdentityId 与租户档案保持显式映射                       |
| Qualification | Policy 草稿/发布、资格判断、限购计数、解释                  | Policy、PolicyVersion、Decision、Counter                          | 使用发布版本；计数 CAS 或行锁；Decision 保存输入摘要与解释                          |
| Catalog       | Product、Sku、SourceOffer、Listing、Pool、类目、投放与查询  | Product、Sku、SourceOffer、Listing、Pool、Category                | 发布生成不可变快照；来源映射唯一；有订单引用的商品不可物理删除                      |
| Pricing       | RuleSet、报价、毛利复核、税与舍入、版本查询                 | RuleSet、RuleVersion、Quote、MarginReview                         | Money 整数分；规则版本、输入快照和 Quote 同 UoW；禁止前端算最终价                   |
| Inventory     | 调整、预占、提交、释放、可售查询                            | StockItem、Reservation、Movement                                  | 固定锁序；available = onhand - reserved；同 Reservation 只提交或释放一次            |
| Experience    | Draft、校验、预览、发布、回滚、Head 查询                    | Draft、AssetManifest、Snapshot、PublishedHead                     | Snapshot 不可变；Head 用 CAS 原子切换；资产 Hash 与页面引用一致                     |
| Marketing     | Campaign、Audience、Budget、促销预览、启停、抽奖            | Campaign、Audience、Budget、PromotionQuote、DrawReceipt           | 预算原子占用；叠加顺序版本化；随机结果可复验                                        |
| Cart          | 购物车读取、增删改、选择、合并、清空                        | Cart、CartItem                                                    | CartVersion 乐观锁；只保存意图，不锁价、不锁库存                                    |
| Checkout      | Preview、Confirm、读取 Quote、失效                          | CheckoutQuote、TenderPreview、ReservationReference                | 并行读取后服务端归并；确认时验证版本与时效；Quote 快照不可改                        |
| Order         | 创建、读取、取消、超时、售后申请/决策、时间线               | Order、Suborder、OrderLine、OrderSnapshot、AfterSale              | 固定锁序；商业承诺快照不可漂移；Order 状态不吞并 Payment/Fulfillment 状态           |
| Fulfillment   | 创建履约单、接单、发货、轨迹、签收、退货、异常              | FulfillmentOrder、Shipment、Package、Tracking、Return             | 外部状态按 ProviderVersion 单调推进；重复回调无第二业务效果                         |
| Verification  | 动态码签发、验证、核销、撤销、设备绑定与收据查询            | Challenge、Device、VerificationReceipt                            | Challenge 原子单次消费；设备、门店、Scope 与目标权益全部匹配                        |
| Payment       | Intent、Attempt、回调、查单、退款、撤销、状态查询           | PaymentIntent、Attempt、Tender、Refund、ProviderObservation       | Runtime Inbox Receipt与业务Observation同UoW；退款累计不超实付；Unknown 不盲重试扣款 |
| Voucher       | Program、卡池导入、备券、发券、绑定、核销、冲正、过期       | Program、CardPool、Voucher、Reservation、Redemption               | 卡密加密；状态 CAS；同 Voucher 只成功核销一次；冲正引用原收据                       |
| Benefit       | Plan、预算、批量发放、冻结、扣减、回补、过期、余额查询      | Plan、Budget、Grant、BenefitLedger                                | Ledger 只追加；余额由流水归集；批次子项幂等，恢复前重验额度                         |
| Finance       | Account、分录、对账、差异、结算、提现、发票、关账与查询     | Account、Journal、Reconciliation、Settlement、Withdrawal、Invoice | 借贷必平；行只追加；Maker 与 Checker 不同；关账差异为零                             |
| Channel       | Connection、Mapping、同步、Webhook、重放、Cursor、健康      | Connection、Mapping、SyncRun、WebhookObservation、Cursor          | Runtime Inbox Receipt、业务Observation、Cursor和Owner Outbox同UoW；外部标识映射唯一 |
| Support       | Conversation、Ticket、Assignment、SLA、升级、只读业务上下文 | Conversation、Ticket、Assignment、SlaClock                        | TicketVersion 乐观锁；只保存最小 PII；客服动作不能直改资金事实                      |
| Notification  | Template、Preference、Dispatch、Attempt、回执与查询         | Template、Preference、Dispatch、Attempt                           | businessEvent + recipient + channel 幂等；偏好、强制通知和退订规则同版本            |
| Reporting     | Event 消费、Projection 重建、Metric 查询、Export            | Fact、Projection、Metric、Checkpoint、Export                      | Inbox、Projection 与 Checkpoint 同 UoW；Watermark 单调；可从 Event 重建             |
| Risk          | Policy、Signal、Evaluate、Challenge、Review、Case           | Policy、Signal、Decision、Case                                    | 硬规则优先；Decision 保存模型/规则版本和证据；高风险默认关闭                        |
| Audit         | Append、Query、Digest、Archive、Export、Integrity Verify    | AuditEvent、Digest、Archive                                       | 只追加；PrevHash/Hash 链连续；写业务事实与 Audit 同交易或同 Outbox 因果链           |
| Extension     | Manifest 校验、安装、绑定、启停、健康、升级、卸载           | Manifest、Installation、Binding、Health                           | 版本 CAS；Capability 与最小权限 Allowlist；扩展不得获得通用数据库连接               |

#### 13.0.2 Port、事件、异步流、失败与证明

| 模块          | Ports In / Out                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Trigger / Consumed Events → Emitted Events                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Job、Cache 或 Projection                                                                                 | 失败、补偿与最低证明                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Identity      | In IdentityFacade、PrincipalPort；Out CryptoPort、ClockPort、NotificationPort、IdentityRiskGate                                                                                                                                                                          | `event:MembershipEnrollmentCreated`、`cmd:RecoveryApproved` → IdentityRegistered、SessionCreated、SessionRevoked                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | SessionCleanup、ChallengeExpiry；短 TTL Session Cache                                                    | E01/E46/E58/E74/E75/E76；邀请只保存opaque enrollmentRef，不拥有Role/Scope；失败不创建半会话                                                                                                                            |
| Organization  | In OrgScopePort、OrgQualificationPort、OrganizationReadPort；Out internal ClockPort                                                                                                                                                                                      | `cmd:AdminAssigned` → OrgUnitChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | ClosureRebuild、TreeProjection                                                                           | E02/E06/E61/E62；环检测、跨租户移动、并发移动、重建一致性；失败前滚修复 Closure                                                                                                                                        |
| Access        | In AccessDecisionPort；Out AccessRiskGate、PrincipalPort、OrgScopePort、CapabilityDecisionPort                                                                                                                                                                           | `cmd:CreateMembershipEnrollment`、`event:IdentityRegistered`、`event:MemberImported`、`event:OrgUnitChanged`、`event:CapabilityChanged`、`event:SessionRevoked` → MembershipEnrollmentCreated、AccessVersionChanged                                                                                                                                         | 无Access Job；Enrollment在每次读/消费CAS时按Clock惰性判Expired；Decision Cache按AccessVersion失效        | E01/E02/E03/E73—E76；Enrollment是Role/Scope预授权唯一Owner；过期Ref绝不激活；依赖失败默认拒绝                                                                                                                          |
| Capability    | In CapabilityDecisionPort；Out internal ClockPort                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `cmd:PlanChanged` → CapabilityChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | QuotaReset、UsageProjection                                                                              | E03；并发超额、版本回退、停用传播；绝不以 UI 菜单代替后端判定                                                                                                                                                          |
| Partner       | In PartnerFacade、PartnerCatalogPort、PartnerQualificationPort、StoreReadPort；Out OrganizationReadPort、SecretRefPort、NotificationPort                                                                                                                                 | `cmd:AgreementApproved` → PartnerEnrolled、AgreementChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | OnboardingReview、AgreementExpiry                                                                        | E04/E07/E47/E61/E71；重复入驻、协议过期、品牌/门店归属；失败保持 Draft 或 Review                                                                                                                                       |
| Member        | In AddressReadPort、AddressCommandPort、MemberQualificationPort、MemberSummaryPort、MemberReadPort；Out OrganizationReadPort、ObjectPort                                                                                                                                 | `event:IdentityRegistered`、`event:OrgUnitChanged` → MemberChanged、MemberImported                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | ImportMember、GroupProjection                                                                            | E05/E60/E62/E67—E70/E73；MemberImported仅携带member/org/client/employeeRef/importRowId，角色与Scope由Access自有Policy决定；旧泛化MemberPort为零                                                                        |
| Qualification | In QualificationDecisionPort；Out MemberQualificationPort、OrgQualificationPort、PartnerQualificationPort、ClockPort                                                                                                                                                     | `event:MemberChanged`、`event:OrgUnitChanged`、`event:AgreementChanged` → QualificationDecided、QualificationPolicyChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | PolicyCompile、CounterReset；Decision Cache                                                              | 边界日期、并发限购、解释稳定性；未知依赖默认不授予                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Catalog       | In CatalogPort、CatalogPricingPort、CatalogSkuPort、CatalogPublicationPort、CatalogReadPort；Out PartnerCatalogPort、SearchPort、ObjectPort、CatalogRiskGate                                                                                                             | `event:OfferSynced`、`event:PartnerEnrolled` → ProductChanged、ListingPublished、ListingUnpublished                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | CatalogImport、CatalogSync、SearchProjection、MediaProcess                                               | E04/E08—E10/E26/E59/E63/E64；不存在ChannelCatalogCommand/Port；旧Listing原子生效                                                                                                                                       |
| Pricing       | In PricingQuotePort、PricingPreviewPort；Out CatalogPricingPort、PromotionPricePort、TaxPort                                                                                                                                                                             | `event:ListingPublished`、`event:CampaignChanged` → PriceQuoted、PriceRulePublished                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | PriceSync、QuoteWarm、RuleActivation；Quote Cache 按版本键                                               | E08/E11/E14/E65/E66；税/舍入/最低毛利/规则冲突/时区边界；无有效价则拒绝                                                                                                                                                |
| Inventory     | In InventoryReservationPort；Out CatalogSkuPort                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `event:ListingPublished`、`event:StockSynced`、`event:OrderCancelled`、`event:ReturnInspected` → StockReserved、StockCommitted、StockChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                | ListingPublishedInboxHandler、InventorySync、InventoryImport、ReservationExpiry；Availability Projection | E09显式Handler+Module注册；E27纯事件Inbox；不存在ChannelInventoryCommand/Port；E51只按restockableQty回补                                                                                                               |
| Experience    | In ExperienceFacade；Out CatalogPublicationPort、ObjectPort、CdnPort                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `event:ListingPublished` → ExperiencePublished                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | AssetProcess、CdnWarm、PublicationVerify                                                                 | 断链资产、失效商品、并发发布、CDN 失败；Head 不切换或切回前一 Snapshot                                                                                                                                                 |
| Marketing     | In PromotionPricePort、PromotionQuotePort、PromotionReservationPort；Out CatalogReadPort、PricingPreviewPort、MemberSummaryPort                                                                                                                                          | `event:ListingPublished`、`event:MemberChanged`、`event:OrderCancelled` → CampaignChanged、CampaignApplied、CampaignBudgetChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | CampaignStart、CampaignStop、DrawRun；Audience Projection                                                | E11/E15/E44/E56/E57/E63/E65/E67；预算争抢、释放、叠加冲突、重复抽奖                                                                                                                                                    |
| Cart          | In CartSnapshotPort/CartCommandPort；Out CatalogReadPort、PricingPreviewPort                                                                                                                                                                                             | `cmd:MergeCart`、`event:ListingUnpublished` → CartChanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | AbandonedCartExpiry；可重建 Cart Cache                                                                   | E12/E45/E64/E66；并发设备合并、失效 SKU、数量上限；冲突返回新版本供重试                                                                                                                                                |
| Checkout      | In CheckoutFacade；Out CartSnapshotPort、CartCommandPort、AddressReadPort、QualificationDecisionPort、PricingQuotePort、PromotionQuotePort、PromotionReservationPort、InventoryReservationPort、VoucherTenderPort、BenefitTenderPort、CreateOrderPort、PaymentIntentPort | `event:CartChanged`、`event:QualificationPolicyChanged` → QuoteCreated、QuoteConfirmed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | QuoteExpiry；短 TTL Quote Store                                                                          | E12—E19/E36/E45/E57/E60各有Port Test；地址只存脱敏快照；QuoteConfirmed不建单                                                                                                                                           |
| Order         | In CreateOrderPort、OrderReadPort、PaymentOrderPort、OrderSupportReadPort；Out ClockPort                                                                                                                                                                                 | `cmd:Create/Cancel/AfterSale/CheckPaymentCapture` → OrderPlaced、OrderCancelled、AfterSaleOpened、AfterSaleApproved                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | OrderExpiry、AfterSaleDeadline；商业Order Read Projection                                                | E19/E30/E40；跨模块写Port逐Operation，HTTP命令不伪装Public Port；PaymentOrderPort只锁定校验orderVersion/payable/cancelled，不写PaymentState；组合OrderView从Reporting的Payment投影装配，Order模块不消费PaymentCaptured |
| Fulfillment   | In FulfillmentPort；Out ChannelFulfillmentPort、OrderReadPort、NotificationPort                                                                                                                                                                                          | `event:PaymentCaptured`、`event:OrderCancelled`、`event:AfterSaleApproved`、`event:FulfillmentStatusReceived` → FulfillmentRequested、FulfillmentShipped、FulfillmentDelivered、FulfillmentFailed、ReturnInspected                                                                                                                                          | FulfillmentDispatch、TrackingPoll、ReturnRecovery                                                        | Provider Timeout Unknown先查单；E38/E39/E48/E50/E51、质检退款与回补各自闭合                                                                                                                                            |
| Verification  | In VerificationPort；Out VoucherRedemptionPort、MemberReadPort、StoreReadPort                                                                                                                                                                                            | `cmd:IssueChallenge/RevokeDevice/VerifyCode` → CodeVerified、CodeRejected                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | ChallengeRotate、ReceiptProjection；一次性 Nonce Cache                                                   | E25/E70/E71；DeviceRevoked不伪装跨模块Event；同步核销一次；CodeVerified只作证据                                                                                                                                        |
| Payment       | In PaymentIntentPort/PaymentFacade/Webhook；Out PaymentProvider、PaymentRiskGate、PaymentOrderPort、BenefitTenderPort、VoucherTenderPort、InventoryReservationPort、PromotionReservationPort                                                                             | `event:OrderCancelled`、`event:AfterSaleApproved`、`event:ReturnInspected` → PaymentCaptured、PaymentFailed、PaymentRefunded、LatePaymentRecoveryOpened、RefundRecoveryOpened                                                                                                                                                                               | ProviderSubmit、PaymentQuery、RefundRecovery、WebhookReplay                                              | PaymentIntent只由E36创建；Prepare只建Attempt并入`payment.provider.submit`，不得消费OrderPlaced补建第二Intent；E21/E32/E40—E44/E50/E55；RefundLeg全成功才发PaymentRefunded                                              |
| Voucher       | In VoucherTenderPort/VoucherRedemptionPort；Out CryptoPort                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `event:OrderCancelled` → VoucherIssued、VoucherReserved、VoucherRedeemed、VoucherReversed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | CardImport、VoucherExpiry、ReservationRecovery                                                           | E25同步命令是唯一核销驱动；无VerificationRead反向Port；卡密、核销、冲正竞态全测                                                                                                                                        |
| Benefit       | In BenefitTenderPort；Out MemberSummaryPort、QualificationDecisionPort                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `event:MemberChanged`、`event:OrderCancelled` → BenefitGranted、BenefitDebited、BenefitRestored、BenefitExpired                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | BenefitGrant、BenefitExpiry、GrantRecovery                                                               | E41/E53/E68/E72；Finance Inbox按Benefit经济腿唯一记账；批次重放、预算/恢复超额全拒绝                                                                                                                                   |
| Finance       | In AccountingPort/ReconciliationPort/SettlementPort；Out TaxPort、BankPort、ObjectPort                                                                                                                                                                                   | `event:PaymentCaptured`、`event:PaymentRefunded`、`event:LatePaymentRecoveryOpened`、`event:VoucherIssued`、`event:VoucherRedeemed`、`event:VoucherReversed`、`event:BenefitGranted`、`event:BenefitDebited`、`event:BenefitRestored`、`event:ChannelSettlementReceived` → EntryPosted、DifferenceResolved、SettlementApproved、PeriodClosed、InvoiceIssued | Reconciliation、Settlement、Invoice、WithdrawalRecovery                                                  | Accounting Leg Owner唯一；RefundRecoveryOpened不订阅；平衡、Maker≠Checker、关账差异为零                                                                                                                                |
| Channel       | In ChannelFacade/Webhook/ChannelFulfillmentPort；Out RemoteOrderSubmitter                                                                                                                                                                                                | `event:ExtensionEnabled`、`cmd:SyncRequested` → OfferSynced、StockSynced、ChannelSettlementReceived、FulfillmentStatusReceived、SyncCompleted                                                                                                                                                                                                               | CatalogSync、PriceSync、InventorySync、WebhookReplay                                                     | E26/E27只发事件且无Command Port；E29异步Finance；Cursor与Outbox同UoW                                                                                                                                                   |
| Support       | In SupportFacade；Out OrderSupportReadPort、MemberSummaryPort、NotificationPort                                                                                                                                                                                          | `event:NotificationDelivered`、`event:NotificationFailed`、`event:FulfillmentFailed` → TicketOpened、TicketAssigned、TicketEscalated                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | SlaTick、AutoAssign、ConversationArchive                                                                 | E30/E33/E39/E49/E69；越权查看、SLA竞态、重复升级；异常工单Inbox幂等                                                                                                                                                    |
| Notification  | In NotificationPort；Out SmsDelivery、WechatDelivery、InappDelivery                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `event:PaymentCaptured`、`event:PaymentRefunded`、`cmd:ChangePreference` → NotificationDelivered、NotificationFailed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Dispatch、Retry、ReceiptPoll；Preference Cache                                                           | E37/E46—E49；每个输入独立Handler；Provider限流、退订、模板变量和Replay全测                                                                                                                                             |
| Reporting     | In ProjectionConsumer/QueryPort；Out ObjectExport                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | MessageCatalog 中 reportingConsumer=true 的逐项 eventId → ProjectionAdvanced、ExportCompleted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Projection、Backfill、Export；Read Cache 带 Watermark                                                    | 重复/乱序/缺口事件、重建、旧水位；失败显示 Stale，不污染交易真相                                                                                                                                                       |
| Risk          | In AccessRiskGate、PaymentRiskGate、IdentityRiskGate、CatalogRiskGate；Out internal SignalProvider                                                                                                                                                                       | `cmd:EvaluateRisk` → RiskCaseOpened、RiskDecisionMade                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | RiskScan、RiskReplay、CaseEscalation；Decision Cache按策略版本                                           | E31/E32/E58/E59；模型超时、规则冲突、对抗输入、人工复核；高风险动作默认拒绝                                                                                                                                            |
| Audit         | In AuditAppender/Query；Out ObjectArchive、KMS                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | OperationCatalog/MessageCatalog 中 auditRequired=true 的逐项 operationId/eventId → AuditRecorded、AuditArchived                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Digest、Archive、IntegrityScan；只读索引 Projection                                                      | Hash 链断裂、归档缺失、重放篡改；冻结删除并发出最高级告警                                                                                                                                                              |
| Extension     | In ExtensionRegistry、ProviderCapabilityPort；Out internal SecretRefPort、HealthProbePort                                                                                                                                                                                | `cmd:ManifestDiscovered`、`job:HealthProbe` → ExtensionEnabled、ExtensionDisabled、ExtensionUpgraded                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | HealthProbe、VersionCanary；Registry Cache                                                               | E34；签名失败、能力越权、升级失败、熔断；原子切回前版本并撤销租约                                                                                                                                                      |

`event:`前缀才进入DomainDependencyGraph并要求唯一Publisher、Consumer、edgeId、Inbox与Replay；`cmd:`是Operation或公开Port触发，`job:`是Job Catalog触发，二者不得伪装成Consumed Event。未带前缀的右侧名称都是该Owner发出的Canonical Event Type，并由`events.yml`生成唯一ID。

#### 13.0.3 容易漂移事件的唯一ID、Publisher与Consumer

下表是本轮审计发现的高风险事件闭合裁决；`events.yml`必须逐行生成Type、Schema、Publisher、Consumer、Inbox、Replay和测试，任何短名、泛化别名或隐式通配都非法。Reporting与Audit的消费边仍由Message Catalog按每个具体eventId展开，不用“所有事件”占位。

| Canonical ID                        | Type                       | 唯一Publisher | 业务Consumer与edgeId                                                          | 强制证明                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `marketing.campaign.changed`        | CampaignChanged            | Marketing     | Pricing / E11                                                                 | Pricing Inbox Duplicate、OutOfOrder、Replay与Quote Cache失效测试                                                                                                                                      |
| `marketing.campaign.budget.changed` | CampaignBudgetChanged      | Marketing     | Reporting按具体ID生成边                                                       | Budget Version、Projection Replay与金额守恒测试                                                                                                                                                       |
| `qualification.policy.changed`      | QualificationPolicyChanged | Qualification | Checkout / E13                                                                | Checkout Inbox幂等、旧Quote失效、重放不重复补偿测试                                                                                                                                                   |
| `order.aftersale.approved`          | AfterSaleApproved          | Order         | Payment / E21仅`returnRequired=false`；Fulfillment / E35                      | 两个独立Inbox；免退与需退分支、授权金额、Duplicate与Replay测试                                                                                                                                        |
| `fulfillment.return.inspected`      | ReturnInspected            | Fulfillment   | Payment / E50；Inventory / E51                                                | inspectionVersion、accepted/rejected/restockableQty、authorizedRefundMoney、Evidence Hash；两Consumer独立幂等与Replay                                                                                 |
| `fulfillment.status.received`       | FulfillmentStatusReceived  | Channel       | Fulfillment / E38                                                             | Provider Version单调、回调验签、防重、倒序与Unknown主动查询测试                                                                                                                                       |
| `payment.captured`                  | PaymentCaptured            | Payment       | Fulfillment / E20；Finance / E22；Notification / E37；Reporting按具体ID生成边 | PaymentOrderPort确认订单可捕获，且Inventory、Voucher、Benefit、Marketing四个Hold/Reservation全部提交后发布；Finance只记外部Tender Leg；Fulfillment恰好一次、通知幂等、GMV/OrderView Watermark与Replay |
| `payment.refunded`                  | PaymentRefunded            | Payment       | Finance / E22；Notification / E37；Reporting按具体ID生成边                    | 所有必需RefundLeg终态后发布；Finance只记外部Tender Leg；累计上限、时间线、通知与Replay                                                                                                                |
| `payment.late.recovery.opened`      | LatePaymentRecoveryOpened  | Payment       | Finance / E22；Payment Recovery Job                                           | 必须携带新增外部入账观察；只记Suspense；Fulfillment Consumer不存在；自动退款和重放测试                                                                                                                |
| `payment.refund.recovery.opened`    | RefundRecoveryOpened       | Payment       | Payment Recovery Job                                                          | 不代表新增资金、Finance Consumer不存在；逐Leg查单/重试/人工修复与Replay测试                                                                                                                           |
| `notification.delivered`            | NotificationDelivered      | Notification  | Support / E33                                                                 | 回执Inbox、Ticket Evidence、Duplicate与Replay测试                                                                                                                                                     |
| `notification.failed`               | NotificationFailed         | Notification  | Support / E33                                                                 | 重试耗尽、Ticket Evidence、Duplicate与Replay测试                                                                                                                                                      |
| `verification.code.verified`        | CodeVerified               | Verification  | Reporting与Audit按具体ID生成边                                                | E25已同步完成核销；本事件只投影验证证据，Replay不得再次调用Voucher                                                                                                                                    |
| `verification.code.rejected`        | CodeRejected               | Verification  | Reporting与Audit按具体ID生成边                                                | 失败不消耗权益、风险证据与Replay测试                                                                                                                                                                  |

对每一行，最低测试集合固定为 Domain Unit、Port Contract、Repository Integration、Handler/UoW、Event Duplicate/OutOfOrder、Job Crash/Resume、Cache Rebuild、Permission/Scope、Failure/Compensation 和 Trace Assertion。任何空字段都表示该模块尚未完成，而不是“无此数据流”。

### 13.1 Identity

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as IdentityController
    participant P as IdentityPipeline
    participant H as LoginHandler
    participant V as CredentialVerifier
    participant R as IdentityRepository
    participant S as SessionRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: LoginInput
    C->>P: validate rate risk target
    P->>H: typed LoginCommand
    H->>R: load principal and credential
    H->>V: verify credential and assurance
    V-->>H: verified principal
    H->>S: create tracked revocable session
    H->>O: SessionCreated
    H-->>C: SessionView
    C-->>UI: secure target session
```

Identity 只写 identity Schema。会员档案和 Membership 由事件或公开 Port 创建，禁止 Identity 直接写 member 或 access。

### 13.2 Organization

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as OrganizationController
    participant H as ChangeHierarchyHandler
    participant R as OrganizationRepository
    participant P as HierarchyPolicy
    participant X as ClosureRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: parent unit expected version
    C->>H: typed command
    H->>R: lock unit and parent
    H->>P: validate kind tenant cycle quota
    P-->>H: decision
    H->>R: save aggregate version
    H->>X: update affected closure paths
    H->>O: OrgUnitChanged
    H-->>C: OrganizationView
```

跨 Tenant 移动一律拒绝；技术 Tenant 可在 UI 隐藏，但不能从数据模型省略。

### 13.3 Access

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Request
    participant S as SessionResolver
    participant M as MembershipRepository
    participant G as GrantRepository
    participant R as ResourceScopeRegistry
    participant C as CapabilityDecisionPort
    participant P as AccessPolicy
    participant D as DecisionSink
    Request->>S: target session
    S-->>Request: principal session version
    Request->>M: active membership
    Request->>G: roles grants denies
    Request->>R: owner resolver resource ids
    Request->>C: capability and quota snapshot
    Request->>P: complete AccessContext
    P-->>Request: allow deny stepup with reasons
    Request->>D: immutable decision evidence
```

ResourceScopeRegistry 由各 Owner 模块注册窄 Resolver，逐步删除知道所有业务表的巨大 access.resource_scope。

注册与导入入会链不经旧`AccessPort`，固定为：

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Admin
    participant AH as CreateMembershipEnrollmentHandler
    participant AR as AccessRepository
    participant AO as AccessOutbox
    participant II as MembershipEnrollmentCreatedInboxHandler
    participant IR as IdentityRepository
    participant User
    participant RH as RegisterIdentityHandler
    participant IO as IdentityOutbox
    participant AI as IdentityRegisteredInboxHandler
    participant MI as MemberIdentityRegisteredInboxHandler

    Admin->>AH: organization role scope invitee expectedVersion
    AH->>AR: lock policy and create one-time Enrollment
    AH->>AO: MembershipEnrollmentCreated(enrollmentRef, purpose, expiry)
    AO-->>II: E76 at-least-once
    II->>IR: create Identity invitation with opaque enrollmentRef
    IR-->>User: invitation through Notification intent
    User->>RH: register with invitation and credentials
    RH->>IR: consume invitation and create Identity
    RH->>IO: IdentityRegistered(identityId, enrollmentRef)
    par Owner inboxes
        IO-->>AI: E75
        AI->>AR: consume Enrollment once and activate Membership/Role/Scope
    and Member profile
        IO-->>MI: create or link Member profile
    end
```

`CreateMembershipEnrollmentHandler` 和`MembershipEnrollment`属Access，在同一Access UoW保存预授权Role/Scope快照、一次Ref Hash、Purpose、Expiry、State、Audit与E76 Outbox。Identity的`MembershipEnrollmentCreatedInboxHandler` 只创建带opaque Ref的Invitation，不复制Grant；E75 Access Inbox只在Enrollment为Pending、未过期、Ref Hash/组织/目的匹配时CAS到Consumed并建Membership。E76/E75重复均由独立Inbox Receipt吸收；E76永久失败保留Enrollment Pending/Review，E75永久失败保留Identity但不授权，通过审计的ManageMembership重新发起，绝不用默认Role放行。Member导入的E73不经Identity Invitation，只在Access Policy校验导入组织/Client后建`invited`状态Membership。

### 13.4 Capability

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as CapabilityController
    participant H as AssignPackageHandler
    participant R as PackageRepository
    participant A as AssignmentRepository
    participant Q as QuotaPolicy
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: package target period
    C->>H: AssignPackageCommand
    H->>R: load published package version
    H->>A: lock active assignments
    H->>Q: validate parent entitlement and quota
    Q-->>H: allowed allocation
    H->>A: close prior save new increment version
    H->>O: CapabilityChanged
    H-->>C: EffectiveCapabilityView
```

套餐版本不可原地修改；菜单、路由和 Use Case 都引用同一个 Capability Code，但 Capability 不替代 Permission。

### 13.5 Partner

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as PartnerController
    participant H as EnrollPartnerHandler
    participant D as PartnerDeduplicator
    participant R as PartnerRepository
    participant P as AgreementPolicy
    participant A as AgreementRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: legal identity scope agreement
    C->>H: EnrollPartnerCommand
    H->>D: normalize and match evidence
    D->>R: find global subject
    R-->>D: canonical subject or none
    H->>P: validate authorization and dates
    H->>A: save enrollment and agreement version
    H->>O: PartnerEnrolled
    H-->>C: PartnerView
```

Supplier、Brand、Store 是全球 Partner；租户可见性来自 Enrollment 和 Agreement，结束关系不删除历史。

### 13.6 Member

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as MemberController
    participant H as ImportMemberHandler
    participant F as ObjectPort
    participant D as MemberMatcher
    participant R as MemberRepository
    participant G as GroupPolicy
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: object reference mapping hash
    C->>H: StartMemberImport
    H->>F: scan and stream validated chunks
    loop each chunk
        F-->>H: normalized candidate rows
        H->>D: deduplicate principal links
        H->>R: upsert tenant profiles
        H->>G: calculate groups and level
        H->>O: MemberChanged
    end
    H-->>C: progress and safe error report
```

自然人 Principal 与租户 MemberProfile 分离；行级失败不泄露其他会员信息。

### 13.7 Qualification

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Caller
    participant S as QualificationService
    participant R as PolicyRepository
    participant M as MemberQualificationPort
    participant G as OrgQualificationPort
    participant P as PartnerQualificationPort
    participant E as SpecificationEngine
    participant D as DecisionRepository
    Caller->>S: member listing quantity context
    S->>R: published rule versions
    par bounded facts
        S->>M: profile groups counters E05
        S->>G: organization unit and city scope E06
        S->>P: supplier brand store agreement scope E07
    end
    S->>E: ordered specifications
    E-->>S: allow reasons evidence
    S->>D: save immutable decision snapshot
    S-->>Caller: QualificationDecision
```

优先级固定为明确拒绝、法规合同、企业资格、商业范围、限购、默认允许。预览与下单共用引擎，下单重新判定。

### 13.8 Catalog

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Channel
    participant I as CatalogIntakeHandler
    participant N as CatalogNormalizer
    participant D as ProductMatcher
    participant R as CatalogRepository
    participant W as ReviewPolicy
    participant L as ListingRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Channel->>I: canonical source records and cursor
    I->>N: normalize unit media attribute
    N-->>I: candidates and errors
    I->>D: barcode brand source key match
    D->>R: load product sku mapping
    I->>R: save source offer and candidate
    I->>W: changed field review
    W-->>I: approved rejected
    I->>L: save listing version
    I->>O: ListingPublished
```

Product 描述是什么，SourceOffer 描述谁提供，Listing 描述在哪个商城卖。三个对象不可混成一张表。

### 13.9 Pricing

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Caller
    participant H as CreatePriceQuoteHandler
    participant O as CatalogPricingPort
    participant R as PriceRuleRepository
    participant E as PricingEngine
    participant M as MarginPolicy
    participant Q as QuoteRepository
    Caller->>H: listing quantity member channel time
    H->>O: cost tax provider facts
    H->>R: effective ordered rule versions
    H->>E: deterministic calculation
    E-->>H: component breakdown and rounding
    H->>M: floor and margin validation
    M-->>H: allow review reject
    H->>Q: persist immutable expiring quote
    H-->>Caller: PriceQuote
```

相同事实、规则版本、舍入和 Clock 必须产生相同报价；人工改价创建新规则版本。

### 13.10 Inventory

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Checkout
    participant H as ReserveInventoryHandler
    participant R as StockRepository
    participant P as AvailabilityPolicy
    participant V as ReservationRepository
    participant M as MovementRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Checkout->>H: order sku quantities expiry
    H->>R: lock stock rows sorted by sku
    R-->>H: onhand active reserved safety version
    H->>P: validate availability
    P-->>H: decision
    H->>V: create active reservations
    H->>M: append reserve movements
    H->>O: StockReserved
    H-->>Checkout: reservation ids versions expiry
```

Commit、Release、Expire 和 Restock 都进入同一 Reservation 状态机并带语义幂等键，禁止直接维护 reservedQty。

### 13.11 Experience

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as ExperienceController
    participant H as PublishExperienceHandler
    participant R as DraftRepository
    participant V as ExperienceValidator
    participant S as SnapshotRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: application draft version ETag
    C->>H: PublishExperienceCommand
    H->>R: lock current draft
    H->>V: schema assets links listing visibility
    V-->>H: report
    H->>S: create immutable snapshot and staged manifest
    H->>O: ExperiencePublished
    H-->>C: publication id snapshot version
```

CDN 预热由提交后消费者执行，完整性确认后才原子切换 PublishedHead；回滚只切 Head。

---

### 13.12 Marketing

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Checkout
    participant H as QuotePromotionHandler
    participant R as CampaignRepository
    participant A as AudiencePolicy
    participant B as BudgetRepository
    participant E as PromotionEngine
    participant Q as PromotionQuoteRepository
    Checkout->>H: cart qualification context time
    H->>R: effective campaign versions
    H->>A: audience stacking eligibility
    A-->>H: applicable campaigns
    H->>B: available budget snapshot
    H->>E: deterministic benefit calculation
    E-->>H: discount reward breakdown
    H->>Q: save evidence and expiry
    H-->>Checkout: PromotionQuote
```

叠加、互斥、预算、次数、退款返还由 Campaign 版本定义；抽奖使用可审计随机源。

### 13.13 Cart

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as CartController
    participant H as ChangeCartHandler
    participant R as CartRepository
    participant L as CatalogReadPort
    participant Q as QuantityPolicy
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: add change remove input
    C->>H: typed command
    H->>R: load current cart and version
    H->>L: visible listing summary
    H->>Q: normalize quantity and hard limit
    H->>R: save cart version
    H->>O: CartChanged
    H-->>C: CartView and warnings
    C-->>UI: authoritative cart
```

Cart 保存购买意图，不锁价格或库存；localStorage 只可缓存未登录草稿，登录后以服务端 Cart 为准。

### 13.14 Checkout

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as CheckoutController
    participant H as CreateCheckoutQuoteHandler
    participant Ports as Qualified Read Ports
    participant E as CheckoutComposer
    participant R as CheckoutRepository
    participant S as QuoteSigner
    UI->>C: selected cart address tender
    C->>H: CreateCheckoutQuote
    H->>Ports: parallel bounded fact requests
    Ports-->>H: cart qualification price promotion voucher benefit stock
    H->>E: compose totals rejections versions
    E-->>H: canonical quote
    H->>S: sign hash and expiry
    H->>R: save immutable quote
    H-->>C: CheckoutQuoteView
```

Checkout 还拥有 PurchaseProcess 协调器。它通过 Owner Port 完成原子下单，不读取各域表。

### 13.15 Order

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Checkout
    participant H as CreateOrderHandler
    participant P as OrderPolicy
    participant S as OrderSnapshotFactory
    participant R as OrderRepository
    participant A as AfterSaleRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Checkout->>H: validated order creation facts
    H->>P: validate commercial invariants
    P-->>H: allowed
    H->>S: immutable product price identity address party snapshots
    S-->>H: order aggregate data
    H->>R: save order suborders lines version
    H->>O: OrderPlaced
    H-->>Checkout: OrderReceipt
    Note over A: after sale is separate aggregate referencing order lines
```

Order 不依赖 Payment 或 Checkout 内部实现，也不直接读 Cart、Inventory、Voucher、Benefit 表。

### 13.16 Fulfillment

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Inbox
    participant H as StartFulfillmentHandler
    participant R as FulfillmentRepository
    participant P as FulfillmentSplitPolicy
    participant C as ChannelFulfillmentPort
    participant T as TrackingRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Inbox->>H: PaymentCaptured event
    H->>R: load or create by semantic key
    H->>P: split supplier location method
    H->>R: save pending fulfillment orders
    H->>O: FulfillmentRequested
    Note over H,C: commit before provider call
    H->>C: async submit canonical requests
    C-->>H: receipt unknown or rejection
    H->>T: append tracking observation
    alt definitive rejection
        H->>O: FulfillmentFailed
    else accepted or unknown
        H->>R: save receipt or recovery state without new public event
    end
```

外部失败不回滚已支付订单；未知状态先查询，不能盲目重复提交。

### 13.17 Verification

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Store
    participant C as VerificationController
    participant D as DeviceAuthorizer
    participant H as VerifyCodeHandler
    participant R as ChallengeRepository
    participant S as VerificationScopePolicy
    participant V as VoucherRedemptionPort
    participant O as Outbox
    Store->>C: dynamic code item amount
    C->>D: authorize device clerk store
    C->>H: VerifyCodeCommand
    H->>R: consume challenge once with lock
    H->>S: validate mall store agreement purpose
    H->>V: redeem authorized asset
    V-->>H: redemption receipt
    H->>O: CodeVerified
    H-->>C: safe receipt
```

动态码短时、一次性、绑定用途；请求中 mallId 不能扩大授权，金额或商品变化必须新建 Challenge。

### 13.18 Payment

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Checkout
    participant I as PaymentIntentPort
    participant C as CreatePaymentIntentHandler
    participant H as PreparePaymentHandler
    participant R as PaymentRepository
    participant A as TenderAllocationPolicy
    participant Q as JobQueue
    participant P as ProviderSubmitJobHandler
    Checkout->>I: create intent and tender plan E36
    I->>C: CreatePaymentIntent semantic key order version hold refs
    C->>A: allocate immutable tender plan from existing Owner holds
    C->>R: insert intent exactly once in PurchaseProcess UoW
    C-->>Checkout: intent id and plan version
    Checkout->>H: prepare existing intent and client context
    H->>R: lock existing intent, reject missing or version mismatch
    H->>R: save one pending attempt by intent and semantic key
    H->>Q: enqueue payment.provider.submit in same UoW
    H-->>Checkout: PaymentAction pending with poll or SSE location
    Q->>P: claim committed prepared attempt
    P->>R: claim prepared provider attempt
    P->>P: call provider outside transaction
    P->>R: finalize success failure or unknown
```

`CreatePaymentIntentHandler`是E36的唯一Intent/Tender Plan落点，只复用Checkout已经建立的Inventory、Voucher、Benefit和Marketing Hold引用，不再次Hold，也不创建Attempt或调用Provider。`PreparePaymentHandler`只能针对既有Intent创建一个Attempt并在同UoW写`payment.provider.submit`；`ProviderSubmitJobHandler`是唯一预支付外调者。`OrderPlaced`对Payment的Consumer、补建Intent分支、`CreatePayment`/`CreatePrepay`第二命令和同步Provider调用全部必须为零。回调先 durable Inbox，后由 Payment 状态机处理。PaymentSettlement 不得反查 runtime.outbox 获取订单事实。

### 13.19 Voucher

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as VoucherController
    participant H as IssueVoucherHandler
    participant A as VoucherApprovalPolicy
    participant P as CardPoolRepository
    participant R as VoucherRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: approved reserve preview id
    C->>H: IssueVoucherBatch
    H->>A: verify approval separation and expiry
    H->>P: lock available cards stable order
    H->>R: create voucher instances
    H->>P: mark cards allocated
    H->>O: VoucherIssued
    H-->>C: batch receipt
```

卡号、券实例和核销记录分离；卡密信封加密，核销与冲正使用唯一外部引用。

### 13.20 Benefit

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant UI
    participant C as BenefitController
    participant H as StartGrantBatchHandler
    participant P as BenefitPlanRepository
```
