```mermaid
    participant B as BudgetRepository
    participant M as MemberSummaryPort
    participant J as BenefitGrantJobHandler
    participant O as Outbox
    UI->>C: plan audience amount
    C->>H: StartGrantBatch
    H->>P: approved plan version
    H->>M: freeze recipient snapshot
    H->>B: reserve total budget
    H-->>C: batch id

    alt recipient grant committed
        J->>O: BenefitGranted
    else recipient rejected
        J->>B: save typed row failure for retry or review
    end
```

批次支持 Pause、Resume、Cancel 未执行部分和重试；预算占用、实发、撤回、过期、退款均有独立分录。

---

### 13.21 Finance

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Event as OwnerEvent
    participant I as FinanceInbox
    participant H as PostJournalHandler
    participant P as PostingPolicy
    participant R as JournalRepository
    participant A as AccountRepository
    participant B as BalanceProjection
    participant O as Outbox
    Event->>I: deliver eventId ownerEventId aggregateVersion
    I->>I: claim consumerId eventId sourceEventId
    I->>H: immutable accounting fact and economicLegId
    H->>R: find ownerEventId semantic idempotency reference
    H->>P: build balanced debit credit entries
    P-->>H: immutable journal
    H->>A: lock accounts stable order
    H->>R: append posted journal and entries
    H->>B: update rebuildable balance projection
    H->>O: EntryPosted
    H->>I: commit effect and Inbox receipt in one UoW
```

Finance 内部分 accounting、reconciliation、settlement、invoice 四个子域，共享 Finance Module 公共查询与人工操作合同。Payment、Voucher、Benefit和Channel只发布E22—E24/E29登记的Owner Event；各自独立Finance Inbox Handler是唯一自动记账入口，源Owner不接收同步Journal Receipt，也不存在面向这些Owner的同步`FinancePort`。Console或内部受权流程只能经`AccountingPort`、`ReconciliationPort`、`SettlementPort`执行查询/人工命令，不能代替事件记账。

### 13.22 Channel

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Scheduler
    participant H as RunChannelSyncHandler
    participant R as ConnectionRepository
    participant G as ProviderRegistry
    participant P as Provider
    participant M as AnticorruptionMapper
    participant S as SyncRunRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Scheduler->>H: connection sync kind
    H->>R: active connection and secret reference
    H->>G: resolve signed declared capability
    G-->>H: provider adapter
    H->>P: pull batch after cursor
    P-->>H: remote batch next cursor receipt
    H->>M: validate normalize
    H->>S: persist raw hash mapping row errors
    alt catalog or price sync
        H->>O: OfferSynced
    else inventory sync
        H->>O: StockSynced
    else statement sync
        H->>O: ChannelSettlementReceived
    end
    H->>O: SyncCompleted
    H->>S: advance cursor after durable acceptance
```

Channel 只拥有连接、原始报文、映射和游标；Finance 不读 channel.statement，Catalog 不读 Provider JSON。

### 13.23 Support

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant User
    participant C as SupportController
    participant H as CreateTicketHandler
    participant R as ConversationRepository
    participant A as AssignmentPolicy
    participant S as SlaPolicy
    participant T as TicketRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    User->>C: message resource reference
    C->>H: CreateOrAppendConversation
    H->>R: load scoped conversation
    H->>A: qualified available agent
    H->>S: priority and deadlines
    H->>T: save sanitized message and ticket
    H->>O: TicketAssigned
    H-->>C: TicketView
```

附件进入私有对象存储；客服只看最小必要 PII，AI 建议不能自动退款或改变订单事实。

### 13.24 Notification

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Source as EventOrNotificationPort
    participant C as CreateDispatchIntentHandler
    participant T as TemplateRepository
    participant P as PreferenceRepository
    participant R as DispatchRepository
    participant J as JobQueue
    participant H as DispatchJobHandler
    participant G as DeliveryRegistry
    participant D as DeliveryAdapter
    participant O as Outbox
    Source->>C: typed event or DispatchIntent
    C->>T: approved template version
    C->>P: consent preference quiet hours
    C->>R: create pending dispatch by semantic key
    C->>J: enqueue notification.dispatch in same UoW
    J->>H: claim dispatch after commit
    H->>G: resolve declared channel
    G-->>H: adapter
    H->>D: send idempotently outside transaction
    D-->>H: receipt retryable or permanent failure
    H->>R: append attempt and provider reference
    alt delivered
        H->>O: append NotificationDelivered once
    else retryable and budget remains
        H->>J: enqueue notification.retry with backoff
    else permanent or retry budget exhausted
        H->>O: append NotificationFailed once
    end
```

`NotificationPort`只进入`CreateDispatchIntentHandler`并持久化Pending Dispatch；`DispatchJobHandler`才可调用Delivery Adapter。一次Retryable失败不得发布`NotificationFailed`，只有明确Permanent或重试预算耗尽才发布一次最终失败事件。模板变量使用白名单 DTO，交易通知与营销通知使用不同同意规则。

### 13.25 Reporting

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Inbox
    participant H as ProjectionHandler
    participant W as WatermarkRepository
    participant F as FactRepository
    participant P as ProjectionRepository
    participant C as Cache
    participant Q as ReportQuery
    Inbox->>H: typed ordered event
    H->>W: compare aggregate version
    W-->>H: next duplicate or regression
    H->>F: append normalized fact
    H->>P: update scoped aggregates
    H->>W: advance consumer watermark
    H->>C: invalidate versioned keys
    Q->>P: scope period dimensions cursor
    P-->>Q: metrics and watermark
```

Reporting 只读自身 Fact 与 Projection，不回查业务 Schema；导出冻结 Query、AccessContext 和水位线。

### 13.26 Risk

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Usecase
    participant H as EvaluateRiskHandler
    participant R as RiskPolicyRepository
    participant S as SignalReadPorts
    participant E as RiskEngine
    participant C as RiskCaseRepository
    participant O as Outbox
    Usecase->>H: actor action resource amount context
    H->>R: published policy versions
    H->>S: velocity device lists prior cases
    S-->>H: minimized signals
    H->>E: deterministic and scored rules
    E-->>H: allow challenge review deny evidence
    alt review or deny
        H->>C: create or update case
        H->>O: RiskCaseOpened
    end
    H-->>Usecase: safe decision reason
```

策略必须版本化、灰度、回放并监控误伤；模型分数不能绕过确定性拒绝、额度或人工审批。

### 13.27 Audit

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Pipeline
    participant H as RecordAuditHandler
    participant D as AuditRedactor
    participant S as EvidenceSigner
    participant R as AuditRepository
    participant A as ArchiveJob
    Pipeline->>H: access decision action hashes reason
    H->>D: remove secrets minimize PII
    D-->>H: sanitized evidence
    H->>S: chain prior digest and sign
    S-->>H: integrity digest
    H->>R: append immutable record
    R-->>H: audit id
    A->>R: stream retention eligible records
    A->>A: encrypt archive verify delete hot copy
```

高风险写的 Audit 失败必须失败关闭。读取和导出 Audit 本身也写 Audit。

### 13.28 Extension

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Admin
    participant C as ExtensionController
    participant H as InstallExtensionHandler
    participant V as ManifestVerifier
    participant R as ExtensionRepository
    participant G as ExtensionRegistry
    participant E as Extension
    participant O as Outbox
    Admin->>C: signed package config refs
    C->>H: InstallExtensionCommand
    H->>V: signature api permissions schema
    V-->>H: validated manifest
    H->>R: save disabled installation
    H->>G: register declared capabilities

    E-->>G: healthy evidence
```

升级先并行验证新版本，再小流量、原子切换 Registry；失败切回前一版本，卸载保留业务映射和审计证据。

---

## 14. 合同、事件与异步管线根治

### 14.1 当前缺口

现有 Operation Catalog 能驱动路由，但 input 和 output 仍缺完整 Schema，SDK 公开 call 的泛型默认是 unknown；事件 payload 同样是 unknown。现有绿色审计只能证明目录和文件存在，不能证明协议正确。

当前约 706 处普通 Error 与少量 DomainError 并存，Error Mapper 依赖 message 前缀猜测。这个模型必须删除。

### 14.2 Contract v2

217 只表示 2026-08-25 的现有目录基线，不是终态合同数，更不能被描述为“217 个已强类型 Operation”。Contract v2 的目标数量由补齐 Product Detail、Order Detail、Finance Preview 等缺口后的 Catalog 自动生成；质量门只比较 Catalog Hash 和逐项完整性，不写死业务数量。

Contract Source 为唯一手写真值，生成：

```text
Contract Source
├─ OpenAPI
├─ OperationCatalog
├─ Server Parser
├─ Handler Interface
├─ Typed SDK
├─ Error Union
├─ Event Schema
├─ Consumer Type
├─ Permission Matrix
└─ Requirement Trace
```

每个 Operation 必须有明确的：

```text
PathInput
QueryInput
BodyInput
SuccessOutput
ErrorUnion
CursorContract
IfMatchRequired
IdempotencyScope
ResourceResolver
Permission
Capability
Assurance
RateClass
Requirements
```

SDK 的 generic call 只能作为包内部实现，不向应用开发者公开。应用使用：

```text
commerce.order.orders.read(input, context)
commerce.catalog.products.detail.read(input, context)
commerce.finance.reconciliations.preview(input, context)
```

### 14.3 统一响应

成功：

```json
{
  "data": {},
  "meta": {
    "requestId": "opaque",
    "nextCursor": "opaque or null",
    "watermark": "optional UTC",
    "version": 1
  }
}
```

失败：

```json
{
  "error": {
    "code": "STABLE_CODE",
    "message": "safe localized message",
    "details": {},
    "retryable": false
  },
  "meta": {
    "requestId": "opaque"
  }
}
```

DomainError 是判别联合，不从 Error.message 推导 HTTP 状态。未知错误只在服务端记录安全指纹，对外为 INTERNAL_ERROR。

### 14.4 Event Envelope v2

```text
eventId
eventType
eventVersion
schemaVersion
aggregateType
aggregateId
aggregateVersion
tenantId
scopeId
actorId
occurredAt
correlationId
causationId
traceId
payloadHash
payload
headers
```

Outbox 最低唯一约束：

```text
aggregateType + aggregateId + aggregateVersion + eventType
```

消费者幂等：

```text
consumer + eventId
```

投影按 aggregateVersion 拒绝旧版本覆盖新版本；每个消费者的业务效果和 Inbox Complete 在一个事务中。

### 14.5 Operation Pipeline

保留 OperationHandler Registry，删除并行的 God ModuleOperations、未采用 CommandBus 与 QueryBus，最终只有一条入口：

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    Parse --> Identity
    Identity --> Access
    Access --> Risk
    Risk --> Idempotency
    Idempotency --> Transaction
    Transaction --> Handler
    Handler --> Aggregate
    Aggregate --> Repository
    Repository --> Outbox
    Outbox --> Audit
    Audit --> Commit
    Commit --> Present
```

每个 Operation 对应一个 Schema、一个 Controller、一个 Handler。共享 Pipeline，不共享巨型 OperationId 到 SQL Closure 映射。

### 14.6 Job 可靠性

目标`definitions/jobs.yml`唯一包含72项`kind=business`与4项`kind=technical`，总计76项；完整`jobId→Owner→Handler→唯一文件`镜像见受Hash约束的《福利商城架构和补齐修改清单》11.2及执行卷12.2。Payment的`payment.provider.submit`唯一负责提交后外调预支付Provider；`payment.refund.recover`虽沿用Recovery命名，却是所有外部RefundLeg的唯一提交器，按payload `action=submitOrRecover`处理Pending初次提交、Unknown先查单和Retryable受控重试，不新增第五个Payment Job。四项技术ID固定为`runtime.outbox.relay`、`runtime.message.recover`、`runtime.idempotency.expire`、`observability.telemetry.expire`；不存在泛化`cleanup` Job。CI同时断言`business=72`、`technical=4`、`total=76`和三类Hash，当前32项全部进入同一`JobContinuity.ts`逐项处置。

每个 Job 定义：

```text
id
owner
queue
payloadSchema
aggregateKey
concurrency
deadline
lease
retryPolicy
idempotency
deadletter
runbook
metrics
```

必须补齐：

- claim 时直接回收过期 Running Lease，或用独立高频 Recovery；不能最多滞留一小时。
- Job Payload 强类型。
- 同聚合按 aggregateVersion 顺序执行，不靠 occurredAt 猜顺序。
- Deadline 到期后中止外部请求。
- Deadletter Replay 使用原 Event、Consumer、Attempt、预览和 Step-up。
- 重试只针对显式 Retryable Error，使用指数退避与 Jitter。
- 外部超时后的 Unknown 状态进入 Query Recovery，不盲重试有副作用命令。

---

## 15. 数据模型、事务、锁与数据库

### 15.1 Schema 所有权

| Schema           | 唯一写 Owner       |
| ---------------- | ------------------ |
| identity         | Identity           |
| organization     | Organization       |
| access           | Access             |
| capability       | Capability         |
| partner          | Partner            |
| member           | Member             |
| qualification    | Qualification      |
| catalog          | Catalog            |
| pricing          | Pricing            |
| inventory        | Inventory          |
| experience       | Experience         |
| marketing        | Marketing          |
| cart             | Cart               |
| checkout         | Checkout           |
| ordering         | Order              |
| fulfillment      | Fulfillment        |
| verification     | Verification       |
| payment          | Payment            |
| voucher          | Voucher            |
| benefit          | Benefit            |
| finance、invoice | Finance            |
| channel          | Channel            |
| support          | Support            |
| notification     | Notification       |
| reporting        | Reporting          |
| risk             | Risk               |
| audit            | Audit              |
| extension        | Extension          |
| runtime          | Foundation Runtime |

跨域只存 Opaque ID、版本、Hash 和必要不可变 Snapshot。跨域 Join 只允许：

1. Owner 自己的表内 Join。
2. Reporting 消费事件后的自身 Projection。
3. 经批准、只读、稳定 Contract View，且有 Owner、版本与性能测试。

普通模块禁止直接 SELECT 或 WRITE 其他业务 Schema。

### 15.2 所有表共同字段

业务表按适用性包含：

```text
id
tenant_id
scope_id
state
version
created_at
created_by
updated_at
updated_by
```

- ID 使用 UUIDv7 或有序、不可猜测 ID。
- 金额使用 bigint minor 与 ISO currency。
- 时间使用 timestamptz UTC。
- 乐观并发通过 version。
- 外部标识使用 Provider 加 ExternalId 复合唯一。
- PII 使用 Envelope Encryption，保存 keyVersion。
- Append-only 表禁止 Update 与 Delete。

### 15.3 锁顺序

全系统固定以下`LockRank`；跨Owner共享事务必须先由各Port返回LockIntent，再由`LockCoordinator`一次性按Rank与同类ID排序加锁，之后才允许Mutation。时序图中的业务调用顺序不能推翻该表。新插入且尚不可见的Order/Reservation/Outbox行不需要`select for update`，但其唯一约束必须在同一事务中验证。

| Rank | 可锁资源                                      | 同类排序键                     | 适用流程                                         |
| ---: | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
|   10 | Inbox Receipt / Idempotency / PurchaseProcess | semanticKey                    | 所有命令与Consumer先Claim                        |
|   20 | Cart                                          | cartId                         | Checkout Confirm                                 |
|   30 | Qualification Counter                         | policyId、subjectId            | 限购扣减；纯Decision/Price Quote只读不加锁       |
|   40 | Existing Order / AfterSale / Return           | orderId、afterSaleId、returnId | Capture、Cancel、售后、质检；新Order插入不占该锁 |
|   50 | Inventory StockItem / Reservation             | skuId、reservationId           | Reserve/Commit/Release/Restock                   |
|   60 | Voucher / Voucher Reservation                 | voucherId                      | Hold/Capture/Reverse/Release                     |
|   70 | Benefit Account / Lot                         | accountId、lotId               | Hold/Debit/Restore/Expire                        |
|   80 | Marketing Budget / Reservation                | campaignId、reservationId      | Reserve/Commit/Release                           |
|   90 | PaymentIntent / Attempt                       | paymentId、attemptId           | Provider Observation与Capture                    |
|  100 | Refund / RefundLeg                            | refundId、legId                | 退款计划、回调和恢复；先Payment再Refund/Leg      |
|  110 | Finance Account                               | accountId                      | 仅Finance Inbox自己的UoW                         |

Journal、Entry、Outbox、Audit是append-only写，不作为反向锁点。Checkout路径是`10→20→30→50→60→70→80→新Order→新Payment→append`；Capture是`10→40→50→60→70→80→90→append`；退款是`10→90→100→append`，各Benefit/Voucher/Provider Leg在提交后独立推进；Finance是`Inbox Claim→110→append Journal/Entry/Outbox`。任何Trace出现Rank下降、同类ID未排序、未声明锁或跨OwnerEvent Consumer共享事务，CI和Runtime Guard同时拒绝。

所有聚合更新使用：

```sql
update owner.aggregate
set state = ..., version = version + 1
where id = :id and version = :expected_version
```

更新 0 行返回 VERSION_CONFLICT，不自动覆盖。

### 15.4 库存

```text
StockItem
  skuId
  locationId
  onhand
  safety
  version

Reservation
  reservationId
  orderId
  skuId
  quantity
  state
  expiresAt
  version

StockMovement
  movementId
  skuId
  kind
  quantity
  reference
  idempotency
  occurredAt
```

Available 等于 Onhand 减 Active Reservation 减 Safety。Reservation 只能 Commit、Release 或 Expire 一次。

### 15.5 订单快照

订单创建时冻结：

- Product、SKU、标题、图片和属性。
- Listing、Mall、Supplier、Store 和履约责任方。
- Price、Tax、Shipping、Promotion、Voucher、Benefit 与 Tender。
- Qualification Decision 与 Rule Versions。
- 收货地址加密信封与安全展示摘要。
- Provider Mapping 与 Source Version。
- 商城 Published Snapshot。

历史页面只读订单快照，不用当前商品覆盖。

### 15.6 财务账本

```text
Journal
  journalId
  type
  businessReference
  currency
  state
  occurredAt
  version

JournalEntry
  entryId
  journalId
  accountId
  side
  amountMinor
  tenantId
  enterpriseId
  mallId
  supplierId
  orderId
```

数据库 Constraint 和 Deferred Trigger 共同断言借贷平衡；应用 Policy 只提供更早的友好错误。

### 15.7 RLS 与数据库角色

- shopapp：ApiMain 的最小权限角色，只执行公开 Repository 所需操作。
- shopjob：JobsMain，拥有有限异步表和 Owner 命令权限。
- shopmigration：仅 CI 发布时使用。
- shopread：受控分析或只读副本。
- Public 和 Anonymous 默认无业务表权限。

SECURITY DEFINER 函数必须固定 search_path、显式 Schema、最小 Grant，并有越权合同测试。

### 15.8 迁移原则

- 164 个现有迁移是历史 Ledger，不删除、不改写。
- 所有修复只追加新的 14 位时间戳 SQL。
- 每个迁移包含目的、前置断言、影响、验证和前向补偿。
- 空库顺序重放、生产 Schema Clone 升级、数据回填和回滚演练均通过后才能发布。
- 应用硬切不等于破坏性 Schema 同步切；先 Additive，稳定后再 Drop 或 Revoke。

---

## 16. 唯一目标代码目录

### 16.1 根目录

最终代码根严格使用权威架构规定的 Shop。当前 Shop/smart-wing 不是被改名为另一棵嵌套目录，而是在工作树清洁、分支冻结、迁移 Manifest 和 Hash 确认后，把获准保留的内容移动到 Shop 对应目录；完成后删除嵌套 .git、旧构建入口与迁移源。除脚本、审计、配置、SQL、测试、冻结品牌资产和协议强制名称外，自有文件与目录不得使用下划线、连字符或空格。

根目录不再存在 admin-web、auth-web、storefront-web、wechat-miniapp、commerce-api、core-read-cache 等旧式重复命名和第二实现。

VI资产的精确Producer只有`tools/tokengen/index.ts`调用`Generator.ts`；`Schema.ts`验证`packages/design/src/tokens.json`、`mobile-platforms.json`和四个冻结SVG，`Generator.test.ts`固定排序、数值单位、跨端映射、颜色/字号/圆角/动效约束及重复生成零Diff。`tokens.json`与`mobile-platforms.json`是Canonical输入，`tokens.css`和应用公开资产是Derived输出。四个Canonical SVG恰好为`brand-mark.svg`、`brand-lockup-horizontal.svg`、`wing-code-symbol.svg`、`wing-pattern.svg`；它们各自确定性复制到`apps/console/public/brand/<same-name>`与`apps/auth/public/brand/<same-name>`，因此Derived目标恰好八个。Generated对每一份Derived资产验证`canonicalPath/canonicalHash/derivedPath/derivedHash/mode`且两Hash必须相等；禁止手改、重命名、额外Logo或应用内第二母版。

迁移时只复用旧脚本已经通过测试的语义：outerShop的`scripts/build-brand-assets.mjs`、`build-web-tokens.mjs`、`check-miniapp-vi.mjs`、`check-vi.mjs`、`check-vi.test.mjs`、`token-convergence.test.mjs`、`vi-rules.mjs`、`vi-source.mjs`、`vi-baseline.json`，以及nestedCandidate的`scripts/build-web-tokens.mjs`、`build-miniapp-theme.mjs`。FileDisposition逐文件记录源Hash，将规则去重迁入上述Generator/Schema/Test与一份Canonical测试基线；只有新Generator逐输出Hash相等、Web与Miniapp Snapshot通过、旧脚本Reference为零后才删除旧脚本，禁止保留第二入口或把两个脚本整段并排复制。

`docs/operations/FileDisposition.yml`的唯一Producer是`tools/quality/src/FileDisposition.ts`中的Canonical Renderer，不存在手写副本。生成顺序固定为：读取六份已签Source Snapshot与版本化裁决输入→排序并验证全部Source/Target记录→渲染不含自身观测Hash的Canonical YAML→原子写临时文件并fsync/rename→计算最终文件Hash→把该Hash仅写入外层`postManifestCandidate`与FileDisposition Report。YAML中它自己的`create`记录使用`contentRule=canonicalSelf`且不得出现`sha256`字段，避免自Hash不动点；其他目标按Schema携sourceHashes或生成规则。Report Decoder证明YAML规范化Hash、外层Manifest Hash与Git中的最终字节全等。运行Evidence JSON不进入Source create清单；只跟踪`docs/operations/evidence/.gitignore`，`quality/**|archive/**|cutover/**`均由Producer创建、签名、清零或归档。

`infrastructure/container/compose.yml`是唯一本地拓扑，恰好声明`postgres`、`redis`、`minio`、`queue`四个服务；`queue`是用于Event至少一次传输的RabbitMQ Quorum Queue，PostgreSQL Outbox/Inbox仍是幂等与经济事实真值。Compose的`image`只能使用`${POSTGRES_IMAGE:?}`、`${REDIS_IMAGE:?}`、`${MINIO_IMAGE:?}`、`${QUEUE_IMAGE:?}`；四个不可变`registry/name@sha256:digest`值只写在`images.lock.yml`。`Database.ts`校验Lock Schema/Digest后把四值与每次新生成的测试凭据写入600权限临时Env File，Compose本身不保存版本、Secret或固定宿主端口。`Generated.ts`校验四个Lock Key与四个Compose变量恰好集合相等，多、少、重复或非Digest引用都失败。

`tests/config/integration.vitest.ts`仅收集`tests/integration/**/*.test.ts`，排除recovery/journey/node_modules/构建输出，使用Node、isolate、`passWithNoTests=false`、30s Test/120s Hook Timeout，并从生成Test Config获取有界Worker数与每Worker独立Schema/Bucket/Vhost。`tests/config/recovery.vitest.ts`仅收集`tests/recovery/**/*.test.ts`，排除integration/journey/node_modules/构建输出，使用Node、isolate、`passWithNoTests=false`、`fileParallelism=false`、`maxWorkers=1`、120s Test/300s Hook Timeout。两者只导入`packages/config/src/generated/TestConfig.ts`和Vitest API，不启动容器、不读业务Secret、不复制Runtime Config。

#### 16.1.1 与权威架构目录的逐项一致性

| 权威项                       | 最终落点                | 裁决                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 代码根 Shop                  | Shop                    | 完全一致；Shop/smart-wing 仅是一次性迁移源                                                                |
| 六客户端                     | Shop/apps               | 完全一致                                                                                                  |
| Commerce 服务                | Shop/services/commerce  | 完全一致                                                                                                  |
| 合同、SDK 与基础包           | Shop/packages           | 完全一致                                                                                                  |
| 渠道扩展                     | Shop/extensions/channel | 完全一致；不再另建 provider 根，Provider 实现在 channel 内按 Manifest 注册                                |
| 容器、云、网络与运维         | Shop/infrastructure     | 完全一致；阿里云和 Cloudflare 是 cloud 内实现，不成为架构层目录                                           |
| 生成器与统一质量入口         | Shop/tools              | 完全一致；本地环境与Seed归入container或database/tools；质量检查只归tools/quality，不保留根scripts第二入口 |
| 架构、决策、运维、需求、安全 | Shop/docs               | 完全一致；Runbook 归 docs/operations                                                                      |
| VI 包目录                    | Shop/packages/design    | 服从权威架构目录；冻结SVG文件名和内容不变，VI文档中的旧包路径在同一版本化提交中原子更新                   |

`tools/quality`内部固定为无依赖安装/启动器`bootstrap.mjs`、唯一编排器`src/Quality.ts`、唯一DAG`src/TaskCatalog.ts`、上列20个精确命名的任务Runner、`src/FileDisposition.ts`全文件总账、`src/audit/*`语义审计和版本化配置；`npm run quality`只调用Bootstrap，Bootstrap只能安装锁定依赖并启动Quality，不能编排或跳过Task。FileDisposition必须为20个Runner各生成一条无Source、单Target的`create`，禁止以`task/*.ts`或空目录代替。旧根`scripts/**`的有效语义迁入后整根删除。

本方案已把最终路径裁决为 packages/design：迁移时必须以当前冻结资产Hash为输入，在同一变更集中更新包名、导入、Generator、VI文档与派生制品，再删除旧包。任何实际差异进入 docs/decisions，包含旧路径、新路径、理由、Owner、迁移时间、迁移Hash、删除证据和两份权威文档的新Hash；长期存在两套目标树一律禁止。

```ts
export const ProductWorkspace = defineWorkspace({
  id: 'product',
  title: '商品管理',
  path: '/product',
  icon: ProductIcon,
  scopes: ['platform', 'distributor', 'enterprise', 'mall'],
  permission: 'catalog.listing.read',
  capability: 'catalog.listings.read',
  load: () => import('./route/WorkspaceRoute'),
});
```

Registry 只保存元数据和 Lazy Loader，不保存业务对象或全局 Scope。

`foundation`只含稳定端口、Pipeline和跨模块策略；`adapter`只含这些端口的技术实现，唯一绑定点是`bootstrap/Container.ts`。不得再创建`foundation/infrastructure`、`foundation/http`、`foundation/cache`、`foundation/jobs`、服务根`worker.ts`、CommandBus、QueryBus或Message型ErrorMapper。HTTP入口只有`entry/ApiMain.ts`，任务入口只有`entry/JobsMain.ts`；缓存合同固定为`foundation/performance/QueryCache.ts`，Redis实现固定为`adapter/cache/RedisCache.ts`。

`foundation/messaging/MessageBroker.ts`只暴露带Deadline/Abort、Envelope、当次Publisher Confirm Result、Delivery/Ack/Nack和Health的窄合同，不泄漏RabbitMQ Channel/Connection，也不宣称Broker能按messageId回查持久Receipt。`adapter/messaging/RabbitMessageBroker.ts`是唯一AMQP实现，从Event Catalog幂等声明Exchange、Quorum Queue、Dead-letter Route和Consumer Prefetch，使用Publisher Confirm；它不做业务去重、不自己更新Owner状态。`OutboxRelay.ts`只在当次Confirm Ack后标记Published；若publish后、confirm前断线，Outbox保持Pending，按相同`sourceEventId/eventId/aggregateVersion/payloadHash`安全重发，由每个Owner的Inbox Receipt吸收重复。JobsMain的Consumer仍经Inbox Executor。`bootstrap/Container.ts`按生成Runtime Config绑定该Adapter，`tests/integration/messaging/MessageBroker.test.ts`对真`queue`服务运行共享Contract Suite，覆盖Confirm、Confirm Unknown同ID重发不重复业务效果、Redelivery、Order、Prefetch、Reconnect、Deadletter和Graceful Shutdown。Commerce package只新增一个被lockfile精确锁定的AMQP Client生产依赖，禁止第二Broker SDK。

模块骨架，以 Order 为例：

```text
modules/order
├─ public
```

`OrderRoutes.ts`只组合真实HTTP Operation的逐Operation Controller，不包含业务判断；`CreateOrder`只能由Checkout通过E19 `CreateOrderPort`进入，`CheckPaymentCapture`只能由Payment通过operation-specific Port进入，两者都禁止再建HTTP Controller形成第二`entryKind`。Job Catalog直接且仅注册`application/job/OrderExpiryJobHandler.ts`与`application/job/AfterSaleDeadlineJobHandler.ts`，OrderModule在同一注册函数中暴露两份Job Definition/Handler Token并验证唯一jobId、唯一Handler与独立幂等键。`OrderCommand.ts`、`OrderView.ts`、`OrderEvent.ts`、聚合`OrderController.ts`和`interface/job/OrderExpiryJob.ts`均不是目标文件。OrderModule.ts只公开Manifest声明的operation-specific public Port、Port Token和注册函数，不导出PgOrderRepository实例。

### 16.4 Packages

| Package   | 唯一责任                                            | 禁止            |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------- |
| contract  | Operation、Event、Error、Requirement 合同源和生成物 | 运行时 IO       |
| sdk       | Transport、Cookie/Bearer、CSRF、Retry、Typed Client | 业务规则        |
| kernel    | Money、Clock、Result、ID、基础事件类型              | 业务模块依赖    |
| authz     | Permission Catalog、Scope 纯规则                    | 数据库          |
| design    | Token、Brand、基础 UI、无业务状态组件               | 页面和 API      |
| telemetry | Log、Metric、Trace 接口                             | PII 和业务决策  |
| config    | 环境 Schema 与启动校验                              | 默认生产 Secret |
| testing   | Fixture Builder、Fake Clock、Contract Harness       | 生产 Import     |

### 16.5 Extensions

```text
extensions/channel
├─ core
├─ jdproduct
├─ jdfresh
├─ tmallmarket
├─ private
├─ cake
├─ flower
├─ book
├─ directcharge
├─ foodvoucher
├─ movie
└─ meal
```

每个 Provider：

```text
Manifest.ts
Factory.ts
Config.ts
Mapper.ts
Client.ts
Health.ts
capability
```

Vendor 特有实现应合并进相应 Provider 的 integration 子目录或由 Provider Factory 选择，避免 Provider 与 Vendor 两套重复插件层。

### 16.6 命名规则

| 对象            | 规则                       | 示例                                         |
| --------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| 自有目录        | 小写单词，无连接符         | feature、control、reconciliation             |
| TypeScript 文件 | PascalCase，无连接符       | OrderRepository.ts                           |
| React 组件      | PascalCase                 | ProductDrawer                                |
| 函数和变量      | camelCase                  | readOrderDetail                              |
| 常量            | UPPERCASE                  | MAX_BATCH_SIZE                               |
| Operation       | domain.resource.action     | order.orders.read                            |
| Event           | domain[.entity].pasttense  | payment.captured、verification.code.verified |
| 数据库          | snake_case，SQL 例外       | payment_intent                               |
| 迁移            | 时间戳与下划线，SQL 例外   | 20260825120000_add_event_envelope.sql        |
| 测试            | 可带点和连接标识，测试例外 | PurchaseProcess.integration.test.ts          |
| 配置            | 工具强制格式例外           | docker-compose.yml                           |

Event的`entity`段只有在同一Domain内需要区分事实主体时才出现；Domain本身已能唯一表达主体时省略，因此`payment.captured`与`verification.code.verified`都合法且由Event Catalog唯一裁决。不得出现 New、Old、Final、V2、Copy、Temp、Helper、Utils、Common、Misc 等含糊自有命名。版本属于合同或迁移元数据，不写进业务文件名。
