## 30. Definition of Done

### 30.1 架构

- [ ] 唯一 Canonical Tree 为 Shop；Shop/smart-wing 迁移源和嵌套 .git 已删除。
- [ ] 六客户端、ApiMain、JobsMain 边界清楚。
- [ ] 28 个业务模块唯一数据 Owner。
- [ ] 28 模块全数据流登记由 Manifest 生成，入口、Port、事件、Job、缓存、补偿和测试无空字段。
- [ ] DomainDependencyGraph 的全部生成边逐项绑定 Port/Event、图号、Contract Test 与 Trace；WorkspaceReadGraph 独立，运行时额外边为零。
- [ ] 跨 Schema 读写为零，批准例外有合同。
- [ ] Module Import Graph 无环。
- [ ] Module 根不导出 Infrastructure。
- [ ] 一个 Operation 一个 Typed Handler。
- [ ] 当前全部73张Mermaid图通过Parse与无头Render，并有签名Diagram Manifest；源码集合与Manifest双向全等且图数变化已同步。

### 30.2 合同

- [ ] 权威 260821 工作簿重新生成的 296 Requirement 与 21 MVP 标签通过；活动生成链、Canonical生成物、构建输入与发布制品中的旧文件名和旧Hash引用为零；历史审计证据按Typed Disposition保留且不进入零命中扫描。
- [ ] 全部 Operation 有 Input、Output、Error、Scope、Permission、IfMatch、Idempotency。
- [ ] 全部 Event 有 Typed Payload 和 Envelope v2。
- [ ] 全部 Job 有 Typed Payload。
- [ ] OpenAPI、Server、SDK、Consumer 无漂移。
- [ ] Public unknown 为零。

### 30.3 数据与交易

- [ ] PostgreSQL 唯一真相。
- [ ] 下单原子事务和固定锁序。
- [ ] Oversell、重复支付、重复核销和不平衡账为零。
- [ ] Refund 不超 Payment。
- [ ] Snapshot 不漂移。
- [ ] Outbox、Audit 与领域写同事务。
- [ ] Inbox 业务效果与 Complete 同事务。

### 30.4 财务

- [ ] 六个 Finance P0/P1 修复。
- [ ] 差异处理产生权威匹配或平衡调整。
- [ ] Settlement Append-only。
- [ ] Withdrawal 恢复重验额度。
- [ ] Maker 与 Checker 为不同 Principal。
- [ ] 关账差异不为零必定拒绝。

### 30.5 Console 与 VI

- [ ] 五主工作台职责无重叠。
- [ ] 专业域不被硬塞入主工作台。
- [ ] 唯一 Shell、Header、Sidebar、ScopeBar。
- [ ] 动态标题 10 用例通过。
- [ ] Header 无重复模块标题和假数字。
- [ ] 字号、字重、色彩、间距、圆角全部用 Token。
- [ ] WCAG AA、键盘、读屏、200% 缩放、Reduced Motion。
- [ ] 首包和 Chunk Budget 通过。
- [ ] 所有状态和错误齐全。

### 30.6 安全

- [ ] 工作簿凭据轮换并完成扫描。
- [ ] 无验证码绕过、万能码和 Demo Session。
- [ ] CORS、CSRF、CSP、Cookie Target、Webhook Replay 通过。
- [ ] Secret 只由 SecretRef 访问。
- [ ] PII 加密、脱敏和审计。
- [ ] RLS 跨租户测试通过。
- [ ] SBOM、签名、漏洞门通过。

### 30.7 性能与恢复

- [ ] SLO 压测通过。
- [ ] Redis 故障不破坏交易正确性。
- [ ] Provider 熔断与恢复通过。
- [ ] Worker Crash 与 Lease 接管通过。
- [ ] Projection 可从 Event 重建。
- [ ] PITR Restore 达到 RPO 与 RTO。
- [ ] 完整 Runbook 演练。

### 30.8 发布与删除

- [ ] 本地和 CI 均只调用 npm run quality，签名 QualityRun 可重放。
- [ ] 所有手写可维护生产源码文件少于 300 行；历史 Migration 与生成物例外可机械识别，SQL、测试、审计、配置、脚本与文档的独立预算全部通过。
- [ ] Production Release Manifest 完整。
- [ ] 金丝雀 1、10、50、100 通过。
- [ ] 完整账期财务验证。
- [ ] t 流量和 DNS 为零并删除。
- [ ] 旧代码、路由、Role、Function、Mock、Fallback、双写为零。
- [ ] 历史 Migration 和 Audit 保留。

### 30.9 用户硬约束合规矩阵

| 用户硬约束                    | 本方案落点                                  | 完成证据                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 系统架构图                    | 第 3、4、6、14、18、21、25、33 节           | 71图逐张Parse/Render、Diagram Manifest、架构决策与运行实证                                            |

## 32. 最终架构裁决

这套系统不需要推倒重写，也不能继续修补表面。正确做法是：

> **保留已经存在的模块化单体双进程、Operation Catalog、安全管线、UnitOfWork、Outbox、Inbox、Jobs、Provider、RLS、Reporting 和 VI Token；把当前目录化但边界仍虚假的实现，收紧为“唯一数据 Owner、跨域只走 Port 或 Event、每个 Operation 一个 Typed Handler、事务幂等审计 Outbox 一条管线”的真正 DDD 模块化单体。**

前端同样遵循这个裁决：

> **保留旧 Smart 最有价值的视觉和交互，但不保留旧数据层；Console 只剩一套 Shell、一套 Manifest、一套 VI、一套 Typed SDK 和一个正式域名。**

完成后的系统不是“t 加 smart”，而是唯一的智慧翼。

---

## 33. 逐文件执行卷与代码修改总索引

本节把总方案正式落到硬切后的唯一配套执行卷[CodePlan](../Shop/docs/operations/CodePlan.md)；当前审阅迁移源暂位于`Shop/smart-wing/docs/福利商城代码修改清单.md`，FileDisposition会原子移动而非复制，最终链接不指向必删迁移源。两卷不是两套架构：

| 卷           | 唯一职责                                                                                     | 禁止                                 |
| ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| 本总方案     | 业务口径、系统架构、VI、12条跨模块时序、28模块内部时序、数据所有权、SLO、迁移和DoD           | 复制第二套逐文件状态，造成路径漂移   |
| 逐文件执行卷 | 当前路径→动作→目标路径→调用点→测试→删除门；28模块和六客户端的精确施工矩阵                    | 改写总方案的架构、模块数、目标树或VI |
| 机器生成物   | Requirement、Operation、Event、Job、Permission、FileDisposition、Ownership与Evidence Catalog | 人工修改生成结果、用数量替代语义核对 |

本轮不以法律意见、外部审批流程或组织签字作为方案编写前提；但260821工作簿与系统正确性要求的身份、Scope、租户隔离、操作授权、审计和资金控制仍是产品技术功能。删除它们会同时违反功能清单、DDD所有权和交易正确性，因此不属于“抛弃辅助项”。

### 33.2 单一生成与调用真值架构

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    Excel[260821 Workbook\n296 Requirements 21 MVP 20 Providers]
    ReqGen[Requirement Generator]
    Req[Requirement Catalog]
    Contract[Contract Sources\nOperation Event Error Job Permission]
    ContractGen[Contract Generator]
    Server[Typed Controller and Handler Interfaces]
    SDK[Typed SDK]
    Clients[Six Client Manifests and Calls]
    Events[Event Consumers and Jobs]
    DB[Ownership and Database Contracts]
    Tests[Contract Integration Journey Performance Tests]
    Evidence[Signed Release Evidence]

    Excel --> ReqGen --> Req
    Req --> Contract
    Contract --> ContractGen
    ContractGen --> Server
    ContractGen --> SDK
    ContractGen --> Events
    ContractGen --> DB
    SDK --> Clients
    Server --> Tests
    Clients --> Tests
    Events --> Tests
    DB --> Tests
    Req --> Tests
    Tests --> Evidence
```

生成方向只有从左到右。前端DTO、后端Parser、Error Union、Event Payload、Job Payload、Permission元数据和SDK方法不得各自再声明一份。Generator输出记录Source Path、Source Hash、Generator Version、Generated At、Output Hash；Clean Clone重生后必须零Diff。

### 33.3 每个 Operation 的完整连续性

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant R as Requirement Catalog
    participant C as Contract Generator
    participant UI as Feature UI
    participant FC as Feature Controller
    participant FA as Feature API
    participant SC as Scoped Client
    participant SDK as Typed SDK
    participant HTTP as Typed Controller
    participant P as Operation Pipeline
    participant H as Dedicated Handler
    participant D as Aggregate or Policy
    participant Repo as Owner Repository
    participant U as UnitOfWork
    participant A as Audit and Outbox
    participant T as Tests and Evidence

    R->>C: Requirement ID and acceptance
    C-->>SDK: Generated operation method request response and error
    C-->>HTTP: Generated route parser response and error binding
    UI->>FC: User intent
    FC->>FA: Query or command and local state context
    FA->>SC: Operation input query key and explicit scope
    SC->>SDK: Operation method abort version and idempotency
    SDK->>HTTP: method path headers typed body
    HTTP->>P: parsed command or query and context
    P->>P: identity access risk deadline and idempotency
    P->>H: typed input and transaction context
    H->>D: enforce state and invariant
    H->>Repo: read or write owner schema only
    Repo->>U: parameterized persistence
    H->>A: immutable audit and typed event in same write unit
    U-->>P: commit or rollback
    P-->>HTTP: typed result or discriminated error
    HTTP-->>SDK: response envelope and requestId
    SDK-->>SC: typed DTO or stable error
    SC-->>FA: scoped result
    FA-->>FC: view DTO and state transition
    FC-->>UI: render state
    UI-->>T: route component and journey evidence
    A-->>T: database audit event and replay evidence
```

`packages/contract/src/generated/OperationContinuity.ts`为每个旧Operation ID保存且只保存一种处置：`kept`、`moved`、`replaced`或`deletedWithRequirementProof`。每个新Operation反向必须能到Requirement、Controller、Handler、Owner、SDK、适用客户端或系统消费者、Test、Metric和Evidence。以下任一项出现即阻断：

同理，`packages/contract/src/generated/EventContinuity.ts`为当前58个Event ID逐一保存`kept|moved|replaced|deletedWithRequirementProof`，并给每个新增Event记录Publisher、Schema、Consumer、Inbox Idempotency、Replay、Metric和Evidence。目标语义统一使用`payment.captured`/`PaymentCaptured`；当前冻结ID`payment.succeeded`必须以`replaced`硬切，不能仅改可读类名，也不能在运行期同时消费两个ID。逐调用点和Pending Inbox/Outbox迁移见执行卷7.29.3。

- 公共输入、输出、Payload或SDK调用为`unknown`。
- 两个Controller、两个Owner、两个SDK方法或一个无Handler Operation。
- Handler存在但未注册，Route存在但无Page，页面存在但无Manifest。
- Event无Schema/Publisher，Consumer无Inbox/幂等，Job无Payload/Lease/Deadletter。
- DB对象未登记Owner，Repository跨Owner Schema写入。
- 旧Operation、Route、RPC、Package Export或Monitor仍有调用者。

### 33.4 全文件处置闭环

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Freeze[Freeze base commit and workspace]
    Snapshot[Snapshot tracked modified deleted and untracked]
    Scan[Scan every source file and artifact in snapshot]
    Hash[Create content hash and reference graph]
    Classify{Exactly one disposition}
    Keep[Keep]
    Move[Move]
    Refactor[Refactor]
    Rewrite[Rewrite]
    Create[Create target only]
    Delete[Delete candidate]
    DeleteReason{Replacement or no replacement reason}
    Target[Unique target path]
    Verify[Build type contract database journey recovery]
    Runtime[Runtime traffic and reference zero]
    Archive[Content addressed archive]
    ReferenceZero[Static and generated reference zero]
    TrafficZero[Runtime traffic zero]
    DeleteVerify[Predelete build type contract database journey and recovery]
    Remove[Recoverable remove]
    PostDeleteVerify[Postdelete clean build deploy journey and restore]
    Final[Regenerate canonical Shop baseline]

    Freeze --> Snapshot --> Scan --> Hash --> Classify
    Classify --> Keep --> Target
    Classify --> Move --> Target
    Classify --> Refactor --> Target
    Classify --> Rewrite --> Target
    Classify --> Create --> Target
    Classify --> Delete --> DeleteReason --> Archive --> ReferenceZero --> TrafficZero --> DeleteVerify --> Remove --> PostDeleteVerify --> Final
    Target --> Verify --> Runtime
    Runtime --> Final
```

`docs/operations/FileDisposition.yml`是全部Source Root当前文件的机器总账。每个Root固定`{rootId,path,baseCommitOrNull,sourceSnapshotHash,statusManifestHash,parentRootIdOrNull,parentDelegationHashOrNull,delegatedRoots[]}`；父Root的`delegatedRoots`按child rootId排序且每项必须是`{rootId,snapshotHash,delegationHash}`，子Root反向保存完全相等的`parentRootId/parentDelegationHash`；每个Source再保存Source Root ID、相对路径、Hash、模式/链接、Owner、Action、Requirement、Operation/Event/Job、`replacement XOR noReplacementReason`与Delete Gate。Target只按Action条件出现。拆分/合并用带Root ID的`derivedTargets`与`sourceHashes`双向闭合，Delete的Archive Location必须是仓库外内容寻址位置。缺父向或子向任一字段、Hash不对称、同child重复、委托后父Root仍扫描child Entry均失败。SourceRoot的`path`以已验证的Shop Repository Root为唯一解析基准：`outerShop=.`、`nestedCandidate=smart-wing`、`branchReplica=smart-wing-branch-work`、`shopReplica=../Shop1`、`legacySmart=../smart-wing`、`legacySmartCopy=../smart-wing 2`；禁止再加`Shop/`前缀。`tools/quality/src/FileDisposition.ts`强制：

1. 每个Source Root的每个源文件恰好一个动作，唯一Source Key为`rootId + relativePath + sha256`；任何未分类文件、未登记Root、父子Root覆盖空洞或重复计数均失败。
2. `keep/move/refactor/rewrite`恰有一个Canonical Target；`delete`禁止Target、必须有Archive，并且`replacement`与`noReplacementReason`二选一；`create`禁止Source且恰有一个Target。每个目标最多一个Create记录；合并列全部Source Hash，拆分让Source的Derived Targets与Target的Source Hash双向一致。
3. Move/Refactor/Rewrite同步改Package、Import、Dynamic Import、Registry、Route、Menu、CI、Container、Deploy、Monitor和Docs；相同相对路径内容不同必须保存Base/Outer/Nested/Replica Hash、三方裁决、合并结果与测试，禁止Last Write Wins。
4. 所有Delete都必须有静态引用为0、运行流量为0、内容寻址归档、可恢复位置与Recovery Drill；删除前必须通过Build、Type、Contract、Database、Journey与Recovery，删除后必须在Clean Clone再次通过Build、Deploy、Journey与Restore。只有声明`replacement`的分支要求Replacement Evidence，声明`noReplacementReason`的分支要求机器可审计的删除理由和Requirement/Runtime无依赖证明。
5. node_modules、dist、.next、.vinext、.wrangler、Coverage等不进入源码总账，但必须由Ignore/Artifact Audit证明不入Git和生产镜像。
6. 全部Root收束到Shop后重生Canonical Baseline；旧Root总账冻结为Evidence，不继续作为活动配置。每个旧Root只有在自身全部Entry通过删除门、独有内容为零且另一个Root不能代替授权时才可删除。
7. 启动时先对六个Root执行`realpath`，拒绝不存在、循环链接、Root重叠未Delegation、解析后越出allowlist或realpath与Snapshot证据不符。三个`../`同级Root仅获得只读`snapshot`能力；`move/delete/archive`必须在后续受控Cutover中以`rootId + sourceSnapshotHash + exact realpath`单独授权，扫描allowlist不传播给Generator、Build、Test、Runtime、Deploy或任何删除器。

Source Entry不是假设文件总在磁盘上的单一结构，而是封闭Union：`present={path,sha256,size,mode,linkTarget,rawBlobRef}`或`deletedTombstone={path,baseCommitOid,baseTreeOid,baseBlobOid,baseBlobHash,size,mode,rawBlobRef,absentProof}`。Tombstone必须证明Live path缺失，再从冻结Git Object或已发布SourceSnapshot raw blob完整Get/复算原字节；Archive Restore能在隔离destination重建它，DeleteExecutor只写`alreadyAbsent` WAL而不rename。因当前存在725个Deleted状态，缺该分支即整次盘点失败。

FileDisposition的业务`action`与源侧物理退休是两层。生成器必须另产`retiredSourceSet`：包含`delete`以及Target/derivedTargets已完整Get、Hash/测试闭合的`move|refactor|rewrite`源侧，`keep`不得进入；每项绑定action、SourceSnapshot Entry、Target Evidence与replacement/noReplacementReason规则。Archive/Apply/Quarantine/Finalize统一消费retiredSourceSet，不能只处理action=delete，也不能因Target已存在就整目录删除旧Root。当前Canonical targetShop由Root Activation Manifest穷尽其**全部**旧物理Entry（含keep的旧副本），其余legacy/replica Root用retiredSourceSet退休；两者合并后才可证明唯一Shop。

Git内部事实与被忽略磁盘项各有独立Manifest，但唯一Producer同样是删除前的SnapshotCli。`cutover/GitMetadata.ts`由SnapshotCli调用，对每个Git Root解析gitdir/commonDir/worktree关系，冻结HEAD、全部refs、object connectivity、config与逐metadata Entry，生成`git bundle --all`、Ref Manifest和内容寻址raw blobs；两次Restore Drill都必须`git bundle verify`、重建refs、读取所有独有Commit。`.git`文件型worktree指针只退休指针，仍被其他worktree使用的shared commonDir禁止删除。`cutover/EphemeralArtifact.ts`同样由SnapshotCli调用，让Ignore/Artifact Audit逐项lstat/realpath生成`EphemeralArtifactManifest`，字段固定path/type/hash或size/mode/policyReason/reproducible/parentDirs；node_modules、dist、.next、.vinext、.wrangler、Coverage只有被证明可重建才可进入，疑似唯一状态必须升级为Source或Git Metadata。两Manifest及Git Bundle作为raw内容地址由SourceSnapshotSet Core签名绑定，按path排序、拒绝link越Root；Archive/Prepared/apply只消费这同一捕获结果，不得稍后重扫Live磁盘。Apply逐项移入Quarantine，Restore逐项放回，Finalize逐项清理。它们不计业务Requirement，却必须计入Physical Root Zero，禁止最后`rm -rf oldRoot`绕过。

#### 33.4.1 Delete Archive的唯一可恢复合同

`tools/quality/config/archive.yml`是Archive Store唯一配置，只声明`storeId=source`、S3兼容`providerRef`、endpoint/bucket/prefix Reference、`credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl`、非Secret `kmsKeyRef`、最小保留策略和单对象上限，不保存短时credentialRef、端点凭据或密钥值；运行时能力唯一来自Archive Authorization的动作独立archiveStoreCredentialGrantBinding。URI语法固定为`archive://<storeId>/sha256/<64-lowercase-hex>`，解析后唯一Blob Key为`<prefix>/sha256/<hex[0:2]>/<hex[2:4]>/<hex>`；未知scheme/storeId/algorithm、非64位小写Hash或URI Hash与Entry Hash不等都失败。FileDisposition源Entry只写`archive: {storeId: source, keyFrom: sha256}`，生成器以该Entry真实`sha256`编译出完整URI，不持久`content://<generated hash>`或任何占位符。

`tools/quality/config/cutover.yml`是仓库内只读信任策略，只允许版本化的`signerId + algorithm=Ed25519 + immutable KMS/publicVerifyKeyRef + keyVersion + validFrom/validTo + purpose`，固定外部Authorization Receipt/Evidence Store Ref、只读`authorizationBootstrapReaderCredentialRef + authorizationPrefix + expiry`及独立的`checkoutClaimBootstrapReaderCredentialRef + checkoutClaimPrefix + expiry`，并固定允许的Platform Release Issuer、Executor Bundle Signer、Attestor、Node/npm Runtime Policy与Entrypoint集合；不保存私钥。前者只能Get四类Authorization Envelope，后者只能Get CheckoutClaim Envelope，两者都不能Put/List/Delete且不能互换，分别解决“必须先读授权/Claim才能知道如何读自身”的Bootstrap环；Platform Release Issuer项只允许直接验`purpose=ordinary-release-claim`的PlatformReleaseInvocation，不能签Evidence Envelope或业务Authorization。`authorization/TrustPolicy.ts`规范化配置并计算`trustPolicyHash`，Authority/Snapshot/Archive/Cutover四类授权与PlatformReleaseInvocation都必须签入该Hash和各自Attested执行身份。

只有**可执行业务CLI的Authorization**必须共同签入`authorizationReceiptStoreRef/authorizationReceiptCredentialGrantBinding`；CLI Core侧签入相互独立的`evidenceStoreRef/corePublisherCredentialGrantBinding/coreAllowedKinds/corePrefix`、`coreReaderCredentialGrantBinding/coreReaderKinds/coreReaderSubjects`与`coreSignerCredentialGrantBinding`；Launcher侧签入`runReceiptPublisherCredentialGrantBinding`、精确`coreReaderCredentialGrantBinding`、只允许当前subject的`runReceiptRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding`与`launcherSignerCredentialGrantBinding`。所有Binding中的Scope/TTL/Generation由Canonical类型承载，不得再在旁路字段重复保存。Launcher发布ExecutorRunReceipt后必须用该exact-subject Reader完整回读，才可生成SessionEvidenceAssertion；Reader无List/Put。`resource-claim`、`executor-isolation`、`takeover-claim`、`credential-refresh`、`control-recovery`与`session-state-read`是Decoder明确的六类最小控制面例外：resource-claim按operation只可执行一次reserve或expireReservation，其余分别只可隔离旧Executor、reserveTakeover、refreshAuthorization、恢复一个已执行动作的Receipt，或读取exact run最小Projection及同action+token的find。control-recovery绑定已过期原Authorization Ref+Hash/action/token/provider verify/schema与新find-only Grant，只能返回原Provider Receipt或签名Absent，绝不能重做Mutation。六类都绑定Attested ToolLauncher，不启动业务CLI，也不带AuthorizationReceipt、Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader/Signer或业务文件/流量/DB权限；Session/Isolation/Grant Provider以Authorization Hash/nonce/稳定Token防重放。CLI只能写自己的Core并读本相位精确输入；Launcher只能Get当前唯一Core subject、写/回读本次Receipt并签Receipt。Ref不含Secret；任一ambient credential、读写合一、Object Store Credential兼任KMS签名、或scope扩大都失败。任何授权文件自带Key、未允许Signer/Algorithm、吊销或过期Key、Verify Ref版本漂移、执行器、Publisher/Reader或Signer字段缺失都拒绝。`AuthorizationReceipt.ts`与唯一`ObjectAuthorizationReceipt.ts`对可执行业务授权使用外部对象存储与短时Grant条件Put消费`purpose/nonce/authorizationHash`；相同Authorization Hash仅可在Journal未完成且同一Session Lease仍归本执行器时恢复续跑，其他重放或已完成阶段再次执行都失败。

`AuthorizationReceipt.ts`的Port固定为`consume(request,stableToken)/findConsumptionReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；Request固定`{authorizationRef+hash,purpose,nonce,authorizationReceiptCredentialGrantBinding,attestedLauncherHash,expectedSessionOwnershipHashOrReservationHash,canonicalParamsHash}`，Token固定`sha256("authorization-consumption-v1"||authorizationHash||purpose||nonce||attestedLauncherHash)`。唯一Provider以(purpose,nonce,authorizationHash)条件创建并在独立线性事务日志保存原签名Consumption Receipt；回包丢失由control-recovery的`authorizationReceipt consume→findConsumptionReceipt`取回，SignedAbsent才可同Token重试。消费发生在业务CLI获得Session Journal写权之前，因此不写事前WAL storeAction；下一条phaseOpened和receiptConsumed只镜像已验Provider Receipt、Binding Hash与Provider日志位置，不能伪造事后Intent。

同一`cutover.yml`还必须版本化七份非Secret策略；Policy永远不固定某一次短时Credential Ref，只固定`credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl`，具体能力必须由每份Authorization以完整`CredentialGrantBinding`签入并接受模板校验。`telemetryPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/queryCatalogHash/requiredSeries[]/requiredLabels[]/zeroThreshold=0/observationWindow/minFreshness/maxGap/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion`；每个Series以不可变queryId覆盖旧Route、Event、Job、Consumer Lag、Bundle/Deployment与错误流量，禁止通配查询、可选Series或空结果视为0。`trafficControlPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/gatewayRefs[]/schedulerRefs[]/consumerRefs[]/fenceMode=deny-old-and-pause-old/drainTimeout/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/fenceTokenTemplate/openTokenTemplate/fenceNewTokenTemplate`。`deploymentPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/canaryTargetRef/productionTargetRef/configTargetRefs[]/artifactStoreRef/allowedRuntimeEntrypoints=[ApiMain,JobsMain]/allowedStaticComponents=[Console,Storefront,Auth,Store,Supplier,Miniapp]/migrationEntrypoint=MigrationMain/probeEntrypoint=SmokeMain/deployReceiptSchemaVersion/providerVerifyRef/healthUrlPolicy/probeCatalogHash/idempotencyTemplate/timeout/rollbackPolicy`，Verify只能使用由`cutoverRunId+canaryTargetRef+policyHash`确定性派生、create-if-absent且不可跨run复用的Canary DB/Namespace并令`migrationMode=isolated`，生产dark deploy必须`migrationMode=none`。`migrationPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/productionDatabaseRef/canaryNamespaceTemplateHash/canaryAllowedActions=[isolatedMigrate,destroy]/isolatedMigrationTokenTemplate/destroyCanaryTokenTemplate/canaryZeroPredicateHash/snapshotPolicyRef/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/migrationPlanHash/expectedOldSchemaHash/expectedNewSchemaHash/reconcileCatalogHash/repairPolicyHash/timeout`。`canonicalRefPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/remoteRef/allowedRef/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/timeout`。`cutoverSessionPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/storeRef/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/sessionAssertionVerifyRef/sessionAssertionSchemaVersion/sessionAssertionAllowedLauncherPurposes[]/rootSwapAttestationVerifyRef/rootSwapAttestationSchemaVersion/rootSwapCoreSignerPurpose=session-forward-commit/leaseDuration/renewBefore/credentialRefreshBefore/executorIsolationProviderRef/executorIsolationCredentialIssuerRef/executorIsolationCredentialIssuerVerifyRef/executorIsolationCredentialGrantSchemaVersion/executorIsolationScopeTemplateHash/executorIsolationMaxCredentialTtl/executorIsolationVerifyRef/executorIsolationReceiptSchemaVersion/executorIsolationDeadline/resourceLeaseNamespace`，要求按cutoverRunId线性CAS并对跨run资源集合提供单调Fencing Epoch。`mountPolicy`固定`providerRef/credentialIssuerRef/credentialIssuerVerifyRef/credentialGrantSchemaVersion/credentialScopeTemplateHash/maxCredentialTtl/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/options=[readonly,nodev,nosuid,noexec]/sandboxIdentityTemplateHash/consumerEntrypointAllowlistHash/mountTokenTemplate/unmountTokenTemplate/timeout/resourceLeaseNamespace`。每份Apply/Verify/Restore/Activate/Finalize/Forward/Repair/Isolation授权都签入所需Policy Hash，并为每个Provider动作签入互不复用的具体`CredentialGrantBinding`、Verify Ref、Receipt Schema、精确Target/Token与Deadline；Decoder证明Issuer、Scope和TTL符合不可变Policy模板。Continuation可轮换具体短时Binding，但不得改变Policy Hash、Scope模板、Target、Token或动作集合。所有Provider Receipt必须以对应Verify Ref和Schema验签并逐字节回写credentialBindingHash。

`CredentialGrantBinding`是所有Authorization、Request、WAL Attempt Context与Receipt复用的唯一Canonical类型，固定`{credentialGrantRef,credentialGrantHash,scope,expiresAt,fencingEpochOrNull,revocationGenerationOrNull,credentialBindingHash}`；`credentialBindingHash=sha256(JCS(前六字段))`，显式null同样入Hash。`business|sessionStateRead|exactControl|standaloneAuthorized`必须有权威generation，business另必须有epoch；`bootstrapReserve|bootstrapTakeover|bootstrapIsolation`在对应线性CAS产生或确认generation前把epoch/generation按scope显式写null，且三者不得进入GrantSet或调用各自唯一bootstrap动作之外的动作。Trust Policy中用于“先读授权/Claim”的两个静态Bootstrap Reader Ref是唯一不经过Broker的例外，只能读取固定Prefix且不能进入Authorization/Request/WAL/Receipt的Binding字段。任何相位手写不完整别名、裸CredentialRef或在非bootstrap分支缺Hash/Scope/TTL/Generation均由Decoder拒绝。

`execution/CredentialBroker.ts`是所有具体Provider短时能力的唯一签发、恢复与集合状态Port，固定`getOrInitGeneration(request,credentialIssuerInvocation,stableToken)/findGenerationReceipt(brokerRecoveryInvocation)/issue(request,credentialIssuerInvocation,stableToken)/findGrant(brokerRecoveryInvocation)/introspect(credentialGrantBinding)/withResolvedCredential(callBinding,credentialGrantBinding,callback)/sealPendingGrantSet(sealRequest,stableToken)/findGrantSetSeal(brokerRecoveryInvocation)/getPendingGrantSetByBinding(bindingRef)/getGrantSet(grantSetRef,hash)/transitionGrantSets(transitionBinding,transitionPlan,stableToken)/findGrantSetTransitionSet(recoveryInvocation)/isolateGrantSet(isolationBrokerInvocation,stableToken)/findGrantSetIsolation(recoveryInvocation)`。`withResolvedCredential`的callBinding是闭Union：`cutoverBusiness={authorizationRef+hash,provider,action,target,token,currentOwnershipHash,state,workOrNull,epoch,revocationGeneration}`；`standaloneAuthorized={authorizationRef+hash,operationRunId,purpose,provider,action,target,token,attestedLauncherHash,revocationGeneration,generationReceiptHash}`用于Authority/Snapshot/Archive/普通release/Issuer；`exactControl={authorizationRef+hash,context,provider,action,target,token,scopeBindingHash,attestedLauncherHash,revocationGeneration}`，context与Binding.scope中的`cutover|standalone`逐字段全等；`sessionStateRead={authorizationRef+hash,sessionStoreRef,runId,readToken,readerExecutor,revocationGeneration}`；`bootstrapReserve={authorizationRef+hash,sessionStoreRef,action=reserve|findReceiptForReserve,target=resourceSetHash,token,intentHash,subjectExecutor,epochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}`；`bootstrapTakeover={authorizationRef+hash,sessionStoreRef,action=reserveTakeover|findReceiptForReserveTakeover,target=currentOwnershipHash,token,intentHash,subjectExecutor,epochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}`；`bootstrapIsolation={authorizationRef+hash,provider,action=isolate|findIsolationReceipt,target=currentOwnershipHash,token,intentHash,oldExecutor,prospectiveExecutor,epochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}`。其中`findReceiptForReserve|findReceiptForReserveTakeover|findIsolationReceipt`是同Provider同action+token唯一索引的只读恢复名，不进入Mutation action enum。Broker要求callBinding、Binding.scope、Authorization与请求逐字段全等，只在同步callback内给Adapter不可序列化handle；sessionStateRead只能调用read/findStateReadReceipt，三个bootstrap分支各自只能调用其唯一Mutation或同Token find，不能调用任何其他动作。删除逐Set revoke/generation Port；Session/Grant/Generation/Transition Registry仍为同一线性事务边界。

`CredentialGenerationRequest`固定`{payload={issuerPolicyHash,issuerRef,providerRef,subjectExecutor,operationRunId,purpose,attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash,requestedAt,expiresAt},credentialIssuerInvocation,credentialIssuerInvocationHash}`，`requestHash=sha256(JCS(payload))`且Invocation.requestHash必须全等，`stableToken=sha256("credential-generation-v1"||issuerPolicyHash||issuerRef||requestHash||subjectExecutor)`；Registry对subjectExecutor执行create-if-absent或返回单调既有值，签名`CredentialGenerationReceipt`绑定requestHash/token/generation。它是standaloneAuthorized签发前的权威generation Producer；业务Session generation仍只由Session Reserve/Isolation事务产生，不得以此旁路Session。`CredentialGrantIssueRequest`为按scope判别的闭Union，共同字段是`payload={authorizationIntentHash/issuerPolicyHash/issuerRef/providerRef/subjectExecutor/scope/exactActions[]/exactTargets[]/exactTokens[]/issuedAt/expiresAt/attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash}/credentialIssuerInvocation/credentialIssuerInvocationHash`：business必须绑定当前Ownership+active/pending Set意图；standaloneAuthorized必须绑定上述Generation Receipt；bootstrapReserve绑定Resource Claim Authorization；bootstrapTakeover绑定Takeover Authorization；bootstrapIsolation绑定Isolation Authorization、old/prospective executor与grantIsolationHash；sessionStateRead与exactControl绑定各自最小Authorization。`requestHash=sha256(JCS(payload完整判别分支))`且Invocation.requestHash必须全等，`issueToken=sha256("credential-issue-v1"||authorizationIntentHash||issuerRef||requestHash||subjectExecutor)`；禁止调用方自传自由action/target/scope。`CredentialGrantSealRequest`固定`{binding,executorIdentity,authorizationIntentHash,immutableActionSetHash,expectedVersion,expectedSessionHash,expectedExpiry,sortedGrantBindings[]}`，`sealRequestHash=sha256(JCS(request))`且`sealToken=sha256("credential-seal-v1"||authorizationIntentHash||bindingHash||sealRequestHash||executorIdentity)`。Generation、Issue与Seal请求及Token必须先写Broker Intent并fsync，回包丢失时才能逐字节重建bootstrap recovery；同Token不同Hash或同Hash不同Token均冲突。

外部Credential Issuer管理面同样不能依赖ambient Broker身份。每个会出Broker进程的Generation/Issue/Find/Revoke调用必须携Issuer可直接验签的`CredentialIssuerInvocation`，闭Union为`mutation={operation=getOrInitGeneration|issue|revoke,sourceAuthority,issuerPolicyHash,issuerRef,providerRef,subjectExecutor,scopeIntentHash,requestHash,stableToken,attestedBrokerHash,nonce,createdAt,expiresAt,signerId,keyVersion,signature}`或`recovery={operation=findGeneration|findIssuedCredential|findRevocation,sourceAuthority,originalOperation,originalRequestHash,originalToken,brokerIntentHash,attestedBrokerHash,nonce,createdAt,expiresAt,signerId,keyVersion,signature}`。`sourceAuthority`恰为`platformReleaseIntent+hash|authorityCapabilityIntent+hash|snapshotCapabilityIntent+hash|archiveCapabilityIntent+hash|cutoverAuthorizationIntentHash|executorIsolationCapabilityIntent+hash`之一；`AuthorityCapabilityIntent|SnapshotCapabilityIntent|ArchiveCapabilityIntent|ExecutorIsolationCapabilityIntent`与PlatformReleaseIntent相同，都是Trust Policy直接验签、只承诺purpose/scope/action/target/token/subject/template且不含未来Grant或目标Authorization Ref的管理面原语；Broker发Grant后，AuthorizationIssuer才把具体Binding闭合进对应Authorization。Issuer按purpose只接受对应operation/scope/subject，Invocation的Signer/Key必须来自Issuer Policy固定的管理面信任锚。Invocation不携Credential值、不能调用业务Provider，也不能跨subject/token。Broker在首次外调前把完整Invocation Hash写入Broker Intent，Adapter把原字节交给Issuer；Issuer回执必须绑定Invocation Hash、request/token、subject、generation或opaque handle fingerprint和provider signature。find只能由recovery支触发并返回原Receipt/Grant/Revocation或SignedAbsent；revoke计划按Grant成员逐项拥有独立Invocation/Token，禁止一个宽Invocation撤销任意Grant。`CredentialGenerationRequest`、`CredentialGrantIssueRequest`、Isolation revocation plan与所有Broker Receipt都新增`credentialIssuerInvocationHash`并逐项复算；裸authorizationIntentHash、token或mTLS Service Account都不构成调用能力。

`BrokerRecoveryInvocation`是闭Union。`bootstrap={operation=getOrInitGeneration|issue|sealPendingGrantSet|isolateGrantSet,authorizationIntentHashOrGenerationPolicyHash,issuerRef,issuerPolicyHash,bindingRefOrNull,stableToken,expectedRequestHash,attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash,brokerIntentHash,credentialIssuerRecoveryInvocationOrNull}`只用于尚无最终业务Authorization时恢复Generation、Issue、Seal，或以bootstrapIsolation执行后尚未取得prospective generation时恢复同一Isolation事实；会访问外部Issuer的分支必须携上一段recovery Invocation，纯Registry Seal可为null；isolate分支必须额外绑定完整IsolationBrokerInvocation Hash、currentOwnershipHash、old/prospective executor、grantIsolationHash、generationBumpToken和按成员排序的revocation CredentialIssuerInvocation/Token Plan Hash。Broker以已fsync Intent、request Hash、Issuer Invocation Hash和stableToken的唯一索引返回既有Receipt/Grant/Set或签名Absent，不能调用其他find或Mutation。`authorized={recoveryInvocation}`用于Authorization已存在后的find，若find落到外部Issuer同样内含唯一CredentialIssuerInvocation.recovery。findGenerationReceipt/findGrant/findGrantSetSeal按请求阶段接受其中一支并拒绝混用；findGrantSetTransitionSet只接受authorized；findGrantSetIsolation接受bootstrapIsolation recovery或authorized且两者逐字段全等、不得跨支。这样回包丢失时不依赖未来Authorization Ref/generation，也不允许bootstrap恢复扩大到其他业务动作。

Provider原签名、内容寻址`CredentialGrant`的公共字段固定为`schemaVersion/grantId/providerRef/issuerRef/opaqueCredentialRefFingerprint/scope/exactActions[]/exactTargets[]/exactTokens[]/subjectExecutor/issuedAt/expiresAt/revocationHandle/requestHash/stableToken/providerSignature`，其中`scope`是闭Union：`business={cutoverRunId,allowedState,allowedWorkOrNull,fencingEpoch,revocationGeneration}`；`standaloneAuthorized={operationRunId,purpose,attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash,revocationGeneration,generationReceiptHash}`；`bootstrapReserve={cutoverRunId,resourceSetHash,nonce,action=reserve,token}`明确没有尚未产生的epoch/generation；`bootstrapTakeover={cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,isolationReceiptHash,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,prospectiveExecutor,action=reserveTakeover,token}`明确没有新epoch，但必须引用Isolation已经取得的prospective generation事实；`bootstrapIsolation={cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,oldExecutor,prospectiveExecutor,grantIsolationHash,action=isolate,token}`在bump前明确没有可用generation；`sessionStateRead={cutoverRunId,readToken,readerExecutor,revocationGeneration,actions=[read,findStateReadReceipt]}`不预猜state/version/epoch；`exactControl={context,revocationGeneration,binding}`，其中context是`cutover={cutoverRunId,cutoverSessionPolicyHash}`或`standalone={operationRunId,purpose,generationReceiptHash,attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash}`，binding是`attach={action=attach,reservationTicketHash,attachCapabilityHash,pendingGrantSetHash,token}`、`attachTakeover={action=attachTakeover,currentOwnershipHash,takeoverTicketHash,attachCapabilityHash,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,pendingGrantSetHash,normalPhaseHandoffCapabilityHashOrNull,token}`、`phaseHandoff={action=claim|beginIntermediate|beginForward,previousCompletionOwnershipHash,previousCompletionReceiptHash,expectedState,expectedWorkOrNull,nextState,nextWorkOrNull,pendingGrantSetHash,nextTransitionCapabilityHash,token}`、`expireReservation={action=expireReservation,ticketHash,token}`、`expireTakeoverTicket={action=expireTakeoverTicket,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,ticketHash,ticketGeneration,token}`、`refresh={action=refreshAuthorization,currentOwnershipHash,oldSetHash,newSetHash,memberProofHash,token}`、`recovery={originalAuthorizationHash,providerKind,findOperation,originalAction,originalParamsHash,originalToken}`。`normalPhaseHandoffCapabilityHashOrNull`只在Payload同名分支出现并等于上一Completion的`nextTransitionCapabilityHash`，其他attachTakeover分支严格为null。Session动作只能使用cutover context；Authority/Snapshot/Archive/ordinary release/Issuer的sign/store/issue恢复使用standalone context。Isolation Broker原子产生prospectiveExecutorGeneration并写Isolation Receipt，reserveTakeover只验证并搬运该值到Ticket/Ownership；之后才可签business/read/exactControl Grants。Authorization与Grant只携同一scope分支，Decoder逐字段全等并拒绝跨支字段。只有business Grant必须与当前Session state/work/epoch全等；ticketAttach/phaseHandoff exactControl Grant可在pending Set外按各自一次性Capability执行唯一Session动作；任何wildcard或用其他bootstrap/read Grant调用业务Adapter都失败。Grant Ref/Hash不属于EvidenceKind且绝不含Secret值。

上述Canonical `phaseHandoff`分支只在前一`complete|completeIntermediate`已退休active Set后，由Issuer根据最新Completion整包Receipt签发；Grant不进入任何Set，只能一次消费OwnershipReceipt.nextTransitionCapability并在同一CAS激活pending Set。claim边闭表只有正常`Prepared→Applying|Applied→Verifying|Verified→Activating|Activated→Finalizing`和已完成取消`Prepared|Applied|Verified→Restoring`；Running state、Restoring、Finalizing或任意work自环调用claim全部拒绝。beginIntermediate/beginForward只用于`Activating`内前一attempt已经completeIntermediate、当前active Set为空的工作交接。若当前仍有active Set，begin可继续使用owned分支；Handoff不能用于renew/refresh/commit/Forward Ledger/complete或任何业务Provider。重复消费，或action与Port不等、Completion、state/work、Set、Capability、Token任一不等即拒绝。

若完成相位的Executor在`complete`成功后、下一正常相位`claim`前死亡，不把已退休Set重新激活，也不允许新Executor冒用上述`phaseHandoff` Grant。唯一恢复链是`ExecutorIsolation(noActiveSet)→takeover-claim(intendedNextWorkPayload.normalPhaseHandoff)→reserveTakeover→签发下一phase Authorization及pending Set→attachTakeover`。`normalPhaseHandoff`把旧Completion能力作为**受Ticket约束的转换条件**而非新Executor Credential：Store必须同时验证旧Executor已隔离、当前Ownership仍逐字节等于Completion输出、active Set为空、Completion Capability未消费、Ticket prospectiveExecutor/newEpoch/generation与新Authorization全等，并在一个CAS中同时消费Ticket attach Capability和Completion nextTransitionCapability、安装pending Set、执行唯一下一状态转换。旧Executor先完成claim则本CAS因current Ownership/version不等失败；Isolation先成功则旧generation失效，旧Executor不能再claim。不存在“完成后无人有权进入下一相位”的窗口，也不存在Capability转成bearer token的旁路。

ProviderCredentialBroker不假设外部Issuer与本地Vault可分布式原子提交：它先持久化Broker Issue Intent，再调用Issuer的`issue(request.payload,request.credentialIssuerInvocation,stableToken)/findIssuedCredential(credentialIssuerRecoveryInvocation,stableToken)`；无论响应是否丢失都按完整Recovery Invocation+同Token取回同一handle与Issuer Receipt，随后把SDK handle写入KMS封装`CredentialMaterialBinding={grantId,providerRef,encryptedHandle,handleFingerprint,issuedAt,expiresAt,revocationGenerationOrNull,scopeKind}` Vault、fsync Binding/Issuer Receipt并把Hash写入私有索引，最后发布Grant。只有scopeKind=bootstrapReserve|bootstrapTakeover|bootstrapIsolation允许null，Session/Isolation CAS后签发的所有后续Binding必须非空且bootstrap handle不得升级复用。Issuer验签Invocation失败或无法find同Token事实时撤销orphan并停机。findGrant恢复公开Grant与Binding，introspect/withResolvedCredential单次解封并核对fingerprint/expiry/scope/generation；isolateGrantSet/expiry使Binding不可解析。缺Binding即fail closed。

`IsolationBrokerInvocation`完整绑定`executor-isolation Authorization Ref+Hash/isolationBrokerCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapIsolation}/executorIsolationCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapIsolation}/currentOwnershipRef+hash/oldExecutor/oldRevocationGeneration/prospectiveExecutor/grantIsolation/active成员逐项grantRef+hash+issuerRef+revocationHandle或noActiveSet proof/isolationStableToken/isolationProviderToken/attestedLauncherHash`。两动作Token固定且不同：`isolationStableToken=sha256("credential-isolation-broker-v1"||authorizationHash||currentOwnershipHash||grantIsolationHash||oldExecutor||prospectiveExecutor)`，`isolationProviderToken=sha256("executor-isolation-provider-v1"||authorizationHash||currentOwnershipHash||processSetHash||mountSetHash||oldExecutor||prospectiveExecutor)`；二者都进入各自Binding exactTokens、Authorization immutableActionSet、WAL、Recovery与最终Receipt，禁止运行时临时派生。Broker在一个可恢复线性事务中先以`generationBumpToken=sha256("credential-isolation-generation-v1"||authorizationHash||isolationStableToken||oldExecutor||oldGeneration)`把oldExecutor Generation严格加一，再对每个按grantRef排序的active成员用`revocationToken=sha256("credential-isolation-revoke-v1"||authorizationHash||isolationStableToken||grantRef||grantHash||revocationHandle||oldGeneration)`调用Issuer revoke/find，最后对prospectiveExecutor get-or-init generation；任何成员失败保持隔离中且只能同Token续跑。签名`GrantSetIsolationReceipt`绑定完整成员计划、每项Receipt、generationBumpToken、新旧generation、prospectiveRevocationGeneration、GrantTransitionOutcome、paramsHash、isolationStableToken和Broker credentialBindingHash。noActiveSet仍必须bump旧generation并取得prospective generation，但成员计划为空且携权威Absent Proof；不存在ambient Broker身份或逐成员自由Token。

Broker生成签名、内容寻址且永不可变的`CredentialGrantSet={executorIdentity,cutoverRunId,authorizationIntentHash,grantSetGeneration,allowedState,allowedWorkOrNull,binding=initialAttach{reservationTicket}|takeoverAttach{takeoverTicket,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,previousCompletionReceiptHashOrNull,nextTransitionCapabilityHashOrNull}|claimOrBegin{currentOwnershipHash,expectedVersion,expectedSessionHash}|refresh{currentOwnershipHash,oldSetHash},immutableActionSetHash,fencingEpochOrNull,revocationGeneration,sorted[{grantRef,hash,issuerRef,revocationHandle,expiresAt}]}`；takeoverAttach的两个Completion字段只在normalPhaseHandoff分支同时非null，其他分支同时为null。仅bootstrap reservation前允许`fencingEpochOrNull=null`，attach前必须由Ticket给出的epoch全等校验。Set本体不内嵌自指Hash、未来Authorization Hash或可变status，唯一外部真值是`GrantSetRef+payloadSha256`。集合转换结果是闭结构`GrantTransitionOutcome=none{reason=actionHasNoSetTransition|pendingSetAbsent|activeSetAbsent,noSetProofOrNull}|committed{receipt}`；pendingSetAbsent/activeSetAbsent必须携对应binding索引或Ownership Projection的Provider签名Absent Proof，普通无转换动作只能用actionHasNoSetTransition且proof=null。

Provider签名`GrantSetTransitionSetReceipt={transitionKind=activateAndRetire|retire|void|revoke,binding,activated={setRef+hash,from=pending,to=active}|null,retiredPrevious={setRef+hash,from=active,to=retired}|null,terminated=[{setRef+hash,from=pending|active,to=void|revoked}],authorizationHash}`；`binding`是闭Union：`initialAttach={runId,reservationTicketHash,attachCapabilityHash,newOwnershipCoreHash,action=attach,paramsHash,token,version,generation}`；`takeoverAttach={runId,previousOwnershipHash,takeoverTicketHash,ticketGeneration,newEpoch,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,takeoverMode=positionResume|domainTransition|normalPhaseHandoff,previousCompletionReceiptHashOrNull,nextTransitionCapabilityHashOrNull,attachCapabilityHash,newOwnershipCoreHash,action=attachTakeover,paramsHash,token,version,generation}`；`ownershipTransition={runId,previousOwnershipHash,newOwnershipCoreHash,action=claim|beginIntermediate|beginForward|refreshAuthorization|complete|completeIntermediate|completeAndRelease,paramsHash,token,version,generation}`；`expiry={ticketKind=reservation|takeover,ticketHash,providerObservedAt,expiresAt,expiryAction=expireReservation|expireTakeoverTicket,paramsHash,token}`；`isolation={authorizationHash,currentOwnershipHash,isolationGeneration,action=isolate,paramsHash,token}`。每个action只能选指定binding：initial attach无previous Ownership；takeover attach必须同时绑定旧Ownership、Ticket/Capability、ticketGeneration/newEpoch/Payload；normalPhaseHandoff恰好要求两个Completion字段非null，其他两种mode恰好要求二者为null；其他Session Set切换只用ownershipTransition；void只用expiry；revoke只用isolation且不预引用reserveTakeover。`activateAndRetire`恰好一份activated并可有一份retiredPrevious；`retire`恰好一份retiredPrevious；`void|revoke`至少一份terminated。claim/beginIntermediate/beginForward/attach/attachTakeover/refreshAuthorization用activateAndRetire，complete类用retire，存在pending Set的过期用void，存在active Set的隔离用revoke；reserve/renew/commitPoint/Forward Ledger CAS以及有签名Absent Proof的过期或隔离使用对应none分支。Ownership不反向引用Receipt Hash，避免Hash环。

每个attached GrantSet必须预含一份仅`refreshAuthorization+findReceipt`、受`credentialRefreshBefore`约束的Refresh Grant；refresh CAS验证其old-set membership proof，绝不能用尚未安装的new-set Grant自举。每个Provider/Store/KMS/Session/Mount Adapter在动作前同时introspect完整CredentialGrantBinding与当前revocationGeneration；Isolation先用`isolateGrantSet`原子bump generation使旧Grant在线失效，再按`activeSet{ref,hash,members}|noActiveSet{lastRetiredSetOrNull,ownershipHash}`执行已签成员计划，并把GrantSetIsolationReceipt与外部process/mount终止Receipt共同封装进ExecutorIsolation Receipt。合法相位边界没有active Set时仍须隔离旧进程、Mount并bump generation，但不得把retired Set冒充active。环境变量、默认Service Account、仅凭字符串Credential Ref、自报Scope或无法introspect的Grant全部失败。

`authorization/EvidenceKind.ts`定义唯一闭枚举：`BootstrapInstall|Task|SourceSnapshot|SourceSnapshotSet|FileDispositionReport|QualityRun|ReleaseArtifact|ReleaseManifest|QualitySignature|SignBundle|ExecutorToolBundle|ExecutorRunReceipt|CheckoutClaim|AuthorityAuthorization|SnapshotAuthorization|ArchiveAuthorization|CutoverAuthorization|CutoverState|RunAttestation|ArchivePrepared|ArchiveVerified|ArchiveRestore|ArchiveDrillSet|AuthorityBlob|AuthoritySnapshot|PostTreeEvidence|CanonicalPostBundle|CutoverPrepared|CutoverApplied|CutoverVerified|CutoverForwardPrepared|CutoverForwardCleaned|CutoverRepairAttempt|CutoverActivated|CutoverRestored|CutoverFinalized|CanonicalRefProviderReceipt`；新增Kind必须同时升级Schema Version、Decoder、Trust Policy和全部生产/消费Contract，不允许自由字符串。Reference/Traffic Zero是Cutover Core内的强类型字段，postdelete结果复用QualityRun/ReleaseArtifact/SignBundle/RunAttestation，Smoke是CutoverVerified内联结果，四者不得另造无Producer Kind。

`EvidenceEnvelope.ts`定义带密码学来源的Canonical Typed Envelope，字段固定为`schemaVersion/kind/mediaType/payloadSha256/payloadSize/trustPolicyHash/subjectId/issuedAt/signerId/keyVersion/algorithm/signature`，其中signature是对前述字段按JCS排序且排除signature本身后的Ed25519签名；JSON Evidence另携带与Hash一致的Canonical payload，二进制Evidence携带不可变blobRef。EvidenceRef指向签名Envelope；消费者必须验证Ref、签名和payload/raw字节。Trust Policy按Kind固定允许Signer/Verify Key：Bootstrap/Task/FileDispositionReport/Quality/ReleaseArtifact/Manifest/Signature/SignBundle/ToolBundle由quality-signer，SourceSnapshot/SourceSnapshotSet由Snapshot授权绑定的snapshot-core-signer，CheckoutClaim由checkout-claim-signer，Authority/Snapshot/Archive/Cutover四类Authorization由authorization-issuer，RunAttestation由attestor，Authority/Archive/Cutover Core由对应授权绑定的core-signer，ExecutorRunReceipt由Launcher signer，只有需要跨Core独立寻址的Canonical Ref Provider Receipt才由Adapter在验Provider原签名后封装为`CanonicalRefProviderReceipt`；错Kind/Key/时间窗均失败。Telemetry/Traffic/Deployment/Migration/Mount/AuthorityMaterialization/AuthorityUnmount/ExecutorIsolation/Session Receipt保持Provider原签名的Canonical Struct，由对应Adapter以授权中的providerVerifyRef/schema完整验证后内联当前Core、Claim、RunAttestation、Session CAS或ExecutorRunReceipt，不进入EvidenceStore、不占EvidenceKind，也不要求Launcher持有Core Signer。原始二进制先PutRaw，外层Envelope再签其Hash。

`authorization/EvidenceEnvelopeBuilder.ts`是无I/O纯Builder：JSON先JCS规范化，二进制先接收已经完整回读验证的BlobRef，再产`payloadSha256/payloadSize/subject canonical bytes`；`EvidenceSigner.ts`固定Port为`sign({canonicalBytesHash,kind,subjectId,purpose,keyVersion,deadline},signerCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken)/findSignerReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`并返回`signerIdentity/keyVersion/algorithm/signature/signerReceipt/credentialBindingHash/stableToken`，唯一`KmsEvidenceSigner.ts`逐项核对Signer Ref、完整Binding、Identity、Key Version、Purpose、Subject、Hash、TTL与generation。签名前先写`signActionIntent`；回包丢失只能按`evidenceSigner sign→findSignerReceipt`恢复，同subject同Hash同Token返回原Receipt，不得重新签名或换Token。私钥禁止进入进程、Bundle或Object Store。Quality Sign、CLI Core与ToolLauncher分别使用Claim/State或相位授权给自己的Signer配置；Publisher只负责Store Put，绝不能兼任签名。只有`GitCanonicalRef` Adapter在验证Provider原签名后，才可按`verify|restore|postcommit-repair.deploymentForwardPatch`授权的精确Canonical Receipt Signer Purpose与独立Receipt Publisher Scope把Receipt包装为`CanonicalRefProviderReceipt` Envelope，并用独立Publisher+exact-subject Recovery Reader执行唯一subject条件写/恢复；Telemetry、Traffic、Deployment、Migration、Mount、AuthorityMaterialization、AuthorityUnmount、ExecutorIsolation和Session Adapter只返回各自Provider原签名Canonical Struct并内联相应Core/Claim/Attestation/Session，禁止使用Core Signer另造Evidence。

`authorization/EvidenceStore.ts`只定义`putRawIfAbsent(stream,expectedHash,size,mediaType,publisherCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken)/getRaw(blobRef,expectedHash,size,readerCredentialGrantBinding)/putSignedIfAbsent(signedEnvelope,publisherCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken)/putSignedSubjectIfAbsent(signedEnvelope,kind,subjectId,publisherCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken)/getSignedBySubject(kind,subjectId,recoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding)/head(ref,readerCredentialGrantBinding)/getExpectedKind(ref,expectedKind,readerCredentialGrantBinding)`；唯一`ObjectEvidenceStore.ts`只验签/复算/存取，绝不调用KMS。所有Mutation Request和Store Receipt逐字节绑定完整Binding、stableToken与credentialBindingHash；所有Read Binding的exactActions/exactTargets必须包含精确BlobRef或kind+subject，不能用prefix替代subject恢复。二进制固定顺序为raw条件写→getRaw完整回读→Builder→Signer→Writer；JSON固定Builder→Signer→Writer。subject方法在同一线性条件写中创建`(kind,subjectId)→EvidenceRef`唯一索引：同subject同Ref重放返回原值，不同Ref冲突，Recovery Reader只可读取授权的exact kind+subject且无List。Canonical CAS Receipt、ExecutorRunReceipt与其他“Put成功但Ref回包可能丢失”的对象必须使用该方法，subject按授权公式确定，恢复不得重签猜Ref。`getRaw`只接受内容地址BlobRef及精确hash/size scope，不解析Envelope；Crash在raw后按Hash恢复。`getExpectedKind`与`getSignedBySubject`都必须完整Get、复算Ref Hash、校验Envelope/Ref/Kind/subject、按Kind选择唯一Decoder和Verify Key并验签，再对二进制payload完整复算Hash/size；head不能替代消费验证。所有Bootstrap/Task/SourceSnapshot/SourceSnapshotSet/FileDispositionReport、ReleaseArtifact、ExecutorToolBundle/RunReceipt、四类Authorization、Archive/Cutover各Core、Quality/Manifest/Signature/SignBundle/CheckoutClaim/RunAttestation、Authority/PostTree/Bundle和CAS Receipt都必须以已验签EvidenceRef出现，不能只给Hash或本地路径。当前`--evidence`只是0600临时输出。Publisher Binding只允许已签allowedKinds/prefix/subjectId/expiry下的PutIfAbsent；Reader/Recovery Binding只允许已签Kind/Prefix/Subject/expiry下的Head/Get/getRaw；三者都无Overwrite/Delete/List且不可互换。未知scheme/store/kind、Signer、Decoder失败或Hash/size不等立即失败。

EvidenceStore Mutation恢复Port另固定为`findMutationReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`，闭映射只接受原`putRawIfAbsent|putSignedIfAbsent|putSignedSubjectIfAbsent`的同store、target/subject、payload Hash、stableToken与credentialBindingHash；条件写前必须持久化storeActionIntent。返回原签名Store Receipt或SignedAbsent；Absent时才允许同Token重试原条件写，禁止find触发Put。`head/getRaw/getExpectedKind/getSignedBySubject`是只读消费，不生成伪Mutation Receipt。

`CheckoutClaimIssuer.ts`定义`issue(payload,issuerPolicy,claimSignerCredentialGrantBinding,claimPublisherCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken)/findIssueReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`，唯一`EvidenceCheckoutClaimIssuer.ts`复用Builder/Signer/Store：先以内层checkout-claim-signer签Canonical Claim，再以同一Trust Policy允许的Envelope purpose签外层`CheckoutClaim`并用独立Publisher Binding发布。普通CI平台与Cutover prepare都必须先有`issuerRef/claimSignerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose/claimPublisherCredentialGrantBinding+prefix`才可取得CheckoutClaimRef；Claim内部给未来Quality用的Publisher绝不自发布Claim。`AuthorizationIssuer.ts`与唯一`EvidenceAuthorizationIssuer.ts`以同样流程把Authority/Snapshot/Archive/Cutover Authorization payload发布成对应Kind，Port同样要求独立issuerSigner/issuerPublisher Binding、stableToken和findIssueReceipt；输入绑定最小Scope。平台与Launcher只传`--authorization-ref`，完整Get/验Envelope与内层签名后才0600临时物化；AuthorizationReceipt消费`authorizationRef+payloadHash`，后续State/restore取得同一个Ref，不依赖本地文件路径。

目标Authorization发布前的Issuer恢复使用`AuthorizationIssuerRecoveryInvocation.bootstrap={issuerPolicyHash,issuerRef,targetAuthorizationKind,targetPurpose,canonicalPayloadHash,issuerSignerCredentialGrantBinding,issuerPublisherCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken,attestedIssuerHash,issuerIntentHash}`；Issuer Intent必须在首次sign/put前fsync，以(targetKind,payloadHash,token)唯一索引返回既有Authorization Evidence Ref+Store/Signer Receipt或SignedAbsent，不依赖尚未存在的目标Authorization Ref。已有业务Authorization内的CheckoutClaimIssuer只用普通control-recovery。两类findIssueReceipt都不能签名、Put、换Token或扩大Kind。

AuthorizationIssuer bootstrap并非把未来目标Authorization当恢复Authority。Bootstrap Invocation还必须携`issuerSignerRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding{exactActions=[findSignerReceipt]}`、`issuerPublisherRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding{exactActions=[findMutationReceipt]}`、`issuerExactSubjectReaderCredentialGrantBinding{exactActions=[getSignedBySubject],exactTargets=[targetKind,subjectId]}`以及Signer/Store Provider Ref、Verify Ref、Receipt Schema和各自deadline；三份恢复Binding与原Mutation Binding互不复用。`EvidenceAuthorizationIssuer`在自己的0600顺序日志持久化`issuerIntent→signReceipt→storeReceipt→exactSubjectRead→issueCompleted`，每步使用由issuer stableToken确定性派生且已在Bootstrap Payload签入的Token。findIssueReceipt只读取这条索引并校验底层Receipt；若底层回包丢失，Issuer用对应find-only Binding恢复，不依赖过期Mutation Binding。只有Signer Receipt、Store Receipt、exact-subject完整回读和Envelope Hash全部闭合才返回Issue Receipt；任一步SignedAbsent才允许用原Mutation Token重试。

ordinaryRelease CheckoutClaim同样没有可依赖的未来Claim Authorization，因此不再引用不存在的`platformReleaseAuthorization`。可信引导明确分两步且无自环：第一步，平台生成由Trust Policy预置Release Issuer公钥直接验证的`PlatformReleaseIntent={schemaVersion,purpose=ordinary-release-intent,claimRunId,expectedCommitOid,expectedTreeOid,sourceTreeHash,toolchainRootHash,authoritySourceIntent{sourceKind,五条Entry期望,materializedRoot/namespace/action+target+token+scopeTemplate+receiptPredicate},qualityCapabilityIntent{allowedKinds,prefix,subject,signerPurposes},issuerPolicyHash,issuerIdentity,keyVersion,createdAt,expiresAt,nonce,signature}`；Intent只承诺模板、Target、Token和Subject，不含任何未来Grant Ref/Hash、Generation Receipt、Materialization Receipt或Claim Payload Hash。CredentialBroker以Intent Hash取得standalone Generation并签发动作互斥的Authority Materialize/Unmount、Quality Writer/Reader/Signer、Attestor/Publisher/Recovery Reader及Claim Signer/Publisher/Recovery能力。第二步，Release Issuer把这些完整Binding与Generation Receipt闭合成`PlatformReleaseInvocation={schemaVersion,purpose=ordinary-release-claim,platformReleaseIntent+hash,claimRunId,expectedCommitOid,expectedTreeOid,sourceTreeHash,toolchainRootHash,authoritySourcePlan,qualityCapabilitySet,claimCapabilitySet,claimIntentHash,issuerPolicyHash,issuerIdentity,keyVersion,createdAt,expiresAt,nonce,signature}`；`authoritySourcePlan`含已签四份Materialize/Unmount Mutation/Recovery Binding但不含未来Materialization Receipt，`claimIntentHash`覆盖最终Claim除未来Materialization/Unmount Provider Receipt内容外的全部不变量和Receipt Predicate。两对象都不是EvidenceKind、不经Evidence Store自举，也不携Credential值。`cutover.yml`只固定Release Issuer身份/算法/公钥版本、允许的Commit来源、External Authority Policy Hash与Credential Issuer模板；Verifier按`Intent→Generation/Grant→Invocation→Materialization Receipt→Claim`逐Hash验证，最终Claim重算claimIntentHash。取得Materialization Receipt后，平台使用`CheckoutClaimIssuerRecoveryInvocation.bootstrap={platformReleaseInvocation+hash,claimRunId,canonicalClaimPayloadHash,claimSignerCredentialGrantBinding,claimPublisherCredentialGrantBinding,claimSignerRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding,claimPublisherRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding,claimExactSubjectReaderCredentialGrantBinding,stableToken,attestedPlatformIssuerHash,issuerIntentHash,provider/verify/schema/deadlines}`发布Claim。EvidenceCheckoutClaimIssuer使用与上一段相同的持久Intent、两层find和exact-subject回读合同；Issuer日记永久绑定Intent、Invocation字节与Hash。cutoverPredelete/forwardCandidate已有Cutover Authorization时才允许使用普通control-recovery；三支Decoder不得混用，Intent/Invocation不能授权任何Archive/Cutover/Provider业务动作。

`execution/AuthorityMaterializationControl.ts`是Quality读取External Authority的唯一物化Port，固定`authorityMaterialize(request,stableToken)/findAuthorityMaterializeReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/authorityUnmount(request,stableToken)/findAuthorityUnmountReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；唯一`ProviderAuthorityMaterializationControl.ts`创建独占只读mount namespace，禁止复用Candidate的`MountControl`。Request Source是闭Union：`externalReadonly={platformReleaseInvocationHash,sourceRootRealpathHash,entries[5]}`或`snapshotPair={authoritySnapshotRef+hash,authoritySnapshotExecutorRunReceiptRef+hash,entries[5]}`；共同签入operationRunId、authorityPolicyHash、materializedRootRealpath/parent/device、namespace、options=`ro,nodev,nosuid,noexec`、ownerExecutor、lease、动作独立`materializeCredentialGrantBinding|unmountCredentialGrantBinding`、Provider Verify Ref/Receipt Schema、Deadline和稳定Token。ordinaryRelease两动作使用standaloneAuthorized Binding并由PlatformReleaseInvocation预签；cutoverPredelete/forwardCandidate/verify使用当前Session business Binding并由对应Cutover Authorization预签。任何Binding都只含一个精确action=`authorityMaterialize|authorityUnmount|findAuthorityMaterializeReceipt|findAuthorityUnmountReceipt`、target与token。回包丢失时必须另签同context、同originalAction/token/paramsHash的exactControl `materializeRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding|unmountRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding`，分别调用`findAuthorityMaterializeReceipt|findAuthorityUnmountReceipt`，SignedAbsent才以原Mutation Token重试。

Provider签名`AuthorityMaterializationReceipt={operationRunId,sourceKind,sourcePairOrInvocationHash,entriesHash,requestHash,materializedRootRealpath+inode+device,namespace,options,ownerExecutor,leaseExpiresAt,stableToken,credentialBindingHash,providerIdentity,signature}`；它只在逐项内容Hash/size/mode全等、路径无符号链接且内核挂载表证实只读后返回。`AuthorityUnmountReceipt={operationRunId,originalMaterializationReceiptHash,sourceKind,requestHash,namespace,unmountToken,credentialBindingHash,mountAbsent=true,namespaceZero=true,entryHandlesZero=true,providerIdentity,signature}`；两份Receipt的canonicalBytes都覆盖schemaVersion、Provider Verify Ref/Receipt Schema、Request Hash、Token和credentialBindingHash，Adapter先验Provider身份/签名再允许内联。PlatformReleaseInvocation或Cutover Authorization先授权materialize；取得Receipt后Issuer才可把`authorityInput + authorityUnmountPlan={unmountCredentialGrantBinding,unmountRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding,unmountToken,deadline,unmountReceiptPredicateHash}`签进Claim或CutoverState。Quality子进程只收到已验Receipt的Canonical投影；退出后普通CI Wrapper或CutoverCli必须先unmount/恢复、验zero并把两份Receipt Hash写入RunAttestation，再允许发布相位Core。Launcher崩溃时同一授权和Token续跑；Lease过期由Provider自动fence但不删除任何Authority Blob。这样首次externalReadonly、后续snapshotPair和postApply共享同一可恢复Producer，而不依赖Fresh Checkout旁边偶然存在的`../docs`目录。

Archive需要独立、先签发且不依赖未来业务Evidence的`tools/quality/src/archive/ArchiveAuthorization.ts`，以消除“先归档还是先授权”的环。每次CLI调用使用一份新授权，载体继承Receipt/Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader与Signer公共Binding，并固定含`phase=prepare|verify|restore|seal`、单次nonce、共享archiveSessionId、唯一operationRunId、trustPolicyHash、已Attested的`executorToolBundleRef/executorCommitOid/executorTreeOid/executorRunAttestationRef/entryName=ArchiveCli/entryHash/nodeBinaryHash/npmCliHash/npmResourceTreeHash`、`archiveConfigHash/archiveStoreRef/archiveStoreCapabilities`、基线`FileDispositionReportRef+hash+preManifestHash+postManifestCandidateHash`、`SourceSnapshotSet Core+Receipt Pair`、按rootId UTF-8排序且恰好`roots[6]{rootId,sourceSnapshotRef+hash,sourceRealpath,sourceEntrySetHash}`、逐个retired Source Entry（present或deletedTombstone）、Git Metadata Bundle/Entry Set与Root Activation中需独立留存的Entry、phase所需前序Core+Receipt、restore destination、时间和签名。`archiveStoreCapabilities`是随phase判别的闭Union：prepare=`{archivePutCredentialGrantBinding,archiveReadbackCredentialGrantBinding,archiveRetentionCredentialGrantBinding,mutationRecoveryPlan[]{originalAction=putIfAbsent|ensureRetentionAtLeast,originalToken,originalParamsHash,recoveryCredentialGrantBinding.scope=exactControl.context=standalone},putTokenSetHash,retentionTokenSetHash,recoveryPlanHash}`；verify/restore=`{archiveReaderCredentialGrantBinding}`；seal=`{archiveReaderCredentialGrantBinding,archiveRetentionCredentialGrantBinding,retentionRecoveryPlan[]{originalAction=ensureRetentionAtLeast,originalToken,originalParamsHash,recoveryCredentialGrantBinding.scope=exactControl.context=standalone},retentionTokenSetHash,recoveryPlanHash}`。Mutation与Read Binding必须为standaloneAuthorized并绑定同operationRunId/purpose/generationReceiptHash；每条Recovery Binding必须是同standalone context的exactControl recovery并逐字绑定唯一originalAction/token/paramsHash。读、写、Retention与find不得复用，Recovery Plan的排序集合及Hash必须与Entry Token集合一一双射；不适用字段严格absent。当前相位授权不绑定未来自身Receipt。prepare从Report取得已签pre Manifest，present从Live lstat复验后归档，deletedTombstone从Snapshot/Base Blob归档并证明Live absent，Git Bundle/Ref Manifest同样归档。prepare为六Root分别生成Canonical ArchiveEntryManifest，并在同一次operationRun内发布唯一聚合Prepared Core；聚合Core按rootId绑定六Root的Snapshot/EntrySet/Manifest Hash、全部Entry及无重复/无遗漏证明，Launcher再产唯一Receipt；verify/restore/seal只读该Entry Manifest与前序Pair。每次restore使用不同run/nonce/destination；seal绑定至少两份Restore Pair，且其中一次必须实际重建Git refs/commit connectivity。四阶段的六Root、Entry与archiveSessionId集合完全相同，禁止glob、目录前缀或只写Root ID。

`tools/quality/src/archive/ArchiveStore.ts`只定义流式`putIfAbsent(request,stableToken)/findPutReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/head(request,readerCredentialGrantBinding)/get(request,readerCredentialGrantBinding)/ensureRetentionAtLeast(request,stableToken)/findRetentionReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；每个Mutation Request直接携动作独立完整Binding。`ObjectArchiveStore.ts`是唯一S3兼容实现，使用内容Hash键、条件创建、服务端KMS加密和Compliance Object Lock。Blob Object metadata只保存内容不变量`sha256/size`，不能保存会随Entry变化的mode、linkTarget、sourceRootId或sourceSnapshotHash；后四者写在按Entry签名并被Evidence绑定的Archive Manifest中，因此六Root相同字节、不同mode或不同Snapshot可以安全复用同一Blob。Put、Get/Head、Retention、find使用四份互不复用的Binding，显式拒绝Delete/List/BypassGovernance。`retentionToken=sha256("archive-retention-v1"||operationRunId||objectVersion||requiredRetainUntil||entryManifestHash)`；Provider以(objectVersion,token)唯一索引原子读取旧retainUntil并仅在不足时延长为`max(old,required)`，签名`RetentionReceipt={objectVersion,oldRetainUntil,newRetainUntil,requiredRetainUntil,token,credentialBindingHash}`且永久返回原old/new。响应丢失必须findRetentionReceipt，SignedAbsent才同Token重试，禁止以重读新现态冒充原Receipt。put同理由findPutReceipt恢复。两类Mutation先写正式storeAction Intent，Completed绑定Provider Receipt；普通文件按字节流式上传，符号链接把link target字节作为自身Blob并在Entry Manifest保存mode/linkTarget，目标内容必须是独立Entry，禁止跟随链接越Root。

`tools/quality/src/archive/ArchiveCli.ts`是唯一Archive业务入口，但不得从源码或tsx直接运行。四阶段都由已验证的只读`ToolLauncher.mjs --entry ArchiveCli --authorization-ref <EvidenceRef>`启动，Launcher公共argv绑定ToolBundle/AuthorizationRef/targetShop，并把Canonical ExecutorPreflightHash传给Bundle内ArchiveCli；入口再接收`prepare|verify|restore|seal --trust-policy --session-id --run-id --evidence`。prepare额外且只接受授权绑定的FileDispositionReport、SourceSnapshotSet Pair与逐Root Snapshot Ref；verify/restore/seal只接受Entry Manifest Ref；restore再要求签名destination。所有阶段先完整Get Authorization Envelope/内签名，证明preflight与Bundle/entry/node/npm一致、命令=phase，再验证Nonce和前序Core+RunReceipt。prepare对present Entry要求Live lstat/hash/mode/link等于Snapshot，对deletedTombstone要求Live absent并从Snapshot raw/base blob完整Get；Git Metadata按签名Manifest处理。verify逐BlobGet；restore/seal不依赖原Root或活动pre Manifest。CLI产Core、子进程退出后Launcher用独立Core Reader验证后才签/发布绑定Core的RunReceipt；ArchiveCli不能删除源文件。

删除放行的顶层证据恰好是三组archiveSession级不可拆Aggregate Pair：`ArchivePrepared Core Ref + prepare ExecutorRunReceiptRef`、`ArchiveVerified Core Ref + verify ExecutorRunReceiptRef`、`ArchiveDrillSet Core Ref + seal ExecutorRunReceiptRef`。三份Aggregate Core都必须逐字绑定同一archiveSessionId、同一FileDisposition/SnapshotSet Pair、按rootId排序的恰好六个Root及其sourceSnapshotHash/sourceEntrySetHash，且每个Root恰好一份phase子Manifest、全局Entry集合无重复无遗漏；Prepared/Verified Core逐Entry记录URI、ObjectVersion、ETag、sha256、size、mode、linkTarget、Root/Snapshot、old/new retainUntil、执行Hash和executorPreflightHash；DrillSet Core含至少两个不同的`restoreRunId + destinationRealpathHash + operatorId + startedAt + completedAt + ArchiveRestore Core Ref + 对应restore ExecutorRunReceiptRef`，两个目标目录、时间和操作者不得相同。Cutover先验证三份Aggregate Pair的session/root/entry集合逐Hash全等，再对每个Core与Receipt分别完整Get、复算并验证Receipt确实绑定该Core/exit=0/postcheck成功，再重验至少两次独立Restore；保留截止必须不早于硬切后90天与第二次成功Drill完成后的策略窗口之较晚者。缺任一Receipt、对象不可读、Hash不等、Retention不足或Evidence不完整都禁止Delete。

#### 33.4.2 受控Cutover与可恢复Remove

`tools/quality/src/authority/AuthorityAuthorization.ts`、`AuthoritySnapshot.ts`与`AuthorityCli.ts`组成AuthoritySnapshotRef的唯一Producer，且先于任何PostTree/Cutover授权运行。`AuthorityAuthorization`的`purpose=authority-capture`载体继承上述Receipt/Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader公共字段，并绑定单次nonce、authorityRunId、trustPolicyHash、已存在且通过Clean CI的`executorToolBundleRef/executorCommitOid/executorTreeOid/executorRunAttestationRef/entryName=AuthorityCli/entryHash/nodeBinaryHash/npmCliHash/npmResourceTreeHash`、Authority Root真实路径Hash、下列五个精确canonicalPath及预期Hash/size/mode、时间和签名；它不依赖任何未来Authority/PostTree/Cutover Evidence，因而没有先有Ref还是先有授权的环。五个条目恰好是`docs/福利商城功能清单260821.xlsx`、`docs/福利商城架构和补齐修改清单.md`、`docs/brand/SMART-WING-UNIFIED-VI-1.0.md`、`docs/代码与前端开发规范.md`、`docs/福利商城全系统根治方案.md`，少一项、多一项、路径别名或Hash漂移都失败。

`AuthorityCli.ts`是该只读捕获的唯一业务入口，但只能由只读ToolBundle中的`ToolLauncher.mjs --entry AuthorityCli --bundle-ref <ExecutorToolBundleRef> --authorization-ref <AuthorityAuthorizationEvidenceRef> --target-shop <signed-realpath>`启动；Launcher cwd固定executorRoot、`shell=false`，先以EvidenceStore完整Get/复算AuthorityAuthorization Envelope与内层签名、消费AuthorizationReceipt，再把0600临时授权路径和Canonical ExecutorPreflightHash只传给Bundle内入口；入口接收`capture --trust-policy --run-id --evidence --executor-preflight-hash <hash>`。入口、Launcher、Node和全部Import不得来自targetShop/PATH，本地绝对Authorization JSON或预置Receipt JSON均为非法旁路。它先证明Executor Bundle/Attestation/entry/node/preflight与授权相等，再验证Trust Policy/过期/Nonce；随后从签名targetShop解析唯一`../docs`Authority Root，逐路径`realpath+lstat`拒绝符号链接、目录、越Root与非普通文件。逐字节复算已签Hash/size后，以`AuthorityBlob` Typed Envelope逐项PutIfAbsent并完整Get验证。`AuthoritySnapshot.ts`生成Canonical Manifest，字段固定为`schemaVersion/authorityRunId/trustPolicyHash/executorPreflightHash/capturedAt/entries[]`，每项只有`canonicalPath/blobRef/sha256/size/mode=0444`，按canonicalPath排序、禁止重复；发布为`AuthoritySnapshot`并返回唯一Ref。子进程退出后Launcher才发布绑定Snapshot Ref与AuthorizationReceipt的ExecutorRunReceiptRef；Cutover prepare授权必须同时绑定Snapshot与该Receipt。捕获不修改Authority文件、不读取接口密钥单元格语义，只按原始字节归档。

`tools/quality/src/snapshot/SnapshotAuthorization.ts`、`SourceSnapshot.ts`与`SnapshotCli.ts`组成六棵来源树和全部物理旁带事实的唯一、独立、先于Cutover prepare运行的捕获相位。外部Issuer在Live来源仍未改动时发布`SnapshotAuthorization` EvidenceRef；Canonical Payload继承四类授权的Receipt/Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader与Signer公共字段，并固定`purpose=source-snapshot-capture/snapshotRunId/nonce/trustPolicyHash/executorToolBundleRef+commit+tree+attestation/entryName=SnapshotCli+entryHash+nodeBinaryHash/npmCliHash/npmResourceTreeHash/baselineFileDispositionReportRef+hash+preManifestHash+postManifestCandidateHash/恰好六个rootId+sourceRealpath+expectedBaseCommit+expectedStatusHash+expectedDevice+expectedParentRealpath/gitCapturePolicyHash+expectedGitRootRelations/ephemeralCapturePolicyHash+allowedIgnoredRootNames/retiredCandidateEntrySetHash/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`。Issuer必须先完整Get普通release发布的FileDispositionReport并从中验证两份Manifest与Architecture Task Payload Hash，因而Snapshot Authorization不消费悬空Manifest Ref。每个Source Entry是封闭Union：`present`必须有path/type/sha256/size/mode/linkTarget或null；`deletedTombstone`必须有path/baseCommit/baseBlobOid/baseBlobSha256/mode/linkTarget或null以及expectedLiveAbsent=true。Git/ignored捕获只读且受Root/Policy约束，可以枚举但不能删除或扩大到未签Root。授权签入snapshot Core Publisher/Reader/Signer与Launcher Publisher/Reader/Signer的独立最小Scope；不得复用未来Quality Publisher或Quality Signer。

平台只用只读Bundle执行`ToolLauncher.mjs --entry SnapshotCli --authorization-ref <SnapshotAuthorizationEvidenceRef> --target-shop <signed-realpath>`。Launcher完整Get/双签验证、消费nonce并产Executor preflight；`SnapshotCli capture`再逐Root/逐Entry按排序重验realpath/lstat/status/baseCommit。`present`读取Live原始字节并PutRaw；`deletedTombstone`必须先证明Live路径缺失，再从已签baseCommit的Git object读取、复算blob OID/hash并PutRaw，禁止把“缺失”当成无内容。它在同一稳定观察窗内调用无main的GitMetadata/EphemeralArtifact，只读生成Git Bundle/Ref/Entry Manifest和Ephemeral Manifest，逐raw blob Put+getRaw回读。`SourceSnapshot.ts`为每Root发布一个`SourceSnapshot` Envelope，字段固定`schemaVersion/snapshotRunId/rootId/sourceRealpathHash/baseCommit/statusHash/delegationHash/entries[path,status,blobRef,sha256,size,mode,linkTarget]`；随后SnapshotCli发布唯一`SourceSnapshotSet` Core，绑定Authorization Ref/Hash、baselineFileDispositionReportRef/hash、preManifestHash、postManifestCandidateHash、六个按rootId排序且完整回读的Snapshot Ref/Hash、gitMetadataManifest+bundle BlobRef/hash、ephemeralArtifactManifest BlobRef/hash、source/git/ephemeral EntrySet Hash、captureStartedAt/completedAt和executorPreflightHash。子进程退出后Launcher用独立Core Reader验证，再发布绑定该Core与AuthorizationReceipt的ExecutorRunReceipt并完成postcheck。Cutover prepare、Archive、PostTree和两次cutover Quality都只能消费这同一`SourceSnapshotSet Core + ExecutorRunReceipt` Pair；Sign不得重新读Live来源或重发Snapshot。普通release没有该Pair。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/PostTree.ts`先于任何Delete构造可重放的最终Git事实。它先完整Get并验证`SourceSnapshotSet Core + ExecutorRunReceipt` Pair及其绑定的基线FileDispositionReport，再只从Pair中恰好六个`SourceSnapshot` Ref及其raw blobs读取输入；随后从Report Payload取得确定性preManifest/postManifestCandidate并复算Hash，按Source Key/Target规则物化最终Shop。本地Live Root只用于之后的Apply漂移复验，不能作为构建输入。它拒绝未登记文件、符号链接越界、Last Write Wins和生成后Dirty Diff；以签名输入固定parent preCommit、author/committer/timestamp/message创建postTreeOid/postCommitOid，并生成CanonicalPost.bundle。PostTreeEvidence签入pre/post OID/tree/hash、FileDispositionReport Ref/Hash、两Manifest Hash、Bundle、SnapshotSet Pair与六Snapshot Ref/Hash及工具版本；预删除Quality必须验证该Bundle，不能在脏迁移树伪造零Diff。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/CleanClone.ts`是无main的窄组件，入口为封闭`mode=initial|forwardPrepare`。`initial`只从已验证CanonicalPostBundle取得原始Git Bundle，并在prepare授权签入的精确`activationCandidateRealpath`检出postCommit；`forwardPrepare`只从已验普通release Pair及`postcommit-forward-prepare(operation=build)`授权固定的Canonical Remote/OID取得字节，在精确`forwardCandidateRealpath`检出forwardCommit，且必须证明`activationCommitted`存在、Traffic Guard held、当前targetShop inode未变。两分支分别绑定自己的commit/tree/sourceTree、candidate/parent/device与allowedRootHash，Decoder拒绝字段混用；路径不得由mktemp随机猜测。`materialize`先断言HEAD/tree/sourceTree和三项Git Clean，记录candidate/target inode/device与每个创建Root；Generator所需ExternalAuthority Snapshot物化在独立只读execution mount，不随candidate rename，每个Blob完整Get并由AuthorityMount记录Ref/hash/mode。predelete或forward Quality/Attestor只允许在签名`qualityEphemeralAllowlist`（node_modules、dist、.next、.vinext、.wrangler、Coverage、docs/operations/evidence下的精确Root）写安装/构建项；Tracked/Git/业务源始终只读。进程全部退出、外部Evidence完成后，QuarantineFinalizer的qualityEphemeralCleanup模式按本次Ephemeral Manifest逐项删除这些可重建项并验零，唯一`sealCandidate(mode)`再复验Git Clean并按path排序生成`RootActivationManifest`；Manifest强制`stage=initial|forwardRepair`、穷尽已清洁candidate全部物理Entry、seal时当前targetShop全部旧物理Entry、共同parent/device和该分支expected commit/tree。Seal先把按path排序的`sealPlanHash`写入`sealIntent`，再对每个Tracked regular file按计划执行`sealEntryCompleted`：普通文件0444、可执行文件0555；目录保留签名0755且Owner限定为当前隔离Sandbox，使未来verify只可在allowlist Root创建临时项，任何非allowlist创建由Physical Manifest/Watcher拒绝。全部Entry完成后才写`candidateSealed` checkpoint；Crash按sealPlan与inode逐项续跑，禁止无Intent chmod。它禁止再次修改Tracked字节、Build成品或接收服务流量，但不以0555祖先目录阻断未来postApply临时allowlist；Prepared/ForwardPrepared Core与Session Completion逐字节传递Manifest、allowlist Hash及路径/设备约束。Apply或Forward Repair前出现新增、缺失或inode/hash漂移即失败。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/RootActivator.ts`是无main的唯一物理Shop激活器，Port为封闭`mode=initialApply|precommitRestore|forwardRepair`。每次两段rename必须各有独立、已fsync的正式WAL Pair：initial为`rootActionIntent(rootAction=activateOld)→rename old→rootActionCompleted`再`rootActionIntent(rootAction=activateCandidate)→rename candidate→rootActionCompleted`；restore为`restoreNew`与`restoreOld`两Pair；forward为`forwardOld`与`forwardCandidate`两Pair。`initialApply`在Fence+Drain+trafficZero后复验`stage=initial` Manifest、candidate与targetShop同父同设备、candidate Clean/OID/tree、old target inode及全部Physical Entry；每次rename后都fsync共同父目录并重验inode。`precommitRestore`只在无ActivationCommit/open Receipt时把newShop移到newShop quarantine、oldShop原子放回并复验原inode/hash。`forwardRepair`只接受ActivationCommitReceipt、held Traffic Guard、`CutoverForwardPrepared` Pair、`stage=forwardRepair` Manifest与`deploymentForwardPatch`授权；它把当前Shop原子移入精确repair quarantine、forward candidate原子改名为唯一targetShop。Canonical `RootSwapResult={forwardId,oldTargetRealpath+inode+device+manifestHash,newTargetRealpath+inode+device+manifestHash,repairQuarantineRealpath,fencingEpoch,forwardOldIntentHash+completedHash,forwardCandidateIntentHash+completedHash}`逐字节绑定四条正式记录及其Hash，且永不把提交后的旧制品恢复为活动目标。`RootSwapResult`不是EvidenceKind或未来Pair。CutoverCli必须把完整Result、两对Intent/Completed的有序WAL记录/prevHash证明、terminalWalHash、Journal inode/device、Session ownership/version/epoch和现场lstat observation规范化为`RootSwapAttestation`，用本授权Core Signer的独立`purpose=session-forward-commit`签名；Session Store接收完整Attestation而非两个裸Hash，验证Signer/Authorization、WAL Hash Chain、Result Hash、资源/epoch与现场不变量后才返回Provider签名ForwardCommitReceipt。Crash若只完成第一段或第二段Intent，按正式记录、inode/path与当前epoch幂等补齐对应Completed/rename；任何第三对象、跨设备或路径漂移停机。Applied或Repair Evidence必须证明目标绝对路径就是授权commit/tree且Clean；Finalize只逐Entry清理所有已消费swap的oldShop，绝不对活动targetShop执行reset/checkout/覆盖。

所有已存在业务Authorization之后的Provider/Broker/Session find-only调用使用唯一签名输入`RecoveryInvocation={recoveryAuthorizationRef,recoveryAuthorizationHash,recoveryCredentialGrantBinding,originalAuthorizationRef,originalAuthorizationHash,providerKind,findOperation,originalAction,stableToken,canonicalParamsHash,attestedLauncherHash}`。下列每个普通find Port实际都只接受这一完整结构。例外闭表只有两项：CredentialBroker在业务Authorization发布前恢复Generation/Issue/Seal使用`BrokerRecoveryInvocation.bootstrap`；Session `reserve|reserveTakeover`在尚未取得返回generation/new epoch时使用`SessionBootstrapRecoveryInvocation={bootstrapAuthorizationRef+hash,bootstrapCredentialGrantBinding,action=reserve|reserveTakeover,actionInstanceId,stableToken,canonicalParamsHash,subjectExecutor,attestedLauncherHash}`。后者只能按Store的(action,token,invocationHash)唯一索引返回完整SessionMutationResultReceipt或签名Absent，绝不能调用其他find或Mutation；普通Session find使用`SessionRecoveryInvocation.authorized={recoveryInvocation}`。Adapter先验相应control-recovery、session-state-read或bootstrap Authorization，再introspect完整Binding并证明providerKind/findOperation/action/token/paramsHash与原Mutation逐字段全等，随后才读Provider事实。任何只传token、构造器Credential、ambient Service Account、跨action find或find触发Mutation都拒绝。

`execution/TelemetryProbe.ts`是窄Port，固定`observe(request,stableToken)/findObservationReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；Request只接受已规范化Telemetry Policy、精确Query Catalog、不可变Observation Window、Deadline/Abort、当前Fencing Epoch与`telemetryCredentialGrantBinding`。`ProviderTelemetryProbe.ts`是唯一Adapter，按providerRef调用监控平台，不得改Query、补默认Series或把No Data当0。Provider必须把窗口截止时可见的原始样本集合冻结为签名Observation Receipt，绑定provider/signature/verifyRef/schema/queryHash/window/seriesHash/epoch/token/credentialBindingHash；响应丢失只能用findObservationReceipt取回同一Receipt，重新发起新观察必须使用新window和新actionInstanceId，禁止因迟到样本回填而重写旧结果。`trafficZero`要求所有必需Series存在、最新样本不旧于minFreshness、窗口无gap、每个值严格等于zeroThreshold；完整Receipt内联CutoverApplied或Repair Core。

`execution/TrafficControl.ts`是流量/任务栅栏窄Port，固定`fenceOld(request,token)/findReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/open(request,token)/fenceNew(request,token)`；`ProviderTrafficControl.ts`是唯一Adapter，只操作授权列出的gatewayRefs、schedulerRefs与consumerRefs。每个Request都直接携该动作独立`trafficCredentialGrantBinding`，Receipt逐字节回写credentialBindingHash。`fenceOld`以Provider原子epoch拒绝旧HTTP入口、暂停旧Scheduler/Consumer的新Claim并保留已Claim任务完成通道，Receipt绑定target集合、epoch、effectiveAt、token和签名；Crash必须用完整RecoveryInvocation从Provider恢复事实。只有Fence Receipt验签、所有in-flight Request/Lease/Unacked Handler排空，而且Telemetry观察窗口完整位于effectiveAt之后，`trafficZero`才成立；回看式零样本、No Data或“先查零再关入口”一律失败。`open` Request来源是封闭Union：`initialActivate={activateAuthorizationRef,originalFenceReceipt,derivedNewTargetMap,openNewToken}`、`postcommitRepair={repairAuthorizationRef,attemptId,parentAttemptPairOrNull,currentHeldFenceReceipt,derivedFinalTargetMap,firstOpenNewTokenOrReopenNewToken}`或`precommitRestore={restoreAuthorizationRef,originalFenceReceipt,verifiedOldTargetMap,reopenOldToken}`；每支都必须绑定当前Session version/epoch、互不复用的trafficCredentialGrantBinding、provider/verifyRef/receiptSchema、精确gateway/scheduler/consumer、Deadline/Abort和已验Target Map，Adapter不得依赖构造时或ambient Credential。`open`只能把同一Fence指向该分支已验签Target，第三目标或跨支Token失败。`fenceNew`仅用于`activationCommitted`之后的前向止损，其Request来源是封闭Union：`initialActivate={activateAuthorizationRef,openReceipt,derivedNewTargetMap,fenceNewToken}`或`postcommitRepair={repairAuthorizationRef,attemptId,parentAttemptPairOrNull,openOrReopenReceipt,derivedFinalTargetMap,fenceRepairedTargetToken}`；两支也绑定动作独立trafficCredentialGrantBinding/Verify/Schema/Deadline及当前epoch，不得指回old或跨支复用Token。初始Receipt/WAL进入activate continuation及CutoverActivated；repair Receipt/WAL进入CutoverRepairAttempt，若修复随后成功也进入CutoverActivated；restore Receipt进入CutoverRestored。所有Crash都先构造并验证对应action+token的RecoveryInvocation再调用findReceipt。

`execution/MountControl.ts`是Authority只读执行视图的窄Port，固定`mount(request,token)/findReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/unmount(request,token)`；唯一`ProviderMountControl.ts`只接受`source=AuthoritySnapshot Core+ExecutorRunReceipt Pair`、`stage=initial|forwardPrepare`、精确mountId/mountRealpath、Resource Keys、`options=readonly,nodev,nosuid,noexec`、由cutoverRunId+phase+nonce派生且尚无Consumer的sandboxId/mountNamespaceId、Entrypoint Hash集合、Fencing Epoch、Deadline和具体`mountCredentialGrantBinding`。Provider以mount Token create-if-absent隔离Sandbox/Mount Namespace；Receipt绑定Snapshot/payload Hash、sandbox/namespace、mount inode/device/options/entrypointAllowlistHash/effectiveAt/token/epoch与credentialBindingHash。Receipt验签前禁止启动子进程；之后仅可spawn清单入口，PID不是Policy先验字段。丢响应以完整RecoveryInvocation find。

`unmount` Request必须重新绑定原Mount Receipt、当前SessionOwnershipReceipt/epoch、当前phase/continuation Authorization、精确mount/sandbox/namespace、独立或refresh后仍有效的`unmountCredentialGrantBinding{scope.exactActions=[unmount,findReceipt]}`、unmount Token与Deadline；Adapter调用前重新introspect完整Binding，禁止复用已过期mount Grant或只凭Receipt获得能力。Unmount Receipt绑定新credentialBindingHash、原Mount Receipt、Namespace销毁与mount/sandbox zero；Prepared/ForwardPrepared绑定Mount+Unmount Receipt及authorityMountZero。mountId/mountRealpath/sandboxId/namespace是Session Resource Key，不能跨run复用。

`execution/ExecutorIsolationControl.ts`是接管前隔离旧执行器的窄Port，固定`isolate(request,token)/findReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；唯一`ProviderExecutorIsolationControl.ts`按`purpose=executor-isolation`最小Authorization终止精确oldExecutorInstance/process set、撤销其Mount并阻止再次取得Lease。Request绑定current Session state/version/oldFencingEpoch、resourceKeys/hash、providerRef、`executorIsolationCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapIsolation}`、`isolationBrokerCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapIsolation}`、verifyRef/schema、deadline、稳定Token/action=isolate，以及闭Union `grantIsolation=activeSet{credentialGrantSetRef+hash+sortedMembers}|noActiveSet{lastRetiredGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull,currentOwnershipHash}`；两份Binding不可复用且外层scope字段逐字节全等。Adapter先调用可恢复`CredentialBroker.isolateGrantSet`取得GrantSetIsolationReceipt，再以同Token派生的providerToken终止旧process/Mount；任一步丢响应都只能从对应find恢复。activeSet分支逐成员使用授权已确定的revocationToken；noActiveSet分支仍bump旧generation、取得prospectiveExecutor generation、终止旧process/Mount并证明当前Ownership没有active Set，禁止把retired Set冒充active。最终ExecutorIsolation Receipt绑定oldEpoch、old/new isolation generation、prospectiveRevocationGeneration、输入Union、完整GrantSetIsolationReceipt、逐项terminated/revoked或noActiveSet事实、GrantTransitionOutcome、effectiveAt、token、两份credentialBindingHash和Provider签名，不预猜尚未产生的newEpoch。ToolLauncher是唯一调用者；随后takeover-claim完整内联并验该Receipt，`reserveTakeover`原子持久化其Hash和prospectiveRevocationGeneration后才产生newFencingEpoch，attachTakeover后的首个业务Journal Checkpoint再绑定Isolation Receipt；无Receipt或任一隔离断言未完成禁止接管。

`execution/DeploymentControl.ts`是窄Port，定义`deploy(request,token)/findReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/rollback(request,token)/findRollbackReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/runProbe(request,token)/findProbeReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；Deploy Request Source是封闭Union：`initial={CutoverPrepared Pair,phase=verify|activate,preparedManifestRef,preparedArtifactBlobRef+hash,preparedArtifactEvidenceRef}`、`forwardRepair={CutoverForwardPrepared Pair,postcommitRepairAuthorizationRef,ActivationCommitReceipt,trafficGuardHeld,forwardManifestRef,forwardArtifactBlobRef+hash,forwardArtifactEvidenceRef}`或`configurationRepair={postcommitRepairAuthorizationRef,ActivationCommitReceipt,trafficGuardHeld,currentVerifiedArtifact+deploymentTargetMap,versionedConfigRef+hash+schema,configTargetRef}`。三支都精确绑定targetRef、动作集合、幂等Token、Fencing Epoch、动作独立`deploymentCredentialGrantBinding`、Verify Ref/Receipt Schema和Deadline/Abort；前两支另绑定component set与migrationMode，第三支只允许把签名版本配置原子应用到`deploymentPolicy.configTargetRefs[]`且已在Resource Registry占有的精确target，不允许任意route操作。Decoder拒绝三类字段交叉；verify只可initial+canary+isolated，初始生产dark deploy只可initial+none，提交后向前部署只可forwardRepair+none/授权migration策略。前两支调用前通过EvidenceStore完整Get对应`ReleaseArtifact` Envelope，确认其指向该分支授权Blob，再以`getRaw`复算hash/size/mediaType并与对应Manifest相等；Adapter只能把该内容地址交给Provider。第三支要求Provider对versionedConfig内容Hash、旧/新配置版本和精确target做CAS。三支Receipt都绑定不可变deployment/config version、artifact或config Hash、targetHash、token、epoch、credentialBindingHash与Provider签名。`rollback(request,token)`只接受restore Authorization、原initial Source、精确Artifact/target/已验Deploy Receipt、current epoch、具体`rollbackCredentialGrantBinding`、Verify Ref/Schema与Deadline；`findRollbackReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`是Crash恢复唯一事实源。`runProbe`只执行授权Artifact中的`SmokeMain`一次性可变探针，Request绑定隔离Namespace、write+cleanup Token、`probeCredentialGrantBinding`、epoch与预期zero-after-cleanup；Receipt必须证明每个写入已对账清零并绑定credentialBindingHash，丢响应必须用完整RecoveryInvocation调用`findProbeReceipt`。`ProviderDeploymentControl.ts`是唯一Adapter并逐来源验证部署/配置/回退/Probe Receipt；所有find都必须携RecoveryInvocation向Provider读取，不能只查本地缓存。

`ReleaseProbe.ts`自身严格只读，Source为封闭Union：`initial={CutoverPrepared Pair,phase=verify|activate}`、`forwardRepair={CutoverForwardPrepared Pair,repairAuthorization,ActivationCommitReceipt,trafficHeld}`或`postcommitCurrentDeployment={repairAuthorization,planKind=databaseRepair|configurationTrafficRepair,ActivationCommitReceipt,trafficHeld,currentVerifiedArtifact,currentDeploymentTargetMap,本轮Migration或Configuration Receipt}`。它不导入targetShop源码，只按该Source签名Probe Catalog对已部署HTTPS实例执行health/startup、Contract/Migration/Catalog Hash、核心只读Query、reconciliation和SLI；需要最小幂等写时只能先WAL记录`providerIntent(providerAction=runProbe)`，再调用DeploymentControl.runProbe，不能在ReleaseProbe内部直接写。初始输出内联CutoverVerified，三种repair输出内联CutoverRepairAttempt或CutoverActivated；deploy/rollback/runProbe及各find Receipt不得跨来源、跨Token、跨Artifact或跨Config。

`execution/MigrationControl.ts`是生产数据切换窄Port，定义`snapshot(request,token)/findSnapshot(recoveryInvocation)/migrate(request,token)/findMigrationReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/repair(request,token)/findRepairReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/reconcile(request,token)/findReconcileReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/destroyCanary(request,token)/findDestroyCanaryReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；每个Mutation Request都显式绑定当前Authorization Ref+Hash、SessionOwnershipReceipt+epoch及动作独立`migrationCredentialGrantBinding`，每个Receipt回写credentialBindingHash。reconcile Request另绑定已验migrate或repair Receipt及Owner Catalog，因而长任务轮换后可使用新的reconcileCredentialGrantBinding，禁止receipt-only或ambient Credential调用。唯一`ProviderMigrationControl.ts`验证Snapshot/PITR、Migration、Repair、Reconcile与Canary Destroy Receipt。Verify不得获得productionDatabase Binding，只能在runId派生且已占Key的canary namespace执行MigrationMain，并在finally以独立destroyCredentialGrantBinding/Token销毁、验namespaceZero后产CutoverVerified。Activate才可在生产库创建PITR、执行固定Plan并逐Owner对账；repair只在提交点后、Traffic held时消费postcommit-repair Authorization。所有Mutation先WAL intent并以Token+epoch执行；各find使用完整RecoveryInvocation，匹配补completed、SignedAbsent才同Token重试。禁止本地执行标志、自动Hook或DeploymentControl暗跑Migration。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/CutoverSessionStore.ts`定义外部线性会话与全局资源栅栏Port。每个Mutation首参是已验签`SessionInvocation={authorizationRef,authorizationHash,authorizationNonce,invocationAuthority,action,actionInstanceId,canonicalParamsHash,stableToken,attestedLauncherHash}`；`invocationAuthority`是闭Union：`bootstrap={sessionCredentialGrantBinding.scope=bootstrapReserve|bootstrapTakeover}`只给reserve/reserveTakeover；`ticketAttach={ticketRef+hash,ticketCapabilityHash,sessionCredentialGrantBinding.scope=exactControl.binding=attach|attachTakeover,pendingGrantSetRef+hash,handoffProof=none|normalPhaseHandoff{previousCompletionReceiptRef+hash,previousCompletionOwnershipHash,nextTransitionCapabilityHash,nextPhase,nextPhaseIntentHash}}`只给attach/attachTakeover，Binding使用Ticket中的权威generation但不属于尚未active的pending Set；普通attach及非normal takeover必须handoffProof=none，同名Payload必须选择normalPhaseHandoff且所有Proof字段逐字节全等；`phaseHandoff={previousCompletionReceiptRef+hash,currentOwnershipRef+hash,nextTransitionCapabilityHash,sessionCredentialGrantBinding.scope=exactControl.binding=phaseHandoff,pendingGrantSetRef+hash}`只给claim，或在当前active Set为空时给beginIntermediate/beginForward；`owned={currentOwnershipRef+hash,sessionCredentialGrantBinding,activeGrantSetRef+hash,membershipProof}`给renew/refresh/commit/Forward Ledger/complete，以及当前active Set尚存在时的beginIntermediate/beginForward，business或exactControl Binding必须属于**调用前**current active Set；`expiry={ticketRef+hash,sessionCredentialGrantBinding.scope=exactControl.binding=expireReservation|expireTakeoverTicket}`只给两种过期。Store按判别支、Binding scope、旧active Set、Completion Capability或Ticket Capability与action逐项introspect，禁止用pending Set自举、只传Hash或ambient
