Grant。Port固定为：`read(stateReadInvocation,runId,readToken)/findStateReadReceipt(stateReadRecoveryInvocation)/findReceipt(sessionRecoveryInvocation)/expireReservation(expirationInvocation,reservationTicket)/reserve(resourceClaimInvocation,runId,resourceKeys[],resourceSetHash,lease)/attach(phaseInvocation,reservationTicket,pendingGrantSetRef,pendingGrantSetHash,expectedState,nextState)/claim(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,expectedState,nextState,pendingGrantSetRef,pendingGrantSetHash,resourceKeys[],resourceSetHash,lease)/renew(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,expectedFencingEpoch,lease)/refreshAuthorization(refreshInvocation,ownershipHandle,currentAuthorizationRef,currentAuthorizationHash,replacementAuthorizationRef,replacementAuthorizationHash,replacementAuthorizationIntentHash,immutableActionSetHash,oldGrantSetRef,oldGrantSetHash,newGrantSetRef,newGrantSetHash,refreshGrantMembershipProof)/expireTakeoverTicket(takeoverExpiryInvocation,currentOwnershipHandle,expiredTakeoverTicket)/reserveTakeover(takeoverInvocation,runningVersion,oldFencingEpoch,executorFenceReceipt,intendedNextWorkPayload,lease)/attachTakeover(phaseInvocation,takeoverTicket,pendingGrantSetRef,pendingGrantSetHash,nextWorkPayload)/beginIntermediate(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,state=Activating,expectedWork,nextWork,pendingGrantSetRef,pendingGrantSetHash,intermediateContext,lease)/beginForward(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,forwardId,pendingGrantSetRef,pendingGrantSetHash,candidateRealpathHash,lease)/commitPoint(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,point=activationCommitted,payloadHash)/reserveForward(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,forwardReservationAssertion)/commitForwardSwap(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,forwardReservationReceipt,rootSwapAttestation)/releaseForward(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,forwardReservationReceipt,forwardReleaseAssertion)/completeIntermediate(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,completionAssertion,completionKind=CutoverForwardPrepared|CutoverForwardCleaned|CutoverRepairAttempt,expectedWork,nextWork,intermediateContext,forwardReleaseReceiptOrNull)/complete(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,completionAssertion,nextState)/completeAndRelease(phaseInvocation,ownershipHandle,completionAssertion,nextState=Restored|Finalized,resourceKeys[],resourceSetHash)`。`StateReadRecoveryInvocation={stateReadAuthorizationRef+hash,stateReadInvocationHash,sessionStateReadCredentialGrantBinding,runId,readToken,attestedLauncherHash}`只可调用findStateReadReceipt；`SessionRecoveryInvocation=bootstrap|authorized`则只用于Mutation find，bootstrap只准reserve/reserveTakeover的同action+token find并返回整包或Absent，authorized只包一份control-recovery。attach类CAS验证一次性Ticket Capability并同时激活pending Set；phaseHandoff消费前一Completion的nextTransitionCapability并同时换Set；normalPhaseHandoff takeover必须在同一CAS再消费Completion Capability；owned begin消费调用前active Set内的控制Grant。四者都绝不能用new Set Grant授权自己。refresh必须完整Get/验replacement Authorization并证明其intent/policy/state/work/epoch/actionSet与new Set全等，再把Authorization Ref+Hash和Set同CAS安装。expireTakeoverTicket只接受调用前takeoverTicketPending、精确旧Ticket及独立token，由Provider权威时钟重验后原子void pending Set并写takeoverPendingIsolated；之后Issuer才能基于新Ownership Receipt签reserve型takeover-claim。两动作的actionInstanceId/token不可复用。

Issuer先以不含未来Authorization Hash的`authorizationIntentHash/immutableActionSetHash`创建不可变pending GrantSet。每个binding使用同一Session/Grant Registry事务内的唯一compare-and-put索引：seal请求必须携`binding ticket、completion或ownership Ref+hash/expectedVersion/expectedSessionHash/expectedExpiry`，CAS再次证明binding仍live、未消费且未过期；同binding与同Canonical Set幂等返回，同binding与不同Set或已expire/replace/transition冲突并撤销本次全部新签Grants、产可find的清理Receipt。expire/replacement对同一索引做互斥CAS，因此`pendingSetSignedAbsent`之后绝不可能再seal，`getPendingGrantSetByBinding`至多返回一份且void可穷尽能力。binding闭Union取initialAttach/reservationTicket、takeoverAttach/takeoverTicket、normalPhaseHandoff/{takeoverTicket+latestCompletion+nextTransitionCapabilityHash}、phaseHandoff/latestCompletion、ownedBegin/currentOwnership或refresh/oldSet；Provider introspect在Session没有匹配GrantSetTransitionSetReceipt前拒绝全部业务动作。Session Store、Grant Registry与Transition Registry由同一线性Provider/Authority实现；attach/claim/attachTakeover/beginIntermediate/beginForward/refreshAuthorization在一个强一致事务中核对Authorization↔Set↔Ticket/Completion/Ownership，并通过一次`transitionGrantSets`执行pending→active及存在时previous active→retired。attach只消费Ticket的一次性attach Capability；claim与Completion后的begin只消费上一Ownership Receipt的一次性nextTransitionCapability；normalPhaseHandoff attachTakeover同时消费Ticket与上一Completion两份Capability；当前active Set仍存在的begin使用old Set内控制Grant。任何分支都不用尚未active的新Session Grant自举。

每次CAS只持久化并返回一份Provider签名`SessionMutationResultReceipt={schemaVersion,cutoverRunId,invocationHash,authorizationRef+hash+nonce,sessionCredentialGrantBinding,grantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull,attestedLauncherHash,actualVersion,actualFencingEpoch,action,actionInstanceId,canonicalParamsHash,stableToken,result,providerIdentity,signature}`；Store逐字节回写Binding并以其credentialBindingHash参与签名，以(action,stableToken)唯一索引保存整包；同参重放或findReceipt只返回整包或以同invocationHash签名的Absent，domain、Ownership与GrantTransition不得拆开恢复。`result`闭Union为`reservation{reservationReceipt,ownershipReceipt=null,grantTransitions=none{reason=actionHasNoSetTransition,noSetProofOrNull=null}}|expiredReservation{expiryReceipt,ownershipReceipt=null,grantTransitions=none{reason=pendingSetAbsent,noSetProofOrNull=pendingSetSignedAbsent}|committed{receipt.transitionKind=void}}|owned{domainReceiptOrNull,ownershipReceipt,grantTransitions=none{reason=actionHasNoSetTransition|activeSetAbsent|pendingSetAbsent,noSetProofOrNull}|committed{receipt}}`。expireTakeoverTicket固定返回owned，domainReceipt是TakeoverExpiryReceipt、ownershipStatus是takeoverPendingIsolated，Grant outcome按pendingSetAbsent|void；绝不复用expiredReservation。普通无转换动作只能用actionHasNoSetTransition+null；reserveTakeover若Isolation证明noActiveSet可用activeSetAbsent。动作闭表固定：reserve/renew/commitPoint/reserveForward/commitForwardSwap/releaseForward为actionHasNoSetTransition；reserveTakeover为actionHasNoSetTransition或activeSetAbsent；expireTakeoverTicket为pendingSetAbsent或void；attach/claim/attachTakeover/beginIntermediate/beginForward/refreshAuthorization为activateAndRetire；complete/completeIntermediate/completeAndRelease为retire；expireReservation为void或pendingSetAbsent。Receipt binding.action必须使用真实Port名并与actionInstanceId/paramsHash全等。Isolation已完成generation bump/revoke，reserveTakeover只绑定其Receipt。所有domain Receipt与最新Ownership互相绑定；commitPoint也返回完整整包。

每次owned成功都返回链式Provider签名`SessionOwnershipReceipt={runId,state,activationWorkOrNull,currentAuthorizationRef,currentAuthorizationHash,currentAuthorizationIntentHash,ownershipStatus,version,sessionHash,leaseGeneration,leaseExpiresAt,serverObservedAt,fencingEpoch,ownerExecutor,previousOwnershipHash,action,actionInstanceId,paramsHash,stableToken,activeGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull+generation,lastRetiredGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull,nextTransitionCapability}`。`ownershipStatus`是Canonical闭Union：`attached={kind=attached}`；`takeoverTicketPending={kind,ticketRef,ticketHash,expiresAt,prospectiveExecutor,prospectiveRevocationGeneration,newEpoch,ticketGeneration,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,isolationReceiptHash}`；`takeoverPendingIsolated={kind,expiredTicketRef,expiredTicketHash,expiredAt,isolatedExecutor,isolatedExecutorRevocationGeneration,oldEpoch,ticketGeneration,isolationReceiptHash}`。`nextTransitionCapability={allowedTransitions[],expectedState,expectedWorkOrNull,expectedVersion,expectedSessionHash,subjectExecutor,expiresAt,generation,singleUseNonce}`。Ownership Receipt与State Projection复用同一ownershipStatus及currentAuthorization Decoder，禁止只传enum、只传Authorization Hash或另存Ticket细节。attach/claim/beginIntermediate/beginForward/refreshAuthorization原子安装新Authorization+Set并退休上一Set；complete类退休本相位Set但保留最后currentAuthorization事实供恢复审计。retired Set在原TTL内只保留对已经执行的exact action+token调用findReceipt的能力；任何新Mutation拒绝。Provider introspect对business Grant必须证明它属于当前Ownership唯一active Set且state/work/auth/epoch全等；后续Mutation只接受最新ownershipHandle并用version/hash/leaseGeneration作CAS，陈旧Handle拒绝。

`refreshAuthorization`只允许同一健康owner在`credentialRefreshBefore`之前、同一state/work/epoch/immutableActionSetHash下原子替换Authorization与Grant Set，不执行业务副作用或改变路径、Target、Token。`SessionEvidenceAssertion`由ToolLauncher在使用exact-subject Core Reader完整Get并验证Evidence Core+ExecutorRunReceipt Pair后签出，闭分支为`forwardReservation|forwardRelease|phaseCompletion`，共同绑定Authorization、Pair Ref+Hash、decoded subject/kind、exit=0、postcheck、intermediateContext、resourceSet、version、epoch及launcherAttestationHash；ForwardReservation另绑定prepared Ledger identity，ForwardRelease另绑定no-root-intent/inode Proof，PhaseCompletion绑定from/to State/Work。Session Store用Policy固定Launcher Verify Key验证Assertion，不相信opaque EvidenceRef。`RootSwapAttestation`由当前Cutover Core Signer按独立purpose签完整Canonical RootSwapResult与WAL Proof；`intermediateContext={attemptId,parentPairRefOrNull,forwardIdOrNull,reasonOrNull,paramsHash}`由Authorization与business Grant精确固定。唯一`ObjectCutoverSessionStore.ts`用对象版本/ETag条件写并验证Provider原签名Receipt，禁止本地锁或Evidence List代替。

每个Mutation都要求`action+actionInstanceId+stableToken+canonicalParamsHash`；同实例重放只返回原Provider Receipt，token复用于不同参数/action或同参数换token重试均拒绝。`read`只接受`scope=sessionStateRead`的exact-run Grant+Hash及readToken，不要求预知将被读取的state/version/epoch；它返回可由findStateReadReceipt恢复的Provider签名最小Projection Receipt，Payload为闭Union：`unowned={runId,state=Empty,reservationStatus=none|pending,reservationTicketRefOrNull+hashOrNull,reservationExpiresAtOrNull,resourceSetHashOrNull,serverObservedAt}`或`owned={runId,state,workOrNull,currentAuthorizationRef,currentAuthorizationHash,currentAuthorizationIntentHash,version,sessionHash,leaseGeneration,leaseExpiresAt,serverObservedAt,epoch,ownershipStatus,resourceSetHash,activeGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull+generation,lastRetiredGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull}`，其中owned复用上一段完整ownershipStatus Union。Empty不伪造Lease/epoch/ownership字段，跨分支字段拒绝；两支都无List、Mutation或其他run字段。`expireReservation`只接受签名Ticket，由Session Provider在同一CAS内用权威时钟重验`serverObservedAt≥ticket.expiresAt`，随后按上述none/void分支释放Keys、回Empty并返回可find的Expiry Receipt；不接受外部自报或未定义的expiry observation。

`nextWorkPayload`是封闭Union：`continuation={expectedState=Preparing|Applying|Verifying|Restoring|Activating,expectedWorkOrNull}`、`restore={expectedState=Preparing|Prepared|Applying|Applied|Verifying|Verified|Activating,nextState=Restoring,predecessor}`、`forwardBuild={expectedState=Activating,forwardId,buildAuthorizationHash,candidateRealpathHash}`、`forwardCleanup={expectedState=Activating,forwardId,reason,expectedLedgerStatus}`、`repair={expectedState=Activating,attemptId,parentAttemptPair,forwardReservationOrNull,planKind=databaseRepair|deploymentForwardPatch|configurationTrafficRepair,repairPlanHash,immutableActionSetIntentHash}`、`resumeIntermediate={expectedState=Activating,currentWork=forwardPreparing|forwardCleaning|repairing,forwardIdOrAttemptId,originalAuthorizationRef+hash,intermediateContextHash,terminalWalHash,expectedLedgerStatusOrNull}`、`finalizeContinuation={expectedState=Finalizing}`、`normalPhaseHandoff={previousCompletionReceiptRef+hash,previousCompletionOwnershipHash,nextTransitionCapabilityHash,expectedState,nextPhase,nextState,nextWorkOrNull,nextPhaseIntentHash}`。normalPhaseHandoff的边闭表恰好为`Prepared/apply/Applying/null`、`Applied/verify/Verifying/null`、`Verified/activate/Activating/continuing`、`Activated/finalize/Finalizing/null`；`nextPhaseIntentHash=sha256(JCS({cutoverRunId,previousCompletionReceiptHash,nextPhase,expectedState,nextState,nextWorkOrNull,resourceSetHash,immutableActionSetHash,policyHashSet,pathsHash,targetsHash,tokensHash,prospectiveExecutor}))`，显式排除尚未产生的Ticket、新epoch、最终Grant Ref和Authorization Ref，消除Hash自环。reserve型takeover-claim、bootstrapTakeover Grant、`reserveTakeover` paramsHash、Ticket Capability与后续Authorization必须逐字节携同一intendedNextWorkPayload；attachTakeover逐字段验证，禁止在同attempt/Ticket下换planKind、Plan、Action Set、下一phase或Journal prefix。reserveTakeover只从attached或takeoverPendingIsolated进入完整takeoverTicketPending Union并返回新Handle；attachTakeover消费该Ticket回attached。Ticket过期时先用expire型takeover-claim调用`expireTakeoverTicket`，它绑定调用前完整takeoverTicketPending、由Provider时钟重验并按none/void处理pending Set，原子输出完整takeoverPendingIsolated；只有读取到该新Ownership Receipt后，Issuer才能签下一份reserve型takeover-claim，不预绑未来状态。

attachTakeover按Payload形成互斥的三种`takeoverMode`，不再把所有“顶层state相同”误称为业务同态转换。`positionResume`只换owner/epoch/GrantSet、不改变业务位置：continuation恰好保持`Preparing|Applying|Verifying|Restoring`的state与null work，或保持`Activating/continuing`；finalizeContinuation恰好保持`Finalizing/null`；resumeIntermediate恰好保持`Activating/forwardPreparing|forwardCleaning|repairing`，并额外验证原Authorization、intermediateContext、terminalWalHash及Ledger逐字节全等，不重建Ledger、不重放begin。`domainTransition`只给restore、forwardBuild、forwardCleanup、repair并严格执行各自闭表中的state/work变化。`normalPhaseHandoff`只执行上一段四条完成边，并额外消费Completion Capability。任何其他attachTakeover自环、任何`beginForward|beginIntermediate`自环、用continuation跨state/work、或用normalPhaseHandoff恢复未完成WAL都拒绝；resume后只能执行原工作剩余WAL动作并走既有complete/completeIntermediate边。

原executor的`beginForward`与forwardBuild attach执行等价CAS，在进入forwardPreparing时创建`ledger[forwardId]={status:building,buildAuthorizationHash,candidateRealpathHash}`；ForwardPrepared complete只允许`building→prepared`，failedBuild cleanup只允许`building→cleanedBy`，unused/superseded cleanup只允许`prepared→cleanedBy`。三项Forward CAS都返回Provider原签名复合Receipt：reserve只允许`prepared→reservedByRepair`；commit完整验证reservation、RootSwapAttestation中的RootSwapResult、WAL Hash Chain及Signer，执行`reservedByRepair→consumedByCompletedRootSwap=<ForwardCommitReceiptHash>`；release完整验证ForwardReleaseAssertion中的CutoverRepairAttempt Pair及no-root-intent/inode Proof，执行`reservedByRepair→prepared`。RepairAttempt complete不再修改Ledger；有pre-swap reservation时必须先release并传入ForwardReleaseReceipt。Crash丢响应必须findReceipt/read后以同Token恢复。

`resourceKeys[]`按`type + normalizedValue` UTF-8排序去重并穷尽targetShopRealpath、canonicalRemoteRef、productionDatabaseRef、每个gateway/scheduler/consumer、canary/production target、由runId派生的canaryDatabaseNamespace、全部configTargetRefs与versionedConfigRegistryNamespace、Quarantine namespace、activation/forward Candidate namespace、Authority Mount namespace/mountId/mountRealpath/sandboxId、独立sessionJournalRealpath、六个Source Root realpath、retiredSourceSet及非keptShared GitMetadata namespace；repair目标必须逐字节等于已占Key。`resourceSetHash`只作完整性校验。Store用一个强一致Resource Registry对象的ETag CAS同时检查并占有全部Key，冲突零写入；Receipt绑定完整Keys/Hash、每Key前后owner/version与epoch，部分交集同样拒绝。

reserve只消费`scope=bootstrapReserve`的最小Resource Claim Grant，精确绑定run、完整resourceSetHash、nonce、action=reserve、reserveStableToken和Attested Launcher，明确不携尚未生成的fencingEpoch。Session Provider在占有全部Key的同一事务中对`executorIdentity`的Generation Registry执行create-if-absent=0或读取既有单调值，并返回签名`reservationTicket/fencingEpoch/revocationGeneration/expiresAt/credentialBindingHash/attachCapability={allowedTransition=Empty→Preparing,expectedResourceSetHash,subjectExecutor,expiry,revocationGeneration,singleUseNonce}`；Issuer只能消费Ticket中的权威generation签business/sessionStateRead/exactControl Grants与pending Set，禁止猜0或自报。初始Reservation过期且从未attach、没有副作用时，Provider以权威时钟在同一CAS内重验expiry，并按唯一binding的`pendingSetSignedAbsent|voidReceipt`闭分支释放Keys、回Empty；nonce永久消费，但Generation Registry保留且只可由Isolation单调bump。

Takeover的reserve型最小授权绑定旧Session/version/epoch、Resource Keys、Attested Launcher、已验Executor Isolation Receipt、`grantIsolation=activeSet|noActiveSet`及强类型intendedNextWorkPayload，并携bootstrapTakeover CredentialGrantBinding。`reserveTakeover`把Payload Hash纳入paramsHash，CAS验证Isolation Receipt中的prospectiveRevocationGeneration，随后返回`takeoverTicket/newFencingEpoch/attachCapability={allowedCurrentState+work,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,expectedVersion+sessionHash,subjectExecutor,expiry,ticketGeneration,singleUseNonce}`；Issuer只能据此签对应epoch/generation的continuation/restore/forward/repair或normalPhaseHandoff下一phase Authorization，attachTakeover要求Payload全等。normalPhaseHandoff时Issuer还必须完整Get上一Completion整包Receipt，验证其Pair、current Ownership、activeSetAbsent、未消费Capability与四边闭表，再把Ticket、Completion、Capability、nextPhaseIntentHash共同写入下一phase Authorization、attachTakeover exactControl Binding和pending Set；任何一个Ref/Hash都不得由Launcher补写。Ticket过期时另签expire型takeover-claim：它只绑定调用前完整takeoverTicketPending、旧Ticket与`scope.exactControl.binding=expireTakeoverTicket`，调用同名Port后才得到takeoverPendingIsolated Ownership Receipt；不恢复oldExecutor/oldEpoch、不释放run资源。下一轮reserve型takeover-claim必须绑定这个已存在的孤立Receipt、递增ticketGeneration、新Isolation Receipt与新Payload。后续claim/renew沿用当前epoch；所有KMS Grant Scope、WAL、Provider Request与Core/Receipt均绑定当前epoch，Provider拒绝旧epoch新Mutation。

状态图固定为`Empty→Preparing→Prepared→Applying→Applied→Verifying→Verified→Activating→Activated→Finalizing→Finalized`，非破坏取消/回滚分支允许`Preparing|Prepared|Applying|Applied|Verifying|Verified→Restoring→Restored`，以及守卫`state=Activating && activationCommitted不存在 && openNew Receipt不存在`成立时`Activating→Restoring`；`ActivatingBeforeCommit`不是状态值。四条正常完成边`Prepared→Applying→Applied→Verifying→Verified→Activating→Activated→Finalizing`的每个“下一相位启动”都恰有两个互斥入口：原Executor活着时用phaseHandoff claim；原Executor已死亡时用normalPhaseHandoff attachTakeover。二者在同一Ownership ETag和同一Completion Capability上竞争且至多一个成功。`Activating`另有不改变顶层状态的封闭`activationWork=continuing|forwardPreparing|forwardReady|forwardCleaning|repairing|repairCheckpoint`。合法begin闭表为：`continuing|repairCheckpoint→beginForward/attachTakeover(forwardBuild)→forwardPreparing`并同CAS创建building Ledger；`forwardPreparing|forwardReady|repairCheckpoint→beginIntermediate/attachTakeover(forwardCleanup)→forwardCleaning`；`continuing|forwardReady|repairCheckpoint→beginIntermediate/attachTakeover(repair)→repairing`。只有`completeIntermediate`可执行工作完成：`forwardPreparing + CutoverForwardPrepared→forwardReady`、`forwardCleaning + CutoverForwardCleaned→repairCheckpoint`、`repairing + CutoverRepairAttempt→repairCheckpoint`；Receipt必须绑定from/to Work、完整intermediateContext、Core/RunReceipt、可选ForwardReleaseReceipt、version和epoch，其他业务工作转换全部拒绝；上一段明确列出的positionResume只改变Ownership，不算业务工作转换。`commitPoint`只能在持有Activating Ownership、expected version/hash/epoch全等且point尚不存在时CAS写入，返回带新version、payloadHash、providerVerifyRef的`ActivationCommitReceipt`。`activationCommitted`之后Store拒绝任何Restoring、Verified回退或abandon，只允许原activate invocation在Lease和原具体Credential仍有效时继续，或由`phase-continuation(continuationKind=activate|forwardBuild|forwardCleanup|repair)`推进到Activated及之后的finalize。长任务必须在`renewBefore`前续租，并在每个WAL intent、Provider/KMS/Store写前重读/续租；失败立即停止且不得先执行副作用。文件系统不能原生识别epoch，因此takeover前必须由Executor Isolation Provider终止旧进程、撤销旧Mount/Credential并返回已验签`executorFenceReceipt(oldInstance,resourceSetHash,oldEpoch,isolationGeneration)`；它只证明旧epoch和旧执行器已隔离，不预猜随后由Session Store CAS产生的newEpoch。无Receipt禁止新执行器触碰WAL或磁盘。幂等身份与并发栅栏分开：stableToken按签名v1公式和actionInstanceId派生，请求另带当前`fencingEpoch`；Provider对同Token+同Action+同paramsHash返回既有事实、允许新epoch续跑未完成动作，只拒绝旧epoch的新Mutation，`findReceipt(action,stableToken)`始终可读历史Receipt。每个runId只有一个Session owner，每组Resource Keys只有一个跨run owner。ToolLauncher在任何副作用前必须先取得并完整验证`attach|claim|attachTakeover`之一的SessionMutationResultReceipt；Core与ExecutorRunReceipt绑定该整包Receipt/version/epoch，Launcher发布Receipt后再complete或completeIntermediate。Restored/Finalized必须调用`completeAndRelease`，在同一个强一致Registry ETag CAS中同时写终态Completion Receipt并释放全部Key；Crash丢响应时以同一Token读取相同Receipt，不存在“已终态但未释放”的窗口。

授权`expiresAt`只控制首次Session attach/claim/attachTakeover，已取得Ownership后的每次Store/Signer/Provider调用仍必须使用未过期短时Grant。`CutoverAuthorization.ts`的`purpose=phase-continuation`绑定原Authorization Ref+Hash与purpose/operation、immutableActionSetHash、当前running state/work/version/fencingEpoch、`currentSessionOwnershipReceiptRef+hash`、resourceSetHash、WAL prefix/terminal Hash、同一ToolBundle/entry/node/npm和新executorInstance。健康owner的凭据轮换只走独立最小`purpose=credential-refresh`及refreshAuthorization，不属于phase-continuation；执行器接管只在两步Takeover Ticket完成后，按原Policy Issuer/Scope Template/TTL轮换Receipt/Evidence/Signer/Reader及Provider Grants并重新绑定当前epoch，不改变Scope模板、路径、Entry、OID、Target、Policy Hash、Token或动作。Lease过期/执行器丢失后：Preparing只能continuation或用restore Payload进入prepareAttempt Restore，Prepared只能normalPhaseHandoff进入apply或用restore Payload执行preparedCancel；Applying/Verifying可continuation或restore；Applied/Verified只能normalPhaseHandoff进入正常下一phase或用restore Payload；Restoring只允许positionResume continuation；Activating在提交点前可positionResume `continuationKind=activate`、resumeIntermediate或严格restore Payload进入activateAttempt restore，提交后白名单恰好是`purpose=credential-refresh|phase-continuation(continuationKind=activate|forwardBuild|forwardCleanup|repair)|postcommit-forward-prepare(operation=build|cleanup)|postcommit-repair`；Activated只能normalPhaseHandoff进入finalize；Finalizing只能`purpose=credential-refresh|phase-continuation(continuationKind=finalize)`并以positionResume接管。attachTakeover必须写入与Ticket Payload匹配的state/activationWork。Session Store拒绝Activated后回到Verified/Restoring；旧epoch Grants由Isolation Provider撤销，长期恢复不依赖过期原Grant也不扩大权限。

`tests/recovery/CutoverSession.vitest.ts`必须以Issuer、Decoder、内存线性Store和Provider签名Fake做同一份表驱动/性质测试，不允许只测Happy Path。正例穷尽：(1) `Prepared/Applied/Verified/Activated`各自在Completion后杀死Executor，证明noActiveSet Isolation、normalPhaseHandoff reserve/attach、两个Capability同CAS消费、正确下一phase Set激活；(2) `Preparing/Applying/Verifying`以及before-commit guard成立的`Activating`以restore attachTakeover进入Restoring，`Prepared/Applied/Verified`分别覆盖活Executor phaseHandoff claim和死Executor restore takeover；(3) continuation、finalizeContinuation与三种resumeIntermediate只换Ownership并保持规定state/work；(4) 在Isolation响应丢失、reserveTakeover提交后丢响应、pending Set seal后崩溃、attachTakeover提交后丢响应、Completion成功但下一Authorization尚未签发五个Crash Point恢复，find必须返回同一Receipt且最终恰好一个active Set。负例穷尽：Running→Restoring调用claim、normalPhaseHandoff从非四个边界、nextPhase/Intent/Completion/Capability/Ticket/Set/epoch/generation任一错配、Completion Capability重复消费、continuation改变state/work、begin自环、positionResume带Completion字段、normalPhaseHandoff缺任一Completion字段、restore伪装normal phase、旧Executor在Isolation后claim、旧claim与takeover并发双赢。性质断言固定为任意操作前缀后`owners≤1 ∧ activeSets≤1 ∧ consumedCapabilities不复活 ∧ activeSet.authorization=ownership.authorization ∧ 非终态要么有live owner/active Set，要么存在唯一可恢复Ticket/Completion Capability路径`；模型遍历全部state/work/payload/method笛卡尔积，未列入闭表的组合必须fail closed。

同一测试还必须直接对Canonical `exactControl.binding`总Decoder做schema矩阵：phaseHandoff缺任一公共字段、action不为`claim|beginIntermediate|beginForward`、action与实际Port不等、claim选择非七条闭边、begin未绑定completeIntermediate或active Set非空都拒绝；合法phaseHandoff必须逐字节往返编码后`credentialBindingHash`稳定，且不得被attachTakeover、refresh、recovery或任何业务Provider Decoder接受。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/CutoverAuthorization.ts`定义阶段化Discriminated Union。`purpose=authorization-revision`是不可执行的业务授权修订分支，字段固定为`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/baseExecutionAuthorizationRef+hash/predecessorRevisionRefOrNull+hashOrNull/revisionNumber/replacementAuthorizationIntentHash/samePurpose+samePhaseOrNull+sameOperationOrNull+sameContinuationKindOrNull+samePlanKindOrNull/samePolicyHashes+paths+targets+tokens+immutableActionSetHash+state+work+epoch+resourceSetHash/replacement CredentialGrantBinding Set及Receipt/Evidence/Signer/Reader bindings/createdAt/expiresAt/signature/noNewCli=true/noAuthorizationReceiptConsumption=true`。Issuer按base purpose的判别支完整Get base与predecessor，要求phase/operation/continuationKind/planKind该出现者逐字节全等、不适用者严格null，且除具体短时Grant/expiry和revision identity外所有字段全等；该Ref只能作为credential-refresh的replacementAuthorizationRef被Session安装，不能启动ToolLauncher、消费Nonce或直接写Core。业务CLI执行时同时绑定baseExecutionAuthorization与currentRevision链，Decoder取当前Revision中的Grant但取base中的不可变权限。

六种最小控制面授权共同绑定`trustPolicyHash`、`attestedLauncher={executorToolBundleRef+hash,executorRunAttestationRef+hash,launcherEntryHash,nodeBinaryHash,executorInstanceId}`；涉及Session的六支还共同绑定`cutoverSessionPolicyHash`。Provider Receipt必须绑定这两个Policy Hash及Launcher结构Hash；各分支下文不再省略这些公共字段，`trust`等别名非法。

`purpose=resource-claim`以`operation=reserve|expire`闭合。reserve只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/sessionStore+providerVerify/resourceKeys[]+resourceSetHash/reservationLease/reserveStableToken/resourceReserveCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapReserve{cutoverRunId,resourceSetHash,nonce,action=reserve,token},fencingEpochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`，明确无fencingEpoch；expire只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/expiredReservationTicket+hash/resourceKeys+hash/expireStableToken/expirationCredentialGrantBinding{scope=exactControl{context=cutover{cutoverRunId,cutoverSessionPolicyHash},revocationGeneration,binding=expireReservation{action=expireReservation,ticketHash,token}}}/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`。Provider在CAS内以权威时钟重验Ticket expiry，不接收外部时间断言。

`purpose=executor-isolation`只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/currentSessionOwnershipReceipt+hash/currentState+activationWorkOrNull+version+sessionHash+oldFencingEpoch+ownershipStatus/resourceKeys+hash/oldExecutorInstance+prospectiveExecutor+processSetHash+mountSetHash/grantIsolation/attestedLauncher/executorIsolationProviderRef/executorIsolationCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapIsolation{cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,oldExecutor,prospectiveExecutor,grantIsolationHash,action=isolate,token=isolationProviderToken},fencingEpochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}/isolationBrokerCredentialGrantBinding{同scope但provider=CredentialBroker,token=isolationStableToken且独立Grant}/executorIsolationVerifyRef+receiptSchemaVersion+deadline+isolationStableToken+isolationProviderToken+generationBumpToken+按grantRef排序的revocationTokenPlan/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`；grantIsolation必须逐字节等于`activeSet{credentialGrantSetRef+hash+sortedMembers}|noActiveSet{lastRetiredGrantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull,currentOwnershipHash}`，其Canonical Hash就是两份scope中的grantIsolationHash；两个主Token、generationBumpToken及每个revocationToken必须按IsolationBrokerInvocation公式复算，immutableActionSetHash同时覆盖`isolateGrantSet(isolationStableToken)`与`isolate(isolationProviderToken)`，跨分支字段拒绝，Receipt原样绑定该Union、两份credentialBindingHash及prospectiveRevocationGeneration。

`purpose=takeover-claim`以`operation=expireTicket|reserve`形成闭Union，并继承公共trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash。expireTicket只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/currentSessionOwnershipReceipt+hash/ownershipStatus=完整takeoverTicketPending/expiredTakeoverTicket+hash/expireTakeoverStableToken/takeoverExpiryCredentialGrantBinding{scope=exactControl{context=cutover{cutoverRunId,cutoverSessionPolicyHash},revocationGeneration,binding=expireTakeoverTicket{action=expireTakeoverTicket,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,ticketHash,ticketGeneration,token}}}/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`，不含未来Isolation Receipt、Payload或新Ticket。reserve只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/currentState+activationWorkOrNull+version+sessionHash+oldFencingEpoch+ownershipStatus=attached|takeoverPendingIsolated/resourceKeys+hash/attestedLauncher/executorIsolationReceipt+hash+prospectiveRevocationGeneration/grantIsolation/intendedNextWorkPayload/prospectiveExecutor/takeoverLease/reserveTakeoverStableToken/takeoverReserveCredentialGrantBinding{scope=bootstrapTakeover{cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash,oldEpoch,isolationReceiptHash,intendedNextWorkPayloadHash,prospectiveExecutor,action=reserveTakeover,token},fencingEpochOrNull=null,revocationGenerationOrNull=null}/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`。reserve Grant不伪造generation；Session CAS验证Isolation Receipt中的prospectiveRevocationGeneration并把同一值写入Ticket/Ownership，禁止重新get-or-init或自报。Payload只能取`continuation|restore|forwardBuild|forwardCleanup|repair|resumeIntermediate|finalizeContinuation|normalPhaseHandoff`八分支且完整进入paramsHash与Capability；repair分支必须再绑定planKind、repairPlanHash与immutableActionSetIntentHash；normalPhaseHandoff必须再绑定上一Completion整包Ref/Hash、Capability Hash、四边之一和nextPhaseIntentHash；其他分支出现这些字段立即拒绝，八支字段不可混用。

`purpose=credential-refresh`只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/currentSessionOwnershipReceipt+hash/currentAuthorizationRef+hash/replacementAuthorizationRef+hash+replacementAuthorizationIntentHash/oldCredentialGrantSetRef+hash/newCredentialGrantSetRef+hash/refreshGrantMembershipProof/sameState+work+epoch+resourceSet+immutableActionSetHash/credentialRefreshCredentialGrantBinding{scope=exactControl{context=cutover{cutoverRunId,cutoverSessionPolicyHash},revocationGeneration,binding=refresh{action=refreshAuthorization,currentOwnershipHash,oldSetHash,newSetHash,memberProofHash,token}}}/refreshStableToken/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`。Store必须完整Get并验证replacement Authorization，证明其intent等于new Set的authorizationIntentHash且Policy/state/work/epoch/actionSet不变；memberProofHash由外层refreshGrantMembershipProof重算全等。成功的Ownership Receipt与State Projection共同持久化replacement Ref+Hash+intent，Crash后不靠调用方记忆。

`purpose=control-recovery`只允许一个已执行动作的find。闭映射表固定为：authorizationReceipt `consume→findConsumptionReceipt`；telemetry `observe→findObservationReceipt`；traffic `fenceOld|open|fenceNew→findReceipt`；deployment `deploy→findReceipt`、`rollback→findRollbackReceipt`、`runProbe→findProbeReceipt`；migration `snapshot→findSnapshot`、`migrate→findMigrationReceipt`、`repair→findRepairReceipt`、`reconcile→findReconcileReceipt`、`destroyCanary→findDestroyCanaryReceipt`；canonical `readSource→findSourceReadReceipt`、`compareAndSwap→findCasReceipt`；mount `mount|unmount→findReceipt`；authorityMaterialization `authorityMaterialize→findAuthorityMaterializeReceipt`、`authorityUnmount→findAuthorityUnmountReceipt`；archiveStore `putIfAbsent→findPutReceipt`、`ensureRetentionAtLeast→findRetentionReceipt`；isolation Provider `isolate→findReceipt`；evidenceSigner `sign→findSignerReceipt`；evidenceStore `putRawIfAbsent|putSignedIfAbsent|putSignedSubjectIfAbsent→findMutationReceipt`；runAttestor `attest→findAttestationReceipt`；checkoutClaimIssuer `issue→findIssueReceipt`；session仅`expireReservation|expireTakeoverTicket|attach|claim|renew|refreshAuthorization|attachTakeover|beginIntermediate|beginForward|commitPoint|reserveForward|commitForwardSwap|releaseForward|completeIntermediate|complete|completeAndRelease→findReceipt`；credentialBroker `transitionGrantSets→findGrantSetTransitionSet`、`isolateGrantSet→findGrantSetIsolation`。Session read由同一session-state-read Authorization恢复，reserve/reserveTakeover由SessionBootstrapRecoveryInvocation恢复；Broker Generation/Issue/Seal、bootstrapIsolation及目标Authorization自身发布由各自bootstrap recovery恢复，均不得塞入control-recovery。Authorization闭字段共同为`nonce/trustPolicyHash/context/originalAuthorizationRef+hash/originalPurpose/providerKind/findOperation/originalAction+originalToken+originalParamsHash/providerRef+verifyRef+receiptSchemaVersion/controlRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`；context是`cutover={cutoverRunId,cutoverSessionPolicyHash}`或`standalone={operationRunId,purpose,generationReceiptHash,attestedIssuerOrLauncherHash}`，Binding.scope必须是同context的`exactControl{context,revocationGeneration,binding=recovery{originalAuthorizationHash,providerKind,findOperation,originalAction,originalParamsHash,originalToken}}`。Session/Traffic/Deployment等只接受cutover；Authority/Snapshot/Archive/ordinary release的Signer/Store/Attestor/Issuer只接受standalone。originalAction必须逐字等于真实Port方法名，展示operation/sourceKind另存；外层字段与scope binding逐项全等，任何自由别名、List、原动作重做或跨token恢复均拒绝。

前述control-recovery闭映射还必须包含`authorizationReceipt consume→findConsumptionReceipt`；它恢复Provider独立事务日志中的授权消费事实，不要求也不得伪造业务WAL Intent。此条是闭映射组成部分，不是可选扩展。

`purpose=session-state-read`只允许`nonce/cutoverRunId/trustPolicyHash/cutoverSessionPolicyHash/sessionStoreRef/readToken/sessionStateReadCredentialGrantBinding{scope=sessionStateRead{cutoverRunId,readToken,readerExecutor,revocationGeneration,actions=[read,findStateReadReceipt]}}/attestedLauncher/createdAt/expiresAt/signature`；该Binding不含未知的state/version/epoch，只能返回签名最小Projection。同一Authorization/Binding以原readToken调用findStateReadReceipt恢复，不需要也不得另签exactControl。revocationGeneration与Broker/Isolation使用同一权威单调值；Isolation bump后旧read Binding同样失效。

六者不启动CutoverCli，不带AuthorizationReceipt、Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader/Signer或业务文件、流量、DB权限。Decoder按Grant scope分支验证Provider签名、Issuer、Canonical exact Scope、统一revocationGeneration和TTL并计算credentialBindingHash；Receipt或Absent Proof绑定`authorizationHash+nonce+attestedLauncherHash+credentialBindingHash+action+paramsHash+stableToken`。resource/takeover完成后，`purpose=phase`只允许`phase=prepare|apply|verify|restore|activate|finalize`；续跑统一为`purpose=phase-continuation + continuationKind=prepare|apply|verify|restore|activate|forwardBuild|forwardCleanup|repair|finalize`并绑定原purpose/operation/Authorization；另有`purpose=postcommit-forward-prepare|postcommit-repair`，forward以`operation=build|cleanup`区分。Decoder按`purpose+phaseOrNull+continuationKindOrNull+operationOrNull+planKindOrNull`闭表验证；恰好只有与purpose对应的一个Discriminator非null，其他严格null，拒绝字段混用。normalPhaseHandoff不新增purpose：Takeover Decoder先验证Payload四边、Completion、Isolation、Ticket和nextPhaseIntentHash；随后phase Decoder只接受与Payload.nextPhase相同的`purpose=phase`，要求takeoverTicket/newEpoch/prospectiveExecutor/pending Set/Completion Capability全部闭合，且禁止phase=prepare|restore或任一Continuation/Forward/Repair判别字段。普通phaseHandoff claim则必须takeoverTicket=null；两个入口字段同时出现立即拒绝。`phase=restore`的入口权威也是闭Union：`completedHandoff={action=claim,expectedState=Prepared|Applied|Verified,previousCompletionReceipt+hash,nextTransitionCapabilityHash,takeoverTicket=null}`或`takeover={action=attachTakeover,nextWorkPayload.kind=restore,expectedState=Preparing|Prepared|Applying|Applied|Verifying|Verified|Activating,takeoverTicket+hash,newEpoch,isolationReceipt+hash}`；Running state选择completedHandoff、Restoring选择任一分支、或takeover缺Isolation/Ticket均在启动CLI前拒绝。

Session Invocation Decoder还必须先按Canonical exactControl.binding判别，再检查Port：phaseHandoff只可进入`claim|beginIntermediate|beginForward`且外层action必须同名，ticketAttach只可进入`attach|attachTakeover`，owned/expiry/bootstrap同理；任何“scope schema合法但Port错位”的调用同样fail closed，不能靠下一层Store补救。

每个可执行分支均有新nonce、cutoverRunId、trustPolicyHash，并继承Receipt/Core/Launcher Publisher/Reader与Signer公共Binding字段；共同且不可省略的执行字段为`executorToolBundleRef/executorCommitOid/executorTreeOid/executorRunAttestationRef/entryName=CutoverCli/entryHash/nodeBinaryHash/npmCliHash/npmResourceTreeHash`，会话字段为`cutoverSessionPolicyHash/sessionStoreRef/sessionCredentialGrantBinding/credentialGrantSetRef+hash/sessionProviderVerifyRef/sessionReceiptSchemaVersion/resourceKeys[]/resourceSetHash/reservationTicketRefOrNull/takeoverTicketRefOrNull/previousCompletionReceiptRefOrNull+hashOrNull/nextTransitionCapabilityHashOrNull/expectedFencingEpoch/expectedSessionState/expectedActivationWork/expectedSessionVersion/expectedSessionHash/sessionLease/sessionTokenDerivationVersion=v1/immutableActionSetHash/sessionJournalRealpath/sessionJournalParentRealpath/sessionJournalDevice/sessionJournalAllowedFileHash`，另有createdAt/expiresAt、signer/keyVersion/signature和该分支唯一字段。previousCompletion与Capability字段只在普通phaseHandoff claim或normalPhaseHandoff takeover出现；前者要求takeoverTicket=null，后者要求takeoverTicket非null，其他执行分支全部为null。具体Session Binding必须经Broker验签并符合cutoverSessionPolicy的Issuer/Scope Template/TTL，其fencingEpochOrNull与revocationGenerationOrNull必须和当前Ownership逐字节全等。每个Session调用实例的稳定Token唯一按`sha256("cutover-session-v1" || cutoverRunId || immutableActionSetHash || action || actionInstanceId)`派生；`actionInstanceId`是该动作的Canonical逻辑身份，renew固定含expectedVersion+leaseGeneration，其他动作含相位/attemptId/forwardId/expectedVersion，Receipt绑定action、actionInstanceId、Canonical paramsHash与token。同一ownership version内的未决Session CAS复用原Token；Takeover先用旧token执行findReceipt，若已成功则消费旧Receipt，若确认absent才按newVersion/newEpoch派生新的Session actionInstanceId与Token。Provider业务动作另以不含version/epoch的logicalActionHash固定Token，Receipt分开绑定`logicalActionHash + attemptContext{sessionVersion,fencingEpoch,credentialBindingHash}`；旧响应存在则复用，absent时才允许更高epoch重试同一logical action。禁止仅按action保存单值，也禁止让Session CAS跨version复用token。Journal是与target/candidate/quarantine均不重叠的专用Resource Key，全部阶段只可追加同一精确文件；prepare只获该文件及父目录fsync权，不因此获得任何Quarantine Payload权限。prepare必须绑定reservationTicket并以attach启动；接管后的continuation/restore/forward/repair必须绑定takeoverTicket/newEpoch并以attachTakeover启动；完成后接管的正常下一phase还必须绑定同一Ticket、上一Completion/Capability和normalPhaseHandoff Payload；同一executor且Lease有效的提交后中间动作以beginIntermediate或beginForward启动；无接管的普通后续phase绑定前一Completion epoch并以claim启动。禁止用一份宽授权跨阶段，Launcher必须先取得匹配的最新SessionOwnershipReceipt与Resource Reservation Receipt才可执行CLI。

`prepare`是唯一不依赖未来PostTree Evidence的预切授权，只绑定已存在的`SourceSnapshotSetRef+SourceSnapshotSetExecutorRunReceiptRef+SourceSnapshotSetHash`及其中恰好六个Source Snapshot Ref/Hash、该Set绑定的`baselineFileDispositionReportRef+hash+preManifestHash+postManifestCandidateHash`、preCommitOid、确定性author/committer/timestamp/message、AuthoritySnapshotRef+其ExecutorRunReceiptRef、canonical Ref基础字段和`authorityMaterializationPlan={source=snapshotPair,authorityPolicyHash,materializedRootRealpath+parent+device,namespace,entriesHash,materializeCredentialGrantBinding,materializeRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding,unmountCredentialGrantBinding,unmountRecoveryCredentialGrantBinding,materializeToken,unmountToken,providerRef,verifyRef,receiptSchemaVersion,lease,deadlines,receiptPredicatesHash}`；它同时预签`targetShopRealpath/targetShopParentRealpath/activationCandidateRealpath/activationCandidateParentRealpath/expectedParentDevice`和上述独立session Journal四字段，使CleanClone有权创建且只能创建候选、Journal Writer有权创建且只能追加该文件。它还固定`predeleteClaimRunId`并完整签入未来release Quality的`qualityEvidenceStoreRef/qualityPublisherCredentialGrantBinding/publisherAllowedKinds/publisherPrefix/qualityReaderCredentialGrantBinding/readerAllowedKinds/readerPrefix/qualitySignerRef/qualitySignerCredentialGrantBinding/qualitySignerIdentity/qualitySignerKeyVersion/qualitySignerPurposes/attestorRef/attestorCredentialGrantBinding/attestorIdentity/attestorKeyVersion/attestorPurpose=quality-completion/attestorSubjectId=predeleteClaimRunId/attestationPublisherCredentialGrantBinding/attestationPublisherAllowedKinds=[RunAttestation]/attestationPublisherPrefix/attestationPublisherSubjectId=predeleteClaimRunId/attestationRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding/recoveryAuthorityContext={kind=cutover,currentAuthorizationHash,cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash}`，以及发布Claim本身所需的`checkoutClaimIssuerRef/checkoutClaimSignerCredentialGrantBinding/checkoutClaimSignerIdentity/checkoutClaimSignerKeyVersion/checkoutClaimSignerPurpose/claimPublisherCredentialGrantBinding/claimRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding`；所有Scope/TTL/epoch/generation/Binding Hash只在完整Binding内出现。Quality Publisher、Attestation Publisher、Recovery Reader、Quality Reader与两类Signer绝不混用。它不允许写Quarantine Payload、CAS或Finalize。Launcher先用resource-claim授权reserve全部Keys，Issuer收到Reservation Ticket/epoch后才签prepare，Launcher再attach `Empty→Preparing`；CutoverCli prepare先完整验证Report与SnapshotSet Pair，创建并fsync session Journal首条记录，执行authorityMaterializationPlan并取得Materialization Receipt，再生成PostTree/Bundle，用EvidenceCheckoutClaimIssuer把该Receipt与Unmount Plan签入`claimMode=cutoverPredelete`且绑定同一SnapshotSet Pair的Claim，在签名activationCandidateRealpath运行唯一Quality，执行/恢复Authority unmount并验证zero，再调用Attestor把Materialization/Unmount Receipt签入RunAttestation，最后调用`CleanClone.sealCandidate(initial)`。Prepared Core绑定Reservation/Resource Keys/epoch、Report/两Manifest Hash、SnapshotSet Pair、六子Ref、sealed RootActivationManifest及五个路径/设备约束、Journal Ref/terminal Hash、Candidate Git/Ephemeral Manifest、predelete Quality/Sign/Attestation、Authority Materialization/Unmount Receipt及authorityZero、确定性ReleaseManifest、稳定ReleaseArtifactBlobRef、run-specific Artifact Evidence Ref及commit/tree/source/contract/migration/catalog Hash；Launcher发布RunReceipt后complete Preparing→Prepared，Session Completion传递同一不变量。

Prepare在创建candidate、AuthorityMount或Quality临时项前先写/fsync canonical `fileActionIntent(fileAction=prepareCreate)`，每个已创建Root记录realpath/inode/device/ownerRunId；正常完成后由sealed RootActivation/Candidate Git/Ephemeral Manifest穷尽。若prepare在Prepared Core前永久失败，禁止直接abandon或释放Resource Keys。外部Issuer必须对失败后的signed Root逐段`lstat`生成按深度/路径排序的精确`PrepareCleanupEntrySet`（每项path/type/hash或size/mode/link/inode/device，禁止目录前缀/通配），再签`phase=restore, predecessor=prepareAttempt`授权，绑定原prepare Authorization、Session Preparing Ownership/epoch、Prepare WAL、candidate/AuthorityMount、Entry Set、unmount Provider/Token和`targetShopUntouched=true`。若已产Prepared Pair但Archive门失败、用户取消或必须更换Action Set，则签`predecessor=preparedCancel`，直接绑定Prepared Pair、sealed RootActivation/Candidate Physical Entry Set和同一targetShop untouched guard，无需重扫推测。Preparing是未完成运行态，只能在Isolation后以`attachTakeover(nextWorkPayload=restore)`原子进入Restoring；Prepared是已完成边界态，原Executor仍活着时以phaseHandoff claim进入Restoring，原Executor死亡时同样走Isolation→reserveTakeover(restore)→attachTakeover。任何`claim Preparing→Restoring`均由Decoder/Store拒绝。QuarantineFinalizer逐文件unlink、反向rmdir并卸载只读Mount，未授权新Entry会令根目录非空而失败，不得递归兜底。CutoverRestored Core+Receipt必须证明candidate/mount/temp路径全零且targetShop inode未变，Session以单次`completeAndRelease(...,Restored,resourceKeys,hash)`原子写终态并释放。这样predelete Quality失败、Archive失败或Action Set改变不会遗留候选，也不会让下一run接管脏路径。

`CutoverPrepared` Core必须从predelete QualityRun的Architecture Task Evidence所绑定的`FileDisposition.report` Ref/Hash，逐Hash提取静态、生成、Registry和Bundle扫描结果，写入`referenceZero={architectureTaskEvidenceRef,fileDispositionReportHash,scannedSourceTreeHash,oldReferenceCount=0,queryHash}`；FileDisposition不是虚构TaskCatalog taskId。Prepared还绑定Git Metadata与Ephemeral Artifact Manifest Ref/Hash及Root Physical Entry Set Hash，使源码、已删Tombstone、Git历史和被忽略磁盘项共同穷尽每个旧Root。

`apply`授权只能在Prepared Pair及Session Prepared Completion整包Receipt验证后签发。它绑定pre/post树、Authority/Archive三组Pair、predelete Sign/Attestation、referenceZero、RootActivationManifest、精确retired Source/Git/Ephemeral Entry Set、全部路径/inode/device；Telemetry运行元组显式为`telemetryPolicyHash/providerRef/telemetryCredentialGrantBinding/queryCatalogHash/requiredSeries+labels/observationWindowHash/telemetryObservationActionInstanceId/telemetryObservationToken/providerVerifyRef/receiptSchemaVersion/deadline`，Traffic元组显式为`trafficControlPolicyHash/providerRef/fenceCredentialGrantBinding/gateway+scheduler+consumer refs/fenceToken/providerVerifyRef/schema/deadline`。Launcher以claim整包Receipt完成Prepared→Applying；CLI先写`providerIntent(providerAction=fenceOld)`，调用fenceOld，排空后写`providerIntent(providerAction=observe)`并观察effectiveAt之后的零窗口，只有Series完整、新鲜、无gap且值严格为0才写drain/telemetry/trafficZero Checkpoint。

通过后先写`fileActionIntent(fileAction=quarantineCreate)`并完成父目录，再由RootActivator写正式rootAction Intent/Completed交换targetShop，DeleteExecutor以fileAction rename逐项移入Quarantine，Tombstone用alreadyAbsent。Applied Core绑定活动targetShop的postCommit/tree/Clean、Root结果和Entry结果；Launcher发布RunReceipt后以完整phaseInvocation/currentOwnership调用complete。Crash按WAL、RecoveryInvocation与inode恢复，不使用fenceIntent等自由别名。

`verify`授权绑定CutoverApplied Pair、Session Applied Completion整包Receipt、同一SourceSnapshotSet Pair和活动`targetShopRealpath/inode/device/postCommitOid/postTreeOid/cleanStatusHash`，且只能引用Prepared已有的predelete ReleaseManifest/稳定Artifact Blob/Evidence。它固定postApplyQualityRunId、`authorityMaterializationPlan={source=snapshotPair,authorityPolicyHash,materializedRootRealpath+parent+device,namespace,entriesHash,四份Materialize/Unmount Mutation与Recovery Binding,两Token,providerRef,verifyRef,receiptSchemaVersion,lease,deadlines,receiptPredicatesHash}`和`recoveryAuthorityContext={kind=cutover,currentAuthorizationHash,cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash}`，并显式签入：Quality Store Writer/Reader各自CredentialGrantBinding；Quality Signer、完整Attestor Signer(identity+keyVersion+purpose+subject)、Attestation Publisher、exact-subject Attestation Recovery Reader、CutoverState Signer/Publisher/Bootstrap Reader各自独立的CredentialGrantBinding、identity、purpose、kind/prefix/subject；禁止裸CredentialRef或复用。

verify的Provider动作闭字段逐项为：Canonical=`policy/provider/remote/ref/oldOid/newOid/canonicalCredentialGrantBinding/casToken/verifyRef/schema/deadline/canonicalReceiptSignerRef+signerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose+subjectId/canonicalReceiptPublisherCredentialGrantBinding+kind+prefix+subjectId/canonicalReceiptRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding+exactKind+subjectId`；Canary Deploy=`deploymentPolicyHash/provider/canaryTargetRef/deployCredentialGrantBinding/deploymentToken/migrationMode=isolated/artifactBlob+hash/verifyRef/schema/deadline`；Canary Migration=`migrationPolicyHash/provider/derivedCanaryNamespace+migrationCredentialGrantBinding+isolatedMigrationToken/destroyCredentialGrantBinding+destroyCanaryToken/providerVerifyRef/schema/deadline/canaryZeroPredicateHash`；可变Smoke=`probeCredentialGrantBinding/probeToken/writeToken/cleanupToken/probeNamespace/zeroAfterCleanup/verifyRef/schema/deadline`。这些Token都是本run实例值并进入Grant exactTokens，不能从Policy模板临时派生。授权绑定原Fence Receipt/epoch且无open Token。

Launcher以claim整包Receipt进入Verifying。CutoverCli先执行/恢复authorityMaterializationPlan并取得签名Materialization Receipt，才可构造下段CutoverState并启动postApply Quality；Quality在真实targetShop运行，只允许allowlist临时写，并从StateVerification取得只读AuthorityInput。Quality结束后逐项清零并复验sealed Manifest，再执行/恢复Authority unmount且证明authorityZero，最后CiRunAttestor把两Receipt与zero签入RunAttestation。任何Tracked/非allowlist写入、未卸载或Receipt不等都失败。Canonical、Deploy、Canary migrate、runProbe与destroyCanary都按WAL正式providerAction执行；destroyCanary在finally无条件运行并以Receipt+namespaceZero作为CutoverVerified必需字段。

`CutoverState.ts`用EvidenceEnvelopeBuilder、state Signer与state Writer发布唯一CutoverState Envelope。Payload固定绑定verify Authorization、Prepared/Applied Pair、`claimSessionMutationResultReceiptRef+hash`、其中的current SessionOwnershipReceipt+hash、Journal prefix、FileDisposition、PostTree/Bundle、Authority/Snapshot Pair、已执行AuthorityMaterializationPlan及Materialization Receipt、尚待执行的Unmount Plan、活动targetShop、toolchain、recoveryAuthorityContext，以及完整Quality Writer/Reader/Signer、Attestor Signer(identity/keyVersion/purpose/subject)、Attestation Publisher与exact-subject Attestation Recovery Reader CredentialGrantBinding。`SessionClaimReceipt`与孤立`claimedVersion`类型删除；Decoder从claim整包的owned.ownershipReceipt复算version/epoch/hash。Launcher只用verify授权预置的State Bootstrap Reader CredentialGrantBinding完整Get/验Envelope，再0600物化并同时传Ref、path和payloadSha256。CheckoutClaimVerifier完整Get所有Pair与Session整包Receipt；StateVerification逐项Hash绑定Materialization Receipt、Unmount Plan、Recovery Context和所有Grant Binding，禁止ambient fallback。

Verify在真实`targetShopRealpath`按“Materialize Authority→postApply Quality→清理临时项→Unmount/zero→Attestor”的顺序取得新的`postApplyQualityRunRef/postApplyReleaseManifestRef/postApplyArtifactBlobRef/postApplyArtifactBlobHash/postApplyArtifactEvidenceRef/postApplySignBundleRef/postApplyRunAttestationRef`，完整Get并验签后，必须在任何CAS或Deploy前逐字节/逐Hash证明：Attestation内的Materialization/Unmount Receipt与State计划全等且authorityZero为真；`postApplyReleaseManifestHash=predeleteReleaseManifestHash`、`postApplyArtifactBlobRef/hash=predeleteArtifactBlobRef/hash`，且Manifest内`commitOid/treeOid/sourceTreeHash/contractHash/migrationHash/catalogHash/components/topologyHash/artifactHash`与Prepared固定值全部相等。两个run-specific Artifact Evidence Ref只要求各自验签并都指向同一稳定Blob，不要求Ref相等。ReleaseManifest必须是与runId/time/signature/EvidenceRef分离的JCS确定性部署描述；不等即保持Fence并进入可恢复Verifying状态。部署对象始终是授权已绑定的predelete稳定Blob，postApply只给独立复算证明。

通过相等门后，Verify依次写正式`providerIntent(providerAction=compareAndSwap)`并执行Canonical CAS，写`providerIntent(providerAction=deploy,sourceKind=initial,migrationMode=isolated)`部署canary，写`providerIntent(providerAction=migrate,providerOperation=isolatedMigrate)`升级派生namespace，再以`providerAction=runProbe`执行可变Smoke并清零。finally无条件写`providerIntent(providerAction=destroyCanary)`，用独立destroy Binding/Token销毁namespace；只有Destroy Receipt、Provider签名namespaceZero和本地资源扫描全零后才可继续。每个Intent均有对应Completed，丢响应按action构造control-recovery RecoveryInvocation，SignedAbsent才同Token重试；Canonical第三OID或Provider冲突停机。CutoverVerified绑定pre/post Quality/Manifest/Artifact/Sign/Attestation、Equality、Canonical/Deploy/Migrate/Probe/Destroy Receipt、namespaceZero、fenceStillHeld及当前SessionMutationResult/Ownership。Launcher发布RunReceipt后以完整phaseInvocation完成Verifying→Verified。Verified只表示可激活，不表示流量开放。

`activate`授权只能在Verified Pair和Session Verified Completion存在后签发。它绑定predelete ReleaseManifest、稳定ReleaseArtifactBlobRef/hash、完整按componentId排序的components/topologyHash、Fence Receipt/epoch与精确gateway/scheduler/consumer，并逐真实动作重新签入互不复用的最小运行字段：Deployment=`deploymentPolicyHash/deploymentProviderRef/darkDeployCredentialGrantBinding/deploymentProviderVerifyRef/deploymentReceiptSchemaVersion/productionTargetRef/migrationMode=none/darkDeployToken/deploymentTimeout/expectedComponentSet+digest+command+staticRoot+health/deploymentReceiptPredicateHash`；Migration=`migrationPolicyHash/migrationProviderRef/snapshotCredentialGrantBinding+migrateCredentialGrantBinding+reconcileCredentialGrantBinding/migrationProviderVerifyRef/migrationReceiptSchemaVersion/productionDatabaseRef/snapshotPolicyRef/migrationPlanHash/expectedOldSchemaHash/expectedNewSchemaHash/reconcileCatalogHash/snapshotToken/migrationToken/reconcileToken/migrationTimeout`；Traffic=`trafficControlPolicyHash/trafficProviderRef/openCredentialGrantBinding+fenceNewCredentialGrantBinding/trafficProviderVerifyRef/trafficReceiptSchemaVersion/gatewayRefs+schedulerRefs+consumerRefs/originalFenceReceipt/openNewToken/fenceNewToken/trafficDeadline`；Telemetry=`telemetryPolicyHash/telemetryProviderRef/liveObservationCredentialGrantBinding/telemetryProviderVerifyRef/telemetryReceiptSchemaVersion/queryCatalogHash/requiredSeries+requiredLabels/minimumLiveWindow+windowHash+observationActionInstanceId+observationToken/minFreshness/maxGap/telemetryDeadline`。每份Binding的exactActions/Targets/Tokens只含对应单动作，并回写到Request/WAL/Receipt的credentialBindingHash；另绑定Owner Reconciliation Catalog、forwardRepairRunbookRefs及expectedState/version/hash。禁止ambient Credential、跨动作/跨Provider Binding复用或借用verify阶段过期Scope。授权不得引用尚未产生的deploymentComponentReceipt。Launcher claim `Verified→Activating`；dark deploy后Adapter逐Receipt验Provider签名、target/token/topology/component/digest/health与上述谓词，再从这些已验Receipt确定性派生`openNewTargetMap=componentId→deploymentComponentReceipt`，集合少一项、多一项或重复都失败。`fenceNewToken`在immutable action set中预签，只能用fenceNewCredentialGrantBinding关闭该派生new Target，不能开放或指向old。

Activate在Fence held下先执行全部可撤销准备：WAL先fsync canonical `providerIntent(providerAction=snapshot)`，MigrationControl.snapshot再创建不可变PITR/Snapshot并完成隔离恢复校验；DeploymentControl把同一Topology dark deploy到productionTarget且强制migrationMode=none，逐组件Health成功；此时无真实业务写。任一步失败且Provider按Token确认未发生`activationCommitted`，可按activateAttempt进度授权只清理已经发生的dark deploy并回到旧系统；Snapshot即使已完成也保留。准备完成后CLI写/fsync `activationCommitIntent(snapshotRef,oldSchema,newSchema,topologyHash,darkDeploymentReceiptSetHash)`，再调用Session Store `commitPoint`条件写不可逆`activationCommitted`并取得Provider签名`ActivationCommitReceipt/version/payloadHash`，完整验签后把Receipt fsync进WAL；从这一刻禁止旧制品restore。若Store已提交而本地尚无completed，必须按Session read/find恢复同一Receipt，不得重复选择payload。

提交后的正常路径是绑定同一`ActivationCommitReceipt`的activate-continuation：MigrationControl按Manifest的MigrationMain Plan升级生产库，逐Owner Reconcile确认Schema/Order/Payment/Inventory/Voucher/Finance水位；TrafficControl.open再用从已验production Receipt Set确定性派生的Target Map，把真实Surface原子指向ApiMain/JobsMain/六端静态components。每个snapshot/darkDeploy/commit/migration/reconcile/open/fenceNew Intent与Provider Receipt都先后fsync，Crash统一findReceipt(token)或Session read恢复。公开流量后完成minimumLiveWindow与Live SLI；异常时必须用预签`fenceNewToken`调用TrafficControl.fenceNew关闭新入口/任务并绑定Receipt，绝不打开旧代码。正常Continuation不能临时更改Migration、Artifact、Topology或Config；发生不匹配时转入下面的有类型前向准备/修复链。

`CutoverAuthorization.ts`还定义同一Authorization EvidenceKind下的`purpose=postcommit-forward-prepare|postcommit-repair`分支，它们不是第七个Cutover终态相位。Issuer只能在完整Get ActivationCommitReceipt、当前Activating Session/work/version/epoch和失败Action事实后签发；流量前置是封闭Union：`preOpenHeld={originalFenceReceipt,findReceipt(openNewToken)=absent,openReceipt=null,fenceNewReceipt=null}`用于Migration/Reconcile在首次open前失败，或`postOpenFenced={openReceipt,fenceNewReceipt}`用于Live SLI/应用在公开后失败。两者都证明旧Target不可再开且真实流量当前held。新执行器必须先走Executor Isolation→takeover-claim→reserveTakeover→带新epoch授权→attachTakeover；原executor Lease仍有效时也只能用beginIntermediate切换Work，不能绕过Session变更Authorization Hash。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/Forward.ts`定义`CutoverForwardPrepared|CutoverForwardCleaned`两个跨执行器Core。`postcommit-forward-prepare(operation=build)`完整绑定普通release基线`QualityRun+SignBundle+RunAttestation+ReleaseManifest+稳定Artifact Blob+run-specific Artifact Evidence` Pair、当前AuthoritySnapshot Core+ExecutorRunReceipt Pair及其exact-subject Reader CredentialGrantBinding，再固定`forwardId/forwardClaimRunId/forwardCommit/tree/sourceTree/forwardCandidateRealpath/parent/device/当前targetShop inode/qualityEphemeralAllowlist/Traffic Guard/sessionJournal/Session epoch`。Canonical read元组固定`canonicalRefPolicyHash/providerRef/remoteRef+allowedRef+expectedOid/canonicalReadCredentialGrantBinding/canonicalProviderVerifyRef/readReceiptSchemaVersion/canonicalReadToken/readDeadline`；`CanonicalRef.readSource(request,token)/findSourceReadReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`返回Provider签名、内容寻址Source Bundle Receipt，CleanClone只消费该Bundle，不访问ambient remote。

build授权还签`authorityMaterializationPlan={source=snapshotPair,authorityPolicyHash,materializedRootRealpath+parent+device,namespace,entriesHash,四份Authority Materialize/Unmount Mutation与Recovery Binding,两Token,providerRef,verifyRef,receiptSchemaVersion,lease,deadlines,receiptPredicatesHash}`、`recoveryAuthorityContext={kind=cutover,currentAuthorizationHash,cutoverRunId,currentOwnershipHash}`及本候选独立短时`qualityEvidenceStoreRef/qualityPublisherCredentialGrantBinding/qualityReaderCredentialGrantBinding/qualitySignerRef+signerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose/attestorRef+attestorCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose=quality-completion+subjectId=forwardClaimRunId/attestationPublisherCredentialGrantBinding+allowedKinds=[RunAttestation]+prefix+subjectId=forwardClaimRunId/attestationRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding{exact kind=RunAttestation,subject=forwardClaimRunId}/checkoutClaimIssuerRef+claimSignerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose/claimPublisherCredentialGrantBinding`；全部绑定forwardClaimRunId、candidate cwd、Authority Pair和epoch，禁止复用基线CI Grant。它不绑定未来Manifest，也不允许swap/CAS/deploy/open。原executor调用beginForward，Takeover以attachTakeover执行同一Ledger CAS，创建`ledger[forwardId]={status:building,buildAuthorizationHash,candidateRealpathHash}`后才可创建candidate。

CutoverCli从已验Source Bundle检出forwardCommit，执行authorityMaterializationPlan并取得Receipt，发布`claimMode=forwardCandidate`且绑定build Authorization、Authority Pair与基线Pair的CheckoutClaim；在candidate重跑完整21节点Quality、Sign和CiRunAttestor，并使用本轮全新Claim/Writer/Reader/Signer/Attestor/Attestation Publisher/Attestation Recovery Reader。候选必须逐Hash等于基线的`ReleaseManifest/稳定Artifact Blob/components/topology/commit/tree/source/contract/migration/catalog`；run-specific Evidence Ref可不同但Blob必须相同。Quality结束后用计划内独立unmount Grant拆Authority mount并验zero，CiRunAttestor把两Receipt写入Attestation；随后清理allowlist、seal stage=forwardRepair并发布CutoverForwardPrepared。Core绑定基线与candidate Pair、Authority Pair、Source Read、Authority Materialization/Unmount Receipt及zero、Candidate Mount/Unmount、Equality Report、candidate inode/device、RootActivationManifest、Artifact/Topology、Physical Entry Set及forwardId。Launcher发布RunReceipt后，用完整phaseInvocation与当前ownershipHandle调用completeIntermediate，原子`building→prepared(pairRef)`。

Forward build在任意`fileActionIntent(fileAction=prepareCreate)`后崩溃可由原动作Continuation或两步Takeover继续；判定永久失败、候选已Prepared但决定不用、或被新候选取代时，必须另签`postcommit-forward-prepare(operation=cleanup)`。Cleanup Payload是封闭Union：`failedBuild`绑定原build Authorization、Session `forwardPreparing`且Ledger=`building`、terminal WAL与Issuer逐项lstat签成的`ForwardCleanupEntrySet`；`unusedPrepared|supersededPrepared`绑定完整ForwardPrepared Pair及Ledger=`prepared`和sealed Physical Manifest。两者都固定candidate/parent/device、Traffic Guard、unmount字段和新的cleanup Token，禁止Root swap/CAS/deploy/migration/open。Session进入`forwardCleaning`，`QuarantineFinalizer(mode=forwardCleanup)`只逐项删除该候选/mount/temp并证明targetShop untouched、candidate payload zero；CutoverCli通过`Forward.ts`发布`CutoverForwardCleaned` Core，Launcher产Receipt并唯一调用`completeIntermediate(forwardCleaning, CutoverForwardCleaned, repairCheckpoint)`，按reason原子执行`building|prepared→cleanedBy=<PairRef>`。Cleanup本身崩溃同样允许原动作Continuation或两步Takeover，禁止直接丢弃Resource Lease。

`tools/quality/src/cutover/Repair.ts`定义失败修复尝试`CutoverRepairAttempt` Core。Repair Payload共同固定`parentActivateAuthorizationRef+hash/parentRepairAttemptPairOrNull/activationCommitReceipt/resourceKeys+hash/fencingEpoch/currentSchemaHash/failedAction+exactProviderReceiptSet/trafficGuard/repairPlan/repairProbe/newLiveWindow/allowedOwnerSet/forbiddenOldTargetHash/fenceRepairedTargetToken/fenceRepairedTrafficCredentialGrantBinding/derivedFinalTargetMapPredicateHash/trafficProviderVerifyRef+receiptSchemaVersion+deadline`。trafficGuard闭Union为`preOpenHeld={originalFenceReceipt,findOpenReceipt=absent,openOrReopenReceipt=null,fenceRepairedTargetReceipt=null,firstOpenNewToken,firstOpenCredentialGrantBinding}`或`postOpenFenced={openOrReopenReceipt,fenceRepairedTargetReceipt,reopenNewToken,reopenCredentialGrantBinding}`；firstOpen/reopen Binding分别只含open动作、派生final target predicate及自己的Token，公共fenceRepairedTarget Binding只含fenceNew动作/Token，三份互不复用。`repairProbe`是闭Union：`none={kind=none}`或`mutable={kind=mutable,deploymentPolicyHash,deploymentProviderRef,deploymentProviderVerifyRef,deploymentReceiptSchemaVersion,currentVerifiedArtifact,currentDeploymentTargetMap,probeCredentialGrantBinding,probeToken,writeToken,cleanupToken,probeNamespace,zeroAfterCleanup,deadline}`；none分支不得携任何Probe字段，mutable分支全部必填且Binding exactActions/Targets/Tokens逐项全等。公共fenceRepairedTargetToken与open/reopen Token互不相等并全部进入immutableActionSet、WAL、Recovery与各自Grant exactTokens。每轮重新签Telemetry Policy、`telemetryObservationToken+actionInstanceId+windowHash`、Telemetry CredentialGrantBinding、Provider Verify/Schema/Query/Window/Deadline，以及本支Traffic/Migration/Deployment/Canonical每个真实动作各自的CredentialGrantBinding；原activate Grant不得借用。

`repairPlan`闭Union：`databaseRepair={migrationPolicyHash,migrationProviderRef,repairCredentialGrantBinding,reconcileCredentialGrantBinding,migrationProviderVerifyRef,migrationReceiptSchemaVersion,productionDatabaseRef,repairPlanRef+hash,expectedSchemaHash,ownerCatalogHash,repairToken,reconcileToken,currentVerifiedArtifact,currentDeploymentTargetMap,timeout,deadline}`；`deploymentForwardPatch`绑定唯一status=prepared且未消费的CutoverForwardPrepared Pair及其基线/candidate Quality Pair、Artifact/Manifest/components/topology/forwardCommit/tree/candidate/RootActivationManifest，并签`canonicalRefPolicyHash/provider/ref/expectedOld+new/casToken/canonicalCredentialGrantBinding/verifyRef/schema/timeout/canonicalReceiptSignerRef+signerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose+subjectId/canonicalReceiptPublisherCredentialGrantBinding+allowedKinds=[CanonicalRefProviderReceipt]+prefix+subjectId/canonicalReceiptRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding+exactKind+subjectId`，以及`deploymentPolicyHash/providerRef/deploymentCredentialGrantBinding/verifyRef/schema/productionTargetRef/migrationMode=none/deployToken/readOnlyReconciliationCatalogHash/health+targetMapPredicate/timeout/deadline`；`configurationTrafficRepair={deploymentPolicyHash,providerRef,configCredentialGrantBinding,providerVerifyRef,receiptSchemaVersion,currentVerifiedArtifact+topology+targetMap,versionedConfigRef+hash+schema,configTargetRef,configDeployToken,validationCatalogHash,readOnlyReconciliationCatalogHash,timeout,deadline}`。配置分支只调用`DeploymentControl.deploy({source:{kind=configurationRepair,...}},configDeployToken)`，真实action始终是deploy，configTarget必须已占Key并在Policy allowlist。只有databaseRepair调用`MigrationControl.reconcile`并消费独立reconcile Binding/Token及repair Receipt；deployment/configuration两支由ReleaseProbe按已签readOnlyReconciliationCatalog执行只读Owner一致性检查，禁止伪造不存在的DeploymentControl.reconcile。三支都携ReleaseProbe所需的已存在Artifact/Target Map；是否执行可变Smoke只由公共repairProbe判别，mutable必须执行runProbe并证明zeroAfterCleanup，none不得持有Probe能力。所有Binding显式含Ref、Hash、scope、expiry、epoch、revocationGeneration和credentialBindingHash。

deploymentForwardPatch先以完整phaseInvocation/currentOwnership调用reserveForward，执行prepared→reservedByRepair。RootActivator完成原子替换并把Canonical RootSwapResult写入completed WAL后，CutoverCli用本授权Core Signer对完整Result+有序WAL proof签`RootSwapAttestation`；随后唯一调用`commitForwardSwap(phaseInvocation,currentOwnershipHandle,forwardReservationReceipt,rootSwapAttestation)`，Store验证后变为consumedByCompletedRootSwap。root intent前失败时，CutoverRepairAttempt Core只绑定Reservation、no-root Proof和计划disposition，不绑定未来ForwardReleaseReceipt；Launcher发布RunReceipt后调用releaseForward取得Release Receipt，再由Session completeIntermediate绑定该Receipt。root位移开始后只能幂等完成swap。RootSwapResult+ForwardCommit进入本轮成功事实；ForwardRelease只进入Session Completion及后续Activated/Finalize总账，避免未来引用环。Canonical Ref与production deployment同步前移，Crash按WAL与RecoveryInvocation恢复。

Session以beginIntermediate或attachTakeover进入`repairing`，并把签名intermediateContext逐字段写入Session Receipt。各分支执行自己的Migration/Deployment/Configuration动作，统一逐Owner reconcile与只读ReleaseProbe；需要可变Smoke时只经DeploymentControl.runProbe+findProbeReceipt。`preOpenHeld`成功后用原先未发生的firstOpenNewToken首次打开最终new Target，`postOpenFenced`用新签reopenNewToken重新打开最终new Target。之后都必须从新Receipt effectiveAt起重跑完整minimumLiveWindow/Live SLI；状态内序列分别为`preOpenHeld→repairing→reconciled→opened→liveVerified`或`opened→fencedForRepair→repairing→reconciled→reopened→liveVerified`，每跳都有WAL、Provider Receipt和epoch。若open/reopen后的Live Window或SLI失败，本Authorization必须先以WAL intent和预签`fenceRepairedTargetToken`调用TrafficControl.fenceNew，Receipt必须绑定本次已验最终Target Map，验签并fsync completed后才可声明Traffic held。若修复仍失败，原动作Continuation或Takeover先用各Provider findReceipt重建事实，再发布`CutoverRepairAttempt` Core；Core的Traffic结果按闭Union编码为`preOpenFailed={originalFence,findOpen=absent,openOrReopen=null,fenceRepaired=null}`或`postOpenFailedFenced={openOrReopenReceipt,fenceRepairedReceipt}`，并绑定Authorization Ref、父Pair、前后Schema/Artifact/Target、逐Action状态、完整Provider Receipt Set、forward candidate disposition、terminal WAL hash和failure class。Launcher产RunReceipt；若存在尚未swap的Forward Reservation，必须先调用releaseForward并取得ForwardReleaseReceipt；随后`completeIntermediate(repairing,Attempt Pair,intermediateContext,ForwardReleaseReceiptOrNull,repairCheckpoint)`只切Work。下一Repair Authorization只能以该Pair为父，并按其Traffic支消费真实Fence事实。成功时不产Attempt，而直接产CutoverActivated Core，绑定完整Authorization→Attempt Pair链、最终Shop/Commit/Artifact/Schema/Reconcile、open/reopen Receipt、新Live Window、全部RootSwapResult与ForwardCommit/Release Receipt；Launcher只有在Ledger每项恰为`consumedByCompletedRootSwap`或`cleanedBy`、不存在building/prepared/reserved悬空候选时，才可complete `Activating→Activated`。

`restore`授权的`predecessor`是七分支Discriminated Union。`prepareAttempt`绑定prepare Authorization、Session Preparing Claim/epoch、Prepare WAL、外部签名的精确Cleanup Entry Set和targetShop untouched guard；`preparedCancel`绑定Prepared Pair、Session Prepared Completion与sealed Candidate Physical Manifest。`applyAttempt`再分`preFence={providerIntent(providerAction=fenceOld)或null,findReceipt(fenceToken)=absent,rootActionIntentAbsent=true}`与`fenced={Fence Receipt,已完成rootAction/fileAction集合}`，两者都绑定Prepared Pair、apply Authorization、Session Applying Claim/epoch和WAL；`applied`绑定Applied Pair与Session Applied Completion。`verifyAttempt`绑定Applied Pair、verify Authorization、Session Verifying Claim/epoch、WAL和闭进度Union：`beforeCanonical`、`canonicalDone{casReceipt}`、`canaryDeployIntent{casReceipt,deployIntent,findDeploy=absent|matching}`、`canaryDeployed{casReceipt,deployReceipt,namespace}`、`canaryMigrationIntent{前序,migrateIntent,findMigrate=absent|matching}`、`canaryMigrated{前序,migrationReceipt}`、`probeIntent{前序,probeIntent,findProbe=absent|matching}`、`probeCompleted{前序,probeReceipt,probeZero}`、`destroyIntent{前序,destroyIntent,findDestroy=absent|matching}`、`destroyed{前序,destroyReceipt,namespaceZero}`；后支必须携全部前序Receipt且跨支字段拒绝。`verified`绑定Verified Pair、verify Authorization、Session Verified Completion以及其中的CAS/Deploy/Migrate/Probe/Destroy/namespaceZero事实；`activateAttempt`绑定activate Authorization、Session Activating Claim/epoch、WAL，并把进度写成`beforeSnapshot={findSnapshot=absent,darkIntentAbsent}`、`snapshotDoneBeforeDark={snapshotReceipt,findDark=absent}`、`darkIntent={snapshotReceiptOrAbsent,darkIntent,findDark=absent|matching}`或`darkDone={snapshotReceipt,darkDeploymentReceiptSet}`。所有activateAttempt分支都必须通过Session和TrafficControl.findReceipt(openNewToken)证明`activationCommitted`与open Receipt不存在。若任一存在，即使本地WAL缺记录也禁止restore，只能activate-continuation/repair。Hash不能代替Authorization Ref，CLI必须完整Get、验签并核对phase/历史权限字段。

`prepareAttempt|preparedCancel`只能调用QuarantineFinalizer.prepareCleanup清本run candidate/mount/temp并证明targetShop untouched，禁止Traffic/CAS/Deployment字段。`applyAttempt.preFence`是no-op业务恢复：仅绑定findReceipt absent证明，不要求Fence Receipt或reopen Token；`applyAttempt.fenced|applied`才需要精确Source/Quarantine Entry Set、Traffic provider/verifyRef、Fence Receipt及reopenOldToken。`verifyAttempt|verified|activateAttempt`必须由当前restore授权按实际进度重新签入动作独立的完整Binding：Canonical反向CAS、Canary Deployment rollback、Migration destroyCanary、Traffic reopen-old各有provider/policy/verify/schema/target/blob/token/timeout；原verify migrate/probe/destroy回包未决时还要用control-recovery Binding先恢复事实。若deploy可能发生，restore必须先以独立`restoreDestroyCanaryCredentialGrantBinding+restoreDestroyCanaryToken`调用/恢复destroyCanary并取得Provider签名namespaceZero，再以独立canaryRollback Binding移除canary deployment，最后反向CAS；即使原destroy已Completed也要完整验证原Receipt与namespaceZero。Restored Core、RunReceipt与`completeAndRelease`共同以前述namespaceZero、Provider资源扫描零和本地namespace零为前置，不能遗留Canary Key。真实流量始终held，最后才以reopen-old Binding/Token开放旧目标，禁止生产openNew Receipt。`activateAttempt`只对其progress中实际intent/completed的动作授权：beforeSnapshot无Snapshot/dark字段，snapshotDone只保留Snapshot，darkIntent先findReceipt，darkDone才用`darkRollbackToken`清理；Snapshot一律保留。activate Authorization必须预承诺`darkRollbackActionInstanceId+darkRollbackToken`进入immutableActionSet但不预发rollback Binding，restore只新签短时Binding，因而所有真实action/token仍是原immutable action set的子集。

Restore入口按调用前状态封闭，不能再用一个宽`claim`覆盖运行态与完成态。`Preparing|Applying|Verifying`以及满足`activationCommitted不存在 && openNew Receipt不存在`的`Activating`都是未完成运行态：无论Lease过期还是显式abort，Launcher都必须先隔离旧Executor，再以`nextWorkPayload=restore{expectedState,nextState=Restoring,predecessor}`执行reserveTakeover，并由restore Authorization携Ticket/newEpoch/pending Set调用attachTakeover原子进入Restoring；这些状态调用claim一律拒绝。`Prepared|Applied|Verified`是已完成边界态：旧Executor仍持有有效generation时可用上一Completion的phaseHandoff claim进入Restoring；旧Executor已死亡时改用同样严格的restore attachTakeover，不能把normalPhaseHandoff误用于restore。已在Restoring中死亡只可positionResume continuation，保持Restoring而不再次执行状态边。`BeforeCommit`只是守卫谓词，不是Session状态值。prepare分支先精确清候选；applyAttempt.preFence不碰业务系统；其他分支在真实流量held下只逆转WAL证明已发生的动作。activateAttempt按progress清理实际dark deployment并保留Snapshot，再按Verify副作用逆序回退canary与Canonical CAS；RootActivator只在root swap completed时恢复旧targetShop，Source WAL只放回completed Entry，最后仅在旧系统确曾被Fence时用reopenOldToken开放old Target。任何第三OID/Receipt/回退失败保持Restoring。Restored Core绑定全部Authorization/Session/resource epoch/WAL/Provider Receipt/Entry，Launcher产Receipt后以`completeAndRelease`原子完成Restoring→Restored并释放全部Keys。

`finalize`授权只接受CutoverActivated Core+Receipt、Session Activated Completion、Activated内的minimumLiveWindow/Owner Reconciliation/SLI、三组Archive Core+Receipt删除门、精确`retiredEntrySetHash/gitMetadataEntrySetHash/ephemeralEntrySetHash/rootActivationManifestHash/forwardRepairRootSwapSetHash/repairQuarantineEntrySetHash/repairWalSetHash/forwardCandidateLedgerHash/quarantineRealpathHash/journalPredecessorHash`和Publisher字段，并签入expectedState=Activated/version/hash。Launcher claim `Activated→Finalizing`；此时系统早已越过point-of-no-return，restore分支不存在。CLI再次完整Get全部Archive/Drill/Activated/Session及每一份ForwardPrepared、ForwardCleaned、RepairAttempt Pair，证明Ledger中每个ForwardPrepared恰好被某个deploymentForwardPatch消费或被ForwardCleaned取消、所有未消费candidate均已物理为零，才写`purgeOpened`并运行QuarantineFinalizer.finalizePurge；之后只有finalize-continuation可接管，直至Core+Receipt通过`completeAndRelease`原子完成`Finalizing→Finalized`并释放全部Keys。

`DeleteExecutor.ts`只接收已解析apply授权、Trust Policy、SourceSnapshotSet Pair、FileDisposition/retiredSourceSet、GitMetadata与EphemeralArtifact Manifest。任何Nonce消费或Journal创建前，先lstat全部Source与Quarantine父目录并断言`st_dev`完全相同；任一跨设备或潜在EXDEV直接拒绝本次Cutover，必须另选同文件系统的仓库外Quarantine，绝不降级为copy/unlink。每个`present`动作再重验签名/过期、realpath/lstat、Source Hash/Mode/Link/Inode/Device、Archive Blob与Entry Manifest、replacement Evidence、Prepared Pair中的`referenceZero`字段与predelete Quality/Sign/Attestation，以及本次Apply在位移前已验签并fsync入同一WAL的`trafficZero`记录；一致时才按精确Source Key原子rename到`quarantineRealpath/<cutoverRunId>/<rootId>/<sourceKeyHash>`。每个`deletedTombstone`必须证明Live absent、Snapshot baseBlob可完整Get且Archive Entry存在，只写`alreadyAbsent`，不伪造rename。Git Metadata、可重建Ephemeral Entry按各自Manifest逐项处理；shared commonDir exclusion只记`keptShared`且必须证明仍有外部worktree owner。Delete完成后才允许由CLI封装CutoverApplied Core、再由Launcher产Applied Receipt，因而执行器绝不反向消费未来Applied Pair。不跟随符号链接、不调用`rm -rf`、不扩展目录或glob，空目录仅在全部授权子Entry移出后反向深度移除。

唯一崩溃恢复Journal固定为所有业务Authorization共同签入的`sessionJournalRealpath`，父目录0700、文件0600，是已占有且与targetShop、candidate、Quarantine、六Source Root和Git commonDir均不重叠的独立Resource Key。prepare只可创建/追加该文件并fsync父目录，不因此获得Quarantine Payload权限；后续相位继续同一Hash Chain，禁止复制、迁移、截断或另开第二Journal。六类最小控制面动作以及ownership-bootstrap `attach|claim|attachTakeover`发生在业务CLI获得WAL写权限之前，不写伪造的phaseOpened；它们由Session/Provider自身线性事务日志和原签名Receipt承载权威事实，必须被下一个业务phaseOpened及首条controlBootstrap Checkpoint共同镜像绑定后，任何业务副作用才可开始。

每条WAL Record的公共Envelope只有`schemaVersion,seq,prevHash,recordKind,session={runId,latestOwnershipHashOrNull,versionOrNull,sessionHashOrNull,leaseGenerationOrNull,leaseExpiresAtOrNull,serverObservedAt,state,workOrNull,fencingEpochOrNull},resourceSetHash,authorizationDiscriminator,recordedAt`。`authorizationDiscriminator`是闭Union：`phase={purpose=phase,phase=prepare|apply|verify|restore|activate|finalize}`；`continuation={purpose=phase-continuation,continuationKind=prepare|apply|verify|restore|activate|forwardBuild|forwardCleanup|repair|finalize}`；`forward={purpose=postcommit-forward-prepare,operation=build|cleanup}`；`repair={purpose=postcommit-repair,planKind=deploymentForwardPatch|databaseRepair|configurationTrafficRepair}`。phase、continuationKind、operation与planKind绝不同时出现；Empty/预约前允许Session nullable字段，Activating之外workOrNull必须为null。

`recordKind`唯一闭Union及各自Payload如下：

- `phaseOpened`：`authorizationRef+hash,nonce,authorizationReceiptKey,immutableActionSetHash,executorToolBundleRef+commit+tree+attestation+entry+node+npm+launcher+preflight hashes,controlReceiptSetHash`；
- `receiptConsumed`：`authorizationReceiptRef+hash,conditionPutReceiptHash,phaseOpenedHash`；
- `fileActionIntent|fileActionCompleted`：`fileAction=prepareCreate|rename|alreadyAbsent|keptShared|quarantineCreate,stage=initial|forwardPrepare|forwardRepair|initialApply|precommitRestore,sourceKey,beforeRealpathOrNull,afterRealpathOrNull,type,hashOrNull,size,mode,linkTargetOrNull,inode,device,ownerRunId,stableTokenOrNull,intentHashOrNull`；quarantine父目录必须先有`fileActionIntent(fileAction=quarantineCreate)→fileActionCompleted`，之后任何rename才合法；precommitRestore的fileAction只允许逆向已经Completed的initialApply Entry且逐Entry绑定原Intent Hash；seal只走独立sealIntent/sealEntryCompleted，不得复用fileAction；
- `rootActionIntent|rootActionCompleted`：`rootAction=activateOld|activateCandidate|restoreNew|restoreOld|forwardOld|forwardCandidate,swapStage=initial|precommitRestore|forwardRepair,manifestHash,old/new path+inode+device,stableToken,intentHashOrNull,rootSwapResultOrNull`；precommitRestore严格先`restoreNew`把当前newShop移入签名quarantine并Completed，再`restoreOld`放回原oldShop并Completed，两Pair不能颠倒、合并或缺一；
- `providerIntent|providerCompleted`：共同字段为`providerAction,actionDetail,requestHash,policyHash,credentialGrantBinding,attemptContext={sessionVersion,fencingEpoch,credentialBindingHash},stableToken,deadline,intentHashOrNull,providerReceiptOrNull,ledgerBeforeOrNull,ledgerAfterOrNull`。`providerAction=mount|unmount|authorityMaterialize|authorityUnmount|fenceOld|observe|snapshot|readSource|compareAndSwap|deploy|runProbe|rollback|migrate|repair|reconcile|destroyCanary|open|fenceNew|reserveForward|commitForwardSwap|releaseForward`；`actionDetail`按action判别：deploy恰为`{sourceKind=initial|forwardRepair|configurationRepair,migrationMode=isolated|none}`，compareAndSwap恰为`{providerOperation=forward|reverse}`，migrate恰为`{providerOperation=isolatedMigrate|productionMigrate}`，repair恰为`{providerOperation=databaseRepair}`，其余动作严格`null`。credentialGrantBinding必须是完整CredentialGrantBinding且Provider Receipt逐字节绑定同一credentialBindingHash；sourceKind/providerOperation不是自由action别名。find-only RecoveryInvocation不产生Mutation Intent，只形成providerReadObservation；
- `storeActionIntent|storeActionCompleted`：`storeAction=putRawIfAbsent|putSignedIfAbsent|putSignedSubjectIfAbsent|archivePutIfAbsent|archiveRetention,storeRef,subjectOrBlobHash,expectedSizeOrNull,requiredRetainUntilOrNull,credentialGrantBinding,stableToken,intentHashOrNull,storeReceiptOrNull`；archiveRetention必须有requiredRetainUntil且Completed绑定原old/new RetentionReceipt，其他支该字段严格null。AuthorizationReceipt消费发生在WAL权限之前，只走Provider独立事务日志并由`receiptConsumed`镜像；Get/head/find是只读Observation，不伪造Mutation；
- `signActionIntent|signActionCompleted`：`signAction=signEvidenceEnvelope|signRunAttestation|signCanonicalProviderReceipt,signerRef,purpose,subjectId,canonicalBytesHash,credentialGrantBinding,stableToken,intentHashOrNull,signerReceiptOrNull`；KMS handle只经CredentialBroker.withResolvedCredential进入一次调用；
- `checkpointRecord`：`checkpointKind=controlBootstrap|executorIsolation|authorizationRefreshed|providerReadObservation|drainComplete|telemetryObservation|trafficZero|purgeOpened|authorityMountZero|candidateSealed|forwardLedgerState,sortedInputReceiptHashes,observationWindowOrNull,entrySetHashOrNull,proofHash,ownershipHash,grantSetRefOrNull+hashOrNull`；
- `sealIntent|sealEntryCompleted`：`sealPlanHash,stage,candidateRoot,pathOrNull,inodeOrNull,modeOrNull,entryHashOrNull`，全部Entry后才允许candidateSealed；
- `cleanupUnlinkIntent|cleanupUnlinkCompleted|cleanupRmdirIntent|cleanupRmdirCompleted`：`cleanupMode=prepare|forward|qualityEphemeral|finalize,purgeEntrySetHash,entryIndex,path,type,hashOrNull,size,mode,linkTargetOrNull,inode,device,intentHashOrNull`；
- `activationCommitIntent|activationCommitCompleted`：`payloadHash,sessionCommitToken,intentHashOrNull,activationCommitReceiptOrNull,latestOwnershipHashOrNull`。

Decoder从Port唯一action enum生成providerAction，不接受自由字符串。Provider Completed必须绑定唯一Intent Hash和原签名Receipt；file/root/cleanup Completed必须绑定Intent与事后lstat；Checkpoint必须绑定完整输入Receipt集合，不能用动作字符串代替证据。首个takeover业务记录必须是`executorIsolation`且绑定Isolation Receipt与最新Ownership/GrantTransition outcome；凭据轮换后的首个业务记录必须是`authorizationRefreshed`并绑定新旧GrantSet及Transition Receipt。`trafficZero`严格依赖fenceOld Completed、drainComplete与完整telemetryObservation；`authorityMountZero`依赖unmount Completed；`candidateSealed`依赖sealIntent与全部sealEntryCompleted；`purgeOpened`必须携已预计算的PurgeEntrySetHash并早于任何finalize cleanup Intent。依赖Hash缺失、乱序或Payload跨分支字段均拒绝。

取得WAL写权限后的任何可变文件、外部Provider、KMS或Store动作都必须重读最新Ownership/epoch，再追加和fsync相应类型Intent，执行携同一stableToken+epoch的动作，最后追加和fsync Completed并同步相关父目录。Session mutation是单独线性控制面：reserve/attach/claim/takeover/renew/refresh/begin/complete/completeAndRelease只由Session Provider原子事务日志与SessionMutationResultReceipt承载，不伪造业务WAL Intent；下一条phaseOpened/controlBootstrap或ExecutorRunReceipt必须镜像最新完整Receipt/Ownership/GrantTransition Hash。`commitPoint`因定义不可逆文件/业务分界，仍使用专用activationCommit Pair；Forward Ledger三项CAS在CutoverCli仍存活时使用对应providerAction Pair。这样CLI退出后由Launcher执行complete/completeIntermediate/completeAndRelease无需Journal append权，Launcher权限仍限Core/RunReceipt/Session。任何Session Receipt丢失按SessionRecoveryInvocation恢复，不以WAL猜测。重启只用同一ToolBundle/Entry/Node/npm/Authorization，或Ticket绑定的严格Continuation/Restore/Forward/Repair；外部Intent无Completed时以RecoveryInvocation读取事实或按inode补齐，SignedAbsent才允许同Token重试，无Intent不得解释位移。restore只逆序撤销已完成且提交点前的可逆动作，activationCommitted后的记录只能前向处理。当前Cutover Core绑定terminalJournalHash、recordCount、末Ownership Hash/epoch与executorPreflightHash，Launcher Receipt再绑定该Core。Journal在Finalized后仍永久保留于独立审计日志卷；原文件与内容地址副本都不得删除。

`QuarantineFinalizer.ts`是无main的唯一逐Entry物理清理器，Port分成无副作用`plan(mode,input)`与有副作用`execute(openedPlan,journal)`，并以封闭Mode隔离权限。`prepareCleanup`只接受Session=Restoring、predecessor=prepareAttempt|preparedCancel、原prepare/Prepared证据、精确PrepareCleanup或sealed Candidate Entry Set、targetShopUntouched和unmount字段；它只清本run initial candidate/AuthorityMount/temp。`forwardCleanup`只接受Session=Activating、activationCommitted、work=forwardCleaning、Traffic held、operation=cleanup和`failedBuild|unusedPrepared|supersededPrepared`精确Entry Set；failedBuild对应building，unused/superseded对应prepared，reserved/consumed拒绝。`qualityEphemeralCleanup`只接受Preparing、Verifying或forwardPreparing Ownership、签名allowlist与本次Ephemeral Manifest，只清可重建项并证明Tracked Manifest不变。

`finalizePurge`的plan只读完整Get并验证Finalizing Ownership、CutoverActivated/Archive三门/Finalize Authorization、Forward Ledger及全部ForwardPrepared/ForwardCleaned/RepairAttempt Pair，从初始RootActivation、retired/Git/Ephemeral/Apply WAL、全部Canonical RootSwapResult+ForwardCommitReceipt、repair WAL和RepairQuarantine Entry Set计算按realpath UTF-8排序的唯一PurgeEntrySet；它先全量lstat并验证type/hash或size/mode/link/inode/device、hardlink与link边界，返回`PurgePlan={entrySet,entrySetHash,inputSetHash,lstatObservationHash}`，绝不写WAL或删除。Tombstone、keptShared、已验零候选、活动targetShop与session Journal不得进入。CutoverCli复算Plan后先追加并fsync `purgeOpened(entrySetHash,inputSetHash,lstatObservationHash)`；execute只接受与该Checkpoint逐字节全等的Opened Plan，再逐项重验。普通文件/符号链接执行`cleanupUnlink intent→fsync journal→unlink itself→fsync parent→completed`；目录在全部授权子项消失后按深度降序cleanupRmdir。四Mode使用不同Decoder、Guard与allowedRootHash，绝不递归、glob、跟随链接或删除共享Git commonDir。

Finalize Crash恢复只接受同一Opened PurgePlan：有Intent且路径缺失时补Completed；有Intent且原inode仍在时重验后重试；无Intent却缺失、inode被替换、目录含未授权Entry或Hash漂移立即停机。完成后对每个退休Root、全部initial/forwardRepair oldShop Quarantine和run payload目录逐Entry零扫描，生成physicalRootZero与quarantinePayloadZero。Journal不是业务Payload：完整PutRaw后，CutoverFinalized Core绑定`journalBlobRef/terminalHash/recordCount/purgeEntrySetHash/purgedCount/purgedBytes/rootZeroHash`；Launcher以独立Core Reader回读后，`completeAndRelease`原子完成Finalizing→Finalized和资源释放。0600 Journal原文件随独立审计日志卷永久保留，外部内容地址副本与Finalized Evidence同样永久保留；任何清理策略均无该路径的Delete Grant。

Prepare发生在任何Delete之前：CutoverCli消费prepare授权，验证基线FileDispositionReport与SourceSnapshotSet Pair，生成PostTree/Bundle/CheckoutClaim，再以ToolBundle内绝对Node/npm-cli和受控生命周期PATH在签名activationCandidateRealpath调用唯一`npm run quality`；`QUALITY_CHECKOUT_CLAIM_REF=<signed-ref>`只是可解析Ref，Bootstrap必须用Trust Policy预置Claim Reader完整验证，不是自证字符串。Quality退出后调用CiRunAttestor，最后发布`CutoverPreparedRef`。只有Prepared中的post OID/tree/source hash、Report/Manifest、SnapshotSet、Authority、Bundle、RootActivationManifest、Quality/Sign/Attestation全部闭合，外部授权方才签发apply授权。Apply按Journal先原子激活整个Shop根，再把其余获准退休Source移入Quarantine，不在原脏工作树checkout、reset、覆盖活动Manifest或声称Clean。postCommit内的`docs/operations/FileDisposition.yml`规范化Hash必须等于基线Report中的postManifestCandidateHash。

`CutoverState.ts`把CutoverPrepared Ref、CutoverApplied Ref、apply/verify Authorization、claim SessionMutationResultReceipt及其owned SessionOwnershipReceipt、session Journal、基线FileDispositionReport及两Manifest Hash、SourceSnapshotSet Pair、PostTree/Bundle/Authority Pair、Authority Materialization Receipt/Unmount Plan/recoveryAuthorityContext和活动targetShop inode/device规范化为Payload，再经State专用Builder/Signer/Writer发布`CutoverState` Envelope。Verify不再调用CleanClone或复用已被rename的candidate，而是在Apply后真实`targetShopRealpath`复验postCommit/postTree/Clean与RootActivationManifest全等。Launcher以verify授权中的只读State Bootstrap Credential完整Get/验签后0600物化，再由ToolBundle绝对Node/npm-cli调用唯一入口`npm run quality -- --cutover-state-ref <EvidenceRef> --cutover-state <0600-state-path> --cutover-state-hash <payloadSha256>`；路径只是一轮临时传输，业务信任锚是Ref+Envelope+Payload三重全等。TaskContext把已验证State传给Generated/Architecture/FileDisposition。postApply门同时证明pre Delete项逐个在Quarantine+Archive、pre非Delete项有迁移/保留闭合、活动post Manifest穷尽HEAD Tree、HEAD/PostTree/expected SourceTree三者相等且三项Git Clean检查全空；Generated零Diff。ReleaseManifest必须写postCommitOid和postTree SourceTreeHash，所以从该commit Fresh Clone可精确重放。

Quality子进程exit=0后，CutoverCli用唯一CiRunAttestor取得post RunAttestationRef，并先完成predelete/postApply确定性ReleaseManifest与稳定Artifact Blob Ref/Hash相等门。`CanonicalRef.ts`闭Port固定为`readSource(request,token)/findSourceReadReceipt(recoveryInvocation)/compareAndSwap(request,token)/findCasReceipt(recoveryInvocation)`；唯一GitCanonicalRef只接受相位Authorization中的Canonical Policy、CredentialGrantBinding和签名Remote/Ref/OID。readSource返回内容寻址Source Bundle与Provider签名Receipt；compareAndSwap Token固定为授权logical action token。WAL统一写`providerIntent(providerAction=readSource|compareAndSwap)`；丢响应先按闭映射构造RecoveryInvocation，再调用对应find，SignedAbsent才允许同Token重试。
