verify、restore与postcommit-repair.deploymentForwardPatch三支都必须完整签入互相独立的`canonicalReceiptSignerRef+signerCredentialGrantBinding+identity+keyVersion+purpose+subjectId`、`canonicalReceiptPublisherCredentialGrantBinding+allowedKind+prefix+subjectId`和`canonicalReceiptRecoveryReaderCredentialGrantBinding+exactKind+subjectId`。Adapter验Provider原签名后，按`subjectId=sha256(cutoverRunId||action||token||fencingEpoch)`条件发布CanonicalRefProviderReceipt；回包丢失由exact-subject Reader恢复，不能重签。remote=old可同Token重试，remote=new+匹配Receipt补Completed，第三OID停机。Restore反向CAS并产CutoverRestored，绝不force/reset。CutoverVerified绑定pre/post Quality、Manifest、Artifact Blob+Evidence、SignBundle、RunAttestation、Equality、CAS、隔离Deployment/Smoke/CanaryDestroy及namespaceZero，只进入activate。所有Manifest/Bundle保存在Evidence Store。

`CutoverCli.ts`是唯一Cutover业务入口，但不得从原Shop源码、tsx或PATH直接执行。平台只调用只读Bundle的`ToolLauncher.mjs --entry CutoverCli --authorization-ref <CutoverAuthorizationEvidenceRef>`；Bundle内入口接收`prepare|apply|verify|activate|restore|finalize`六个终态相位以及Continuation、forward build/cleanup和repair受签分支。prepare产CutoverPrepared（含referenceZero/RootActivationManifest）；apply先Fence/trafficZero、原子激活Shop根、Quarantine其余Entry并产CutoverApplied；verify在真实targetShop运行postApply Quality、CAS、隔离部署与Smoke并产CutoverVerified；activate执行PITR、dark topology、Session activationCommitted、生产Migration/Reconcile、open新流量和Live Window，异常则只沿CutoverForwardPrepared/CutoverForwardCleaned/CutoverRepairAttempt证据链向前，最终产CutoverActivated；restore只处理commit point之前`prepareAttempt|preparedCancel|applyAttempt|applied|verifyAttempt|verified|activateAttempt`七分支；finalize只在Activated Pair、Forward Ledger与全部Attempt Pair完整时用QuarantineFinalizer清理获准Payload并产CutoverFinalized。每个Core都绑定executorPreflightHash，Launcher以独立Core Reader postcheck后分别发布Receipt；下一相位消费Pair。原来源树只作为签名target data，不提供入口或依赖。

initial prepare与`postcommit-forward-prepare(operation=build)`的正常成功路径都必须在授权中签入`mountPolicyHash/authorityMountProviderRef/mountCredentialGrantBinding/unmountCredentialGrantBinding/authorityMountVerifyRef/mountReceiptSchemaVersion/mountId/authorityMountRealpath/mountToken/unmountToken/mountTimeout`，两份Binding的exactAction/exactToken分别只能是mount/mountToken与unmount/unmountToken，不能只给失败Cleanup或复用一份Binding。候选生成前，CutoverCli先写/fsync `providerIntent(providerAction=mount)`并调用MountControl；Receipt丢失时用完整RecoveryInvocation按mountToken find，验签完成后才把PID/Entrypoint allowlist交给子进程。全部Generator/Quality/Attestor退出且Evidence完整发布后，再续租、写/fsync `providerIntent(providerAction=unmount)`、调用原Receipt约束的unmount，验签并确认`lstat mountRealpath=absent`后写providerCompleted与`authorityMountZero`；随后才运行qualityEphemeralCleanup并seal candidate。Prepared或ForwardPrepared Core必须绑定Mount Receipt、Unmount Receipt、zero hash和两条WAL Completed Hash，Launcher postcheck再次证明挂载不存在；任一步失败保持Preparing或forwardPreparing，由原动作Continuation/两步Takeover按RecoveryInvocation完成，不能带残留Mount发布Core或释放Resource Keys。

#### 33.4.3 硬切模块调用、提交点与前向修复时序

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant I as Authorization Issuer
    participant L as ToolLauncher
    participant S as Session Store
    participant X as Executor Isolation
    participant C as CutoverCli
    participant Q as Quality and Evidence
    participant T as Traffic Control
    participant R as RootActivator
    participant D as Deploy and Migration

    I->>L: resource-claim authorization
    L->>S: reserve exact resource keys
    S-->>I: reservation ticket and fencing epoch
    I->>L: prepare authorization bound to ticket and epoch
    L->>S: attach Empty to Preparing
    L->>C: prepare with signed session journal
    C->>Q: PostTree, initial candidate, predelete quality
    Q-->>C: Prepared evidence inputs
    C-->>L: CutoverPrepared Core
    L->>S: complete Preparing to Prepared with Core and Receipt

    alt completing executor remains live
        I->>L: apply authorization bound to Prepared Completion
        L->>S: phaseHandoff claim Prepared to Applying
    else executor died after Completion
        I->>L: executor-isolation authorization noActiveSet
        L->>X: isolate old executor and bump generation
        X-->>L: ExecutorIsolation Receipt
        I->>L: takeover-claim normalPhaseHandoff apply intent
        L->>S: reserveTakeover with Completion Capability hash
        S-->>I: takeover ticket new epoch and generation
        I->>L: apply authorization bound to Ticket and Completion
        L->>S: attachTakeover normalPhaseHandoff Prepared to Applying
    end
    L->>C: apply
    C->>T: fence old, drain, prove traffic zero
    C->>R: initialApply atomic root swap
    C-->>L: CutoverApplied Core
    L->>S: complete Applying to Applied

    I->>L: verify authorization with State scopes
    L->>S: phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover Applied to Verifying
    L->>C: verify active targetShop
    C->>Q: publish CutoverState and run postApply quality
    C->>D: canonical CAS and isolated canary probe
    C-->>L: CutoverVerified Core
    L->>S: complete Verifying to Verified

    I->>L: activate authorization
    L->>S: phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover Verified to Activating
    L->>C: activate with current ownership
    C->>D: PITR snapshot and dark production topology
    L->>S: commitPoint activationCommitted
    alt normal continuation succeeds
        C->>D: production migration and owner reconcile
        C->>T: open derived final target map
        C->>Q: minimum live window and SLI
        C-->>L: CutoverActivated Core
        L->>S: complete Activating to Activated
    else failure after commit
        opt public traffic had opened
            C->>T: fence current new target
        end
        opt forward artifact is required
            I->>L: forward build authorization
            L->>S: beginForward forwardPreparing
            L->>C: build forward candidate
            C->>Q: forward quality and seal forward candidate
            C-->>L: CutoverForwardPrepared Core
            L->>S: completeIntermediate to forwardReady
        end
        I->>L: postcommit repair authorization
        L->>S: beginIntermediate repairing
        L->>C: execute signed repair plan
        opt deployment forward patch
            C->>S: reserve forward ledger entry
            C->>R: forwardRepair atomic root swap
            C->>S: completed root swap consumes candidate
            C->>D: deploy forward artifact and reconcile
        end
        C->>T: open or reopen final target map
        C->>Q: new minimum live window and SLI
        alt repair still fails
            C->>T: fence repaired target
            C-->>L: CutoverRepairAttempt Core
            L->>S: completeIntermediate to repairCheckpoint
        else repair succeeds and ledger has no pending candidate
            C-->>L: CutoverActivated Core with full attempt chain
            L->>S: complete Activating to Activated
        end
    end

    I->>L: finalize authorization
    L->>S: phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover Activated to Finalizing
    L->>C: finalize exact quarantine payload
    C-->>L: CutoverFinalized Core
    L->>S: completeAndRelease Finalized and all resource keys
```

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    Empty --> Preparing
    Preparing --> Prepared
    Prepared -->|phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover| Applying
    Applying --> Applied
    Applied -->|phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover| Verifying
    Verifying --> Verified
    Verified -->|phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover| Activating
    Activating --> Activated
    Activated -->|phaseHandoff claim or normalPhaseHandoff takeover| Finalizing
    Finalizing --> Finalized
    Preparing -->|restore takeover| Restoring
    Prepared -->|phaseHandoff claim or restore takeover| Restoring
    Applying -->|restore takeover| Restoring
    Applied -->|phaseHandoff claim or restore takeover| Restoring
    Verifying -->|restore takeover| Restoring
    Verified -->|phaseHandoff claim or restore takeover| Restoring
    Activating -->|restore takeover only before activationCommitted| Restoring
    Restoring --> Restored

    Preparing -->|positionResume continuation| Preparing
    Applying -->|positionResume continuation| Applying
    Verifying -->|positionResume continuation| Verifying
    Restoring -->|positionResume continuation| Restoring
    Finalizing -->|positionResume finalizeContinuation| Finalizing

    Activating --> Continuing[continuing]
    Continuing -->|positionResume activate continuation| Continuing
    Continuing --> ForwardPreparing[forwardPreparing]
    ForwardPreparing -->|CutoverForwardPrepared Pair| ForwardReady[forwardReady]
    ForwardPreparing --> ForwardCleaning[forwardCleaning]
    ForwardPreparing -->|positionResume resumeIntermediate| ForwardPreparing
    ForwardReady --> ForwardCleaning
    ForwardCleaning -->|positionResume resumeIntermediate| ForwardCleaning
    ForwardCleaning -->|CutoverForwardCleaned Pair| RepairCheckpoint[repairCheckpoint]
    Continuing --> Repairing[repairing]
    ForwardReady --> Repairing
    RepairCheckpoint --> ForwardPreparing
    RepairCheckpoint --> ForwardCleaning
    RepairCheckpoint --> Repairing
    Repairing -->|positionResume resumeIntermediate| Repairing
    Repairing -->|CutoverRepairAttempt Pair| RepairCheckpoint
    Repairing -->|live verified and ledger terminal| Activated
```

### 33.5 当前代码的首批不可交换修改

| 顺序 | 先改文件                                                                                | 原因                                                                                          | 完成后才可删                                                |
| ---: | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
|    1 | `tools/requirementgen`、`docs/requirements/*`                                           | 旧生成物仍写旧工作簿Source和Hash                                                              | 旧Source名、旧Hash和旧生成物入口                            |
|    2 | `packages/contract/definitions/*`、`tools/contractgen`、`packages/sdk`                  | 当前Operation/Event/SDK公共面仍含unknown                                                      | Generic Public Call、手写DTO、手写URL                       |
|    3 | `tools/quality/src/Quality.ts`、`audit/Boundary.ts`、Naming、Callgraph、FileDisposition | 把旧`scripts/**`有效语义迁入唯一质量工具；当前审计只识别单数feature，六端大量features导致假绿 | 根`scripts`第二入口、历史豁免、弱字符串审计                 |
|    4 | `foundation/application`与`foundation/interface`                                        | 建唯一OperationPipeline、Typed Handler、Error/Response Presenter                              | ModuleOperations、CommandBus、QueryBus、message ErrorMapper |
|    5 | Identity跨Owner、Checkout QuoteReader、Order/Payment环                                  | 这是最危险的数据和锁边界                                                                      | 跨Schema SQL、旧PlaceOrder协调、反向Import                  |
|    6 | Finance四子域及六个P0/P1                                                                | 当前差异、SettlementLine、Withdrawal、Overview、Candidate和Statement Kind会破坏账务证据       | Finance旧Action Map和跨Schema读取                           |
|    7 | Module公开面、Event v2、Job Catalog                                                     | 当前根导出Pg实例，事件和Job不足以证明重放顺序                                                 | Concrete Export、旧Envelope、内联Job Catalog                |
|    8 | 六客户端Scope/Abort/Idempotency/Version                                                 | 当前有Store/Supplier/Owner不可达页、Miniapp错域和假409                                        | Generic Client、adminBff、旧Route树                         |
|    9 | Console Manifest/Shell/VI与五主工作台                                                   | 先修共享标题策略和Design Token，避免四端Fork                                                  | 重复H1、features目录、旧CSS真值                             |
|   10 | Provider/Vendor合并、Database Ownership和164迁移后Repair                                | 统一Extension层和表Owner，避免第二插件与第二表名体系                                          | extensions/channel、extensions/vendors、别名表、旧Grant/RPC |

### 33.7 单 Operation 的无兼容硬切时序

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant F as Frozen Branch
    participant G as Generators
    participant N as New Implementation
    participant C as All Callers
    participant Q as Quality Gates
    participant O as Old Implementation
    participant R as Release

    F->>G: regenerate requirement contract sdk and catalogs
    G-->>N: typed interfaces and continuity entry
    N->>N: handler owner repository audit outbox tests
    N->>Q: behavior concurrency scope and recovery comparison
    Q-->>N: pass without public exposure of duplicate operation
    N->>C: atomically update six clients jobs events providers cli and monitors
    C->>Q: callgraph navigation build and journey
    Q-->>O: old callers equal zero
    O->>O: delete route sdk rpc type config and tests tied only to old path
    O->>Q: clean clone full quality and database replay
    Q-->>R: signed immutable artifact
    R->>R: stop old traffic migrate reconcile start same hash smoke
```

禁止以旧Route Redirect、Package Alias、双SDK、双写、兼容View或永不删除的Adapter维持通过。必要的影子读取只用于短期比对，不影响业务响应、不成为第二真相，并在硬切后删除。

### 33.8 最终文档与代码完成关系

文档完成只证明施工信息已经闭合，不证明代码完成。真正完成必须同时满足：

- 总方案中的所有图、Owner、时序、不变量、VI和SLO已被代码与运行证据实现。
- 执行卷中的每条文件动作已进入FileDisposition并完成相应门。
- 296 Requirement全部能从260821原行追到Contract、Code、Test和Production Evidence；21 MVP全部Released。
- 28模块没有God Action Map、跨Owner SQL、根具体实现导出或隐式依赖环。
- 六客户端只走Typed SDK，真实Router/Manifest、动态标题、Scope、Abort、Idempotency、Version和状态矩阵闭合。
- 164个当前迁移Hash不变；后续追加迁移可Fresh Replay、生产副本升级、断点恢复并对账为0。
- `extensions/channel`是唯一渠道插件层；Provider与Vendor第二层、空Provider和假成功均不存在。
- 旧Smart/t运行代码、旧Route/RPC/Package、Mock/Simulation/Fallback、双写与辅助入口已通过可恢复流程移除。

在这些证据全部存在前，本文版本3.0只能被称为“完整根治与代码修改蓝图”，不能被称为“根治已经上线”。
